toḻiṟpeyar iyala -
ñ ṇ n m l v ḷ ṉ - īṟṟu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi ukaraccāriyi peṟṟup puṇartal.
uriñ maṇ porun tirum cel vav tuḷ tiṉ - eṉpaṉa muṟiyē appuḷḷiyīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ. avi muṟiyē
	uriñuk kaṭitu, maṇṇuk kaṭitu, porunuk kaṭitu, tirumuk kaṭitu, celluk kaṭitu, vavvuk kaṭitu, tuḷḷuk kaṭitu, tiṉṉuk kaṭitu eṉa alvaḻikkaṇṇum,
	uriñukkaṭumi, maṇṇukkaṭumi, tirumukkaṭumi, celluk kaṭumi, vavvukkaṭumi, tuḷḷukkaṭumi, tiṉṉukkaṭumi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
	ukaraccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum. vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi avvalleḻuttu mikum.
nakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ porunak kaṭumi eṉa ukaram keṭa akaram peṟum.			(to. e. 296 - 299, 306, 327, 345, 376, 382, 401 nac.)
āciriyar ṉakarīṟu vakarīṟu ivaṟṟi vitantu kūṟiṟṟilar.
(345, 382 nac.)
toḻiṟpeyarp pakupatam -
ūṇāṉ, ūṇāḷ, ūṇār, ūṇatu, ūṇaṉa, ūṇēṉ, ūṇēm, ūṇāy, ūṇīr eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa, ittoḻiliyuṭiyār eṉṉum poruṇmit toḻiṟ peyarp pakupatam. ūṇ eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar aṭiyākap piṟantavi avi.	(naṉ. 133 mayili. uri)
toḻiṟpeyarp puṇarcci -
tolkāppiyaṉār viṉippakutikaḷit toḻiṟpeyar eṉṟē kuṟippiṭuvār. ñ ṇ n m l v ḷ ṉ - oṟṟukkaḷ īṟṟaṉavākiya toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum vanta valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramāttiram peṟṟum, yakaram variṉ iyalpākavum, uyirkkaṇam variṉum iyalpāka vum, (taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟāy nilimoḻi iruppiṉ iraṭṭiyum) puṇarum. (to. e. 296 - 299 nac. uri mutaliyaṉa).
muraṇ eṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar ivvitikku māṟāy, muraṇ kaṭitu - eṉa alvaḻiyiṉum, muraṇkaṭumi muraṭkaṭumi eṉa (ṭakarattōṭu uṟaḻvāy) vēṟṟumiyiṉum puṇarum. (309 nac.)
kaṉ porun - eṉpaṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukaraccāriyi viṭuttu akarac cāriyi peṟṟuk kaṉṉak kaṭumi - porunak kaṭumi - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(346, 299 nac.)
urupiṟku iṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa ciṟupāṉmi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum peṟum.

tōṉṟal tirital keṭutal vikāraṅkaḷ -
cāriyi peṟutalum, uyirum oṟṟum uyirmeyyum eṉṟivi mikutalum, tōṉṟal eṉpatām. iccoṉṉa peṟṟiyē muṉ niṉṟa eḻuttu vēṟupaṭa niṟṟal tiripām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum palavum tokutal kēṭu eṉpatām. immūṉṟu vikāramum irumoḻi mūviṭattumām.
	e-ṭu	:	puḷiyaṅkāy eṉa iṭiyē ammu mikkatu.
			vāṉavil, malittali, uriyaney eṉa muṟiyē uyirum (a), oṟṟum (t), uyirmeyyum (ya) mikkaṉa.
			aṟupatu, maṭkuṭam, ‘tiruttārnaṉ ṟeṉṟēṉ tiyēṉ’ eṉa muṟiyē uyirum (ā), oṟṟum (ṇ), uyirmeyyum (tī) tirintaṉa. uyirmeykku iv vāṟaṉṟit tiripuṇṭāyiṉum kāṇka.
palcāttu, maravēr, aṅki eṉa muṟiyē uyirum (a), oṟṟum (m), uyirmeyyum (ka) keṭṭaṉa.	(naṉ. 153 mayili.)
tōṉṟal vikāram -
itu puṇarcci vikāram mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. irumoḻikaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyē cāriyiyō, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇat tiṟkēṟpa orumeyyeḻuttō tōṉṟutal tōṉṟal vikāramām.
	e-ṭu	:	puḷi + paḻam = puḷiyampaḻam - ‘am’ cāriyi 						tōṉṟiyatu.
			nāy + kāl = nāykkāl - kakaramey tōṉṟiyatu.
			pū + koṭi = pūṅkoṭi - kakarattiṟku iṉamāṉa 						ṅakaramey tōṉṟiyatu.	(naṉ. 154)
na
nakara īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar -
nakara īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar porun eṉpatu. aḵtu alvaḻip puṇarccikkaṇ ukaraccāriyi peṟṟup porunuk kaṭitu, porunu naṉṟu, porunu valitu eṉṟāṟ pōlavum, vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ ukaraccāriyiyē aṉṟi akarac cāriyiyum peṟṟup porunukkaṭumi - porunakkaṭumi, porununaṉmi - porunanaṉmi, porunuvalimi, poruna valimi eṉṟāṟpōlavum muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 208)

nakarīṟṟup potuppuṇarcci -
nakara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iraṇṭē. avi verin, porun eṉpaṉa. iccoṟkaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramum peṟṟum, yakaramum uyirum variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum; vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ ukarac cāriyikku māṟāka akarac cāriyi peṟum. verin eṉṉum col vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi nakaram keṭṭu melleḻuttō valleḻuttō peṟṟup puṇarum. ciṟupāṉmi urupukkuc cellum cāriyiyākiya iṉ vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkum peṟutaluṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	:	porunuk kaṭitu, porunu ñāṉṟatu, porunu valitu, porun yātu, porun aritu - ivi al vaḻi muṭipu.	(to. e. 298 nac)
			porunakkaṭumi verinakkaṭumi, poruna ñāṟci, verinañāṟci, porunavaṉmi, verinavaṉmi, porunayāppu, verinayāppu, porunarumi veri narumi - ivi vēṟṟumi muṭipu.	(299 nac.)	veriṅkuṟi veriñceyki verintali verim puṟam; verikkuṟi vericceyki verittali verippuṟam - vēṟṟumi muṭipu.	(300, 301 nac.)
			poruniṉi veriniṉi	(182 nac.)
			poruniṉkuṟi, veriniṉkuṟi	(299 nac. uri)
nakara īṟu -
nakara īṟu oru coṟkē urittām. porun eṉa varum.			(mu. vī. eḻut. 90)

nakaram etirmaṟip peyarōṭu iṇiyum muṟi -
niṉṟa moḻiyiṉ poruḷi māṟṟutaṟku mutalil niṟuttiya nakara uyirmey, apporuḷmoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu uyirmeyyākil, avvuyir oḻiya uṭal pōm. (‘a’ eṉa niṟkum) aḵtu uyirāyiṉ, nakaruyirmey pirintu uyir muṉṉum uṭal piṉṉum ām. (‘an’ eṉa niṟkum).
varalāṟu:
cañcalam eṉa niṟutti, itaṉi illātavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉak karutiyaviṭattu, nakara uyirmeyyi muṉṉē varuvittuc ‘cārntatu uṭalāyiṉ taṉ uyirpōm’ eṉpataṉāl pirittu nakara oṟṟik keṭuttu, ‘acañcalaṉ’ eṉa muṭikka.
payam, kaḷaṅkam eṉa iviyum avvilakkaṇattāṉ apayaṉ, akaḷaṅkaṉ eṉa muṭikka.
upamaṉ eṉa niṟutti, itaṉi illātavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉak karutiyaviṭattu, nakara uyirmeyyi muṉṉar niṟuttic ‘cārntatu tāṉ āviyēl taṉ āvi muṉ ākum’ eṉpataṉāl pirittu akaratti muṉṉar niṟutti, nakaroṟṟiṉmēl uyiri (ukaram) ēṟṟi, anupamaṉ eṉa muṭikka.
akam eṉpatu pāvam; itaṉi illātavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉak karutiyaviṭattu, ivvilakkaṇattāl anakaṉ eṉka. ucitam eṉpatu yōkkiyam; aḵtu illātatu anucitam eṉa muṭikka.
(nēmi. eḻut. 11 uri)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kuṟippiṭum īreḻuttu moḻi mutaliyaṉa -
ceyyuḷiyalil oṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaramum eḻuttāka eṇṇap paṭātati uṭkoṇṭu, ataṉi eḻuttatikārattum ēṟṟi, oṟṟiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum viṭuttuc coṟkaḷil eḻuttik kaṇakkiṭu muṟiyi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. āṉāl am muṟiyi avarē neṭukap piṉpaṟṟilar.
ā, kā - ōreḻuttorumoḻi; maṇi, varaku, koṟṟaṉ - īreḻuttorumoḻi; kuravu - mūveḻuttorumoḻi; kaṇaviri - nāleḻuttorumoḻi; akattiyaṉār - aiyeḻut toru moḻi; tirucciṟṟampalam - āṟeḻut torumoḻi; perumpaṟṟappuliyūr-ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi (to. e. 45 nac.)
naṭattu, naṭattuvi, naṭattuvippi:  viḷakkam -
ivvāypāṭukaḷuḷ naṭattu varuttu mutaliyaṉa ēvalviṉi- mutal iraṇṭiyum iyaṟṟum viṉimutal iraṇṭiyum mūvaruḷ iruvarkku oru karuttaṉiyum,
naṭattuvi varuttuvi mutaliyaṉa ēval viṉimutal mūṉṟiyum iyaṟṟum viṉimutal mūṉṟiyum nālvaruḷ mūvarkku oru karuttaṉiyum.
naṭattuvippi varuttuvippi mutaliyaṉa ēvalviṉimutal nāṉkiyum iyaṟṟum viṉimutal nāṉkiyum aivaruḷ nālvarkku oru karuttaṉiyum, tantu niṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam iruvarmutal aivari uḷḷuṟutta vāypāṭukaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟavē, varampiṉṟi ēvalmēl ēvalum iyaṟṟalmēl iyaṟṟalumāy varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku vāypāṭu iṉṟu eṉpatum peṟṟām.	(naṉ. 138 caṅkara.)
naṭappi, naṭappippi ēvaṟpakāppatam ākāmi -
eṭuttalōciyāṉ muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉi muṟṟup pakāppataṅkaḷākiya naṭa - naṭappi eṉṟāṟ pōl vaṉavē pakutiyām eṉavum, naṭappippi - varuvippi - naṭattu vippi eṉa ivi (vi pi vikutikaḷ) iṇintuvarum eṉak kūṟiyavaṟṟi irumaṭiyēvaṟ pakāppatam eṉavum, naṭappip pittāṉ - varuvippittāṉ, naṭattuvippittāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟiṟku naṭappippi mutalāṉaviyē pakutiyām eṉavum kūṟuvārumuḷar. avar kūṟuvatu poruntātu. eṉṉiyeṉil, avvāṟu kūṟuvārkkum, naṭappippi mutalāṉavi pakutiyākumiṭattu, apporuṇmiyil tirintu paṭuttalōciyāl acceykimēl peyart taṉmippaṭṭu viṉimāttiriyē uṇartti niṟkum eṉak kūṟavēṇṭutaliṉ, muṉṉili ēvalviṉimuṟṟup pakāp pataṅkaḷ pakuti ākā eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum. mēlum iru muṟi ēvutal kūṟiyatu kūṟal ātaliṉ avi iḻivaḻakkām. ap pakutikaḷāl piṟanta pakupataṅkaḷum avvāṟē iḻi vaḻakkāy muṭiyum.	(i. vi. eḻut. 44)
naṭa, vā mutaliyaṉa -
naṭa vā mutaliya irupattu mūṉṟu īṟṟavākiya ivi mutalākiya coṟkaḷellām muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉimuṟṟākavum, ēṉiya viṉikaḷiṉ pakutiyākavum varum. cey eṉṉum vāypāṭṭiṉa  ivi.
muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉi ‘naṭavāy’ eṉpatē koṇṭu, naṭa eṉpatu ēvalviṉikkup pakutiyē, aḵtu ēvalviṉi aṉṟu eṉpār civañāṉamuṉivar.	(naṉ. 137)
naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa uriccoṟkaḷē -
‘ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē’ ātaliṉ, avaṟṟip peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉap pākupāṭu ceytatu, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa pōlap poruḷ vēṟupāṭu paṟṟik karuvi ceytaṟ poruṭ ṭēyām. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟippum paṇpum iciyum paṟṟi varuvaṉa ellām orunikarāka, cilavaṟṟi uriccol eṉavum cilavaṟṟi vēṟucol eṉavum kōṭal poruntāmiyiṉ avaṟṟi uriccol eṉṟē koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivi kuṇappaṇpu, toḻiṟpaṇpu eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum. kuṇappaṇpum toḻiṟpaṇpum ākiya poruṭpaṇpiṉi uṇarttum col uriccollēyām.
naṭantāṉ eṉpuḻi naṭattalic ceytāṉ eṉa urupu viritaliṉ, naṭa eṉpatu peyarccol aṉṟō eṉiṉ, naṭantāṉ orumoḻittaṉmippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, atu naṭa vittāṉ eṉat taṉcollāl pirittuk kāṭṭal ākāmiyiṉ, naṭattalic ceytāṉ eṉap piṟacollāl kāṭṭi muṭikkappaṭum. naṭa eṉpatu tal eṉṟa pakutipporuḷ vikuti peṟṟavaḻip peyarccollām ātaliṉ, āṇṭu urupēṟṟal amiyum. vaṭanūluḷḷum piriyāt toki piṟacollāl virittuk kāṭṭappaṭum.	(cū. vi. pak. 34 - 36)
naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa muṉṉili ēvalorumiviṉi ākāmi -
naṭavāy vārāy mutaliya muṉṉili ēvalorumi etirkāla viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷ āy vikuti kuṉṟi naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa- vāy niṉṟaṉa allatu, mutaṉilikaḷē ōcivēṟupāṭṭāl muṉṉili ēval orumi viṉikaḷ ākā.	(cū. vi. pak. 32)
nantammi, nuntammi : cāriyi vaḻuvamitiyātal -
paṭarkkikkuriya tammuccāriyi, nantammi nuntammi eṉat taṉmiyilum muṉṉiliyilum varutal ‘ōriṭam piṟaviṭam taḻuvalum uḷavē’ (380) eṉṉum iṭavaḻuvamitiyām.			(naṉ. 246 caṅkara.)
naṉṉūl eḻuttatikārat taṟciṟappup pāyiram -
‘pūmali acōkiṉ’ eṉṟu toṭaṅkum nūṟpā, vaṇakkam atikāram eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉiyum collutaliṉ, taṟ ciṟappup pāyiramām. ‘pūmali acōkiṉ puṉiniḻal amarnta nāṉmukaṟ ṟoḻutu’ eṉa vaṇakkam coṉṉavāṟu; ‘naṉ kiyampuvaṉ eḻuttē’ eṉa atikāram coṉṉavāṟu. (ivvāṟē collatikārattum ‘muccakam niḻaṟṟum muḻumati mukkuṭi accutaṉaṭi toḻutu’ eṉa vaṇakkamum, ‘aṟi kuvaṉ collē’ eṉa atikāramum coṉṉavāṟu kāṇka.) nūliṉi nuvalvāṉ pukuntu īṇṭu vaṇakkam vikka vēṇ ṭiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ‘vaḻipaṭu teyvam vaṇakkam ceytu, maṅkala moḻi mutalāka vakuppavē, eṭuttuk koṇṭa ilakkaṇa ilakkiyam, iṭukkaṇ iṉṟi iṉitu muṭiyum’ eṉpavākaliṉ īṇṭu vaṇakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu.			(naṉ. 55 mayili.)
naṉṉūl cārpeḻuttup pattu eṉṟataṉ nōkkam -
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ceyki oṉṟaṉiyum nōkkic cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟu eṉak karuviceytār ātaliṉ, iv vāciriyar ceykiyum ceyyuḷiyalum nōkkic cār peḻuttup pattu eṉak karuvi ceytār eṉpatuṇarka.			(naṉ. 60 civañā.)
 naṉṉūl mayilinātar uriyil akattiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ -
  ‘peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum moḻimutal aṭaṅkum.’	(naṉ. 130)
‘vayira ūciyum mayaṉviṉi irumpum
ceyiraṟu poṉṉic cemmicey āṇiyum
tamakkami karuviyum tāmām avipōl
 urittiṟam uṇarttalum uriyatu toḻilē’.	(naṉ. 258)
‘palaviṉ iyintavum oṉṟeṉap paṭumē
aṭicil pottakam cēṉi aminta
  katavam māli kampalam aṉiya’	(naṉ. 259)
‘aracumēm paṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭikaḷ
patiṉmarum uṭaṉiruppu iruvarum paṭitta
  paṉṉiru ticiyiṟ coṉṉayam uṭiyavum’	(naṉ. 272)
‘ēḻiyal muṟiyatu etirmuka vēṟṟumi
vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉṟu
ōtiya pulavaṉum uḷaṉoru vakiyāṉ
  intiraṉ eṭṭām vēṟṟumi eṉṟaṉaṉ’	(naṉ. 290)
‘viṉinili urittalum viṉāviṟ kēṟṟalum
  peyarkoḷa varutalum peyarppaya ṉiliyē’.	(naṉ. 294)
‘āṟaṉ urupē atu ātu avvum
vēṟoṉṟu uriyatit taṉakkuri yatieṉa
irupāṟ kiḻamiyiṉ maruvuṟa varumē
  aimpāl urimiyum ataṉtaṟ kiḻami’.	(naṉ. 299)
‘maṟṟuccol nōkkā marapiṉa aṉittum
  muṟṟi niṟpaṉa muṟṟiyal moḻiyē,’	(naṉ. 322)
‘muṟṟuc coṟṟām viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum
  muṟṟuccol eṉṉum muṟimiyiṉ tiriyā’.	(naṉ. 332)
‘kālamum viṉiyum tōṉṟippāl tōṉṟātu
  peyarkoḷ ḷummatu peyarec cammē’	(naṉ. 339)
‘kālamum viṉiyum tōṉṟippāl tōṉṟātu
  viṉikoḷ ḷummatu viṉiyec cammē.	(naṉ. 341)
‘eṉittumuṟ ṟaṭukkiṉum aṉittumoru peyarmēl
niṉittukkoḷa nikaḻum nikaḻttiya muṟṟē
viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
  palapala aṭukkiṉum muṟṟumoḻip paṭiya’,	(naṉ. 354)
‘kaṇṭupāl mayaṅkum aiyak kiḷavi
niṉṟōr varuvōr eṉṟucol nikaḻak
  kāṇā aiyamum pallōr paṭarkki’.	(naṉ. 377)
‘ulaka vaḻakkamum orumuk kālamum
  nilipeṟa uṇartarum mutumaṟi neṟiyāṉ’	(naṉ. 381)
‘acinili iraṇṭiṉum poruḷmoḻi mūṉṟiṉum
  iciniṟi nāṉkiṉum orumoḻi toṭarum’.	(naṉ. 394)
naṉṉūl mayilinātar uriyil avinayac cūttiraṅkaḷ -
‘patiṉeṇ meyyum atuvē mavvōṭu
  āytamum aḷapuari tēytalum urittē’	(naṉ. 59)
‘āṟṟa luṭiuyir muyaṟciyiṉ aṇuiyintu
  ēṟṟaṉa oliyāyt tōṟṟutal piṟappē’	(naṉ. 73)
‘kacatapa ṅavvē ātiyum iṭiyum
ṭaṟiṭi ṇaṉaraḻa laḷiṭi kaṭiyē
  ñanamaya vavvē mūṉṟiṭam eṉpa’.	(naṉ. 101)
‘taṉṉi uṇarttiṉ eḻuttām piṟaporuḷic
  cuṭṭutaṟ kaṇṇēyām col’.	(naṉ. 127)
‘avitām
peyarccol eṉṟā toḻiṟcol eṉṟā
  iraṇṭaṉ pālāy aṭaṅkumaṉ payiṉṟē’,	(naṉ. 130)
‘ṟaṉaḻe ovvum taṉiyum makāramum
  taṉmit tamiḻpotu maṟṟu’,	(naṉ. 149)
  ‘aḻitūu vakiyum avaṟṟiṉ pālē’,	(naṉ. 263)
‘kālam aṟitoḻil karuttaṉōṭu iyiyap
  pālvaki tōṟum paṭumoḻi vēṟē’.	(naṉ. 319)
  pattu eccaṅkaḷ	(naṉ. 359)
  tokipporuḷ ciṟakkumiṭam	(naṉ. 369)
naṉṉūl mayilinātar uriyil tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ -
‘kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
  oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum,’ 		(moḻimarapu 34) (naṉ. 105)
‘colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉṟu
  āyiraṇṭu eṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē.’	(peyariyal 4) (naṉ. 130)
‘viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
niṉiyuṅ kāli kālamoṭu tōṉṟum,	(viṉayiyal 1) (naṉ. 319)
‘orupāṟ kiḷavi eṉippāṟ kaṇṇum
  varuvaki tāṉē vaḻakkeṉa moḻipa’.	(poruḷiyal28) 	(naṉ. 357)
‘uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ ummit tokiyē
palarcol naṭitteṉa moḻimaṉār pulavar’, 	(eccaviyal 25)			(naṉ. 371)
‘palavayi ṉāṉum eṇṇuttiṇi viravuppeyar
  aḵṟiṇi muṭipiṉa ceyyu ḷuḷḷē’.	(kiḷaviyākkam51) 	(naṉ. 377)
  ‘eṇṇuṅ kālum atuataṉ marapē’.	(47) (naṉ. 388)
‘oṉṟaṟi vatuvē uṟṟaṟi vatuvē
iraṇṭaṟi vatuvē ataṉoṭu nāvē
mūṉṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu mūkkē
nāṉkaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇē 
aintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē...’
‘pullum maraṉum ōraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.’
‘nantum muraḷum īraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.’
‘citalum eṟumpum mūvaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.’
‘ñeṇṭum tumpiyum nāṉkaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.’
‘māvum mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
  piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.’	(marapiyal27-32) 	(naṉ. 443-448)
naṉṉūlār ‘tāṉeṭuttu moḻinta’ tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ -
‘viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
  muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutalnūl ākum.’	(naṉ. pāyiram. 6)
‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
  uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē.’	(naṉ. 89 mayili)
  ‘attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi illi.’	(naṉ. 251)
‘yātaṉ urupiṉ kūṟiṟ ṟāyiṉum
  poruḷcel maruṅkiṉ vēṟṟumi cārum.’	(naṉ. 316)
‘muṉṉili muṉṉar īyum ēyum
annili maruṅkiṉ meyūrntu varumē.’	(naṉ. 335)
‘iyaṟkip poruḷi iṟṟeṉak kiḷattal.’	(naṉ. 403)
‘muṉṉattiṉ uṇarum kiḷaviyum uḷavē.’	(naṉ. 407)
‘māeṉ kiḷavi viyaṅkōḷ aciccol.’	(naṉ. 438)
nālvakit terinilip peyareccattiṉ muṉṉum vali iyalpātal -
	uṇṭa uṇṇāniṉṟa uṇṇāta - eṉavum, kaṭikkaṇitta cittiritta veḷutta kaṟuviya amariya - eṉavum, tiṇṇeṉṟa poṉpōṉṟa - eṉavum, cāṉṟa uṟṟa - eṉavum,
viṉi peyar iṭi uriyaṭiyākap piṟanta nālvakit terinilip peyareccattiyum nilimoḻiyāka niṟutti varumoḻiyākak kutiri - cennāy - takar - paṉṟi - eṉṉum vaṉkaṇam mutalākiya coṟkaḷip puṇarppavē iyalpāka muṭintavāṟu.	(naṉ. 167 caṅkara.)
nāval eṉṉum kuṟippu -
nāval eṉpatu neṟpōr teḻikkum pakaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷit turappatoru col. 
‘kāval uḻavar kaṭuṅkaḷattup pōrēṟi
nāvalōoo eṉṟicikkum nāḷōti’	(muttoḷ.)
eṉpataṉāl iḵtu aṟiyalākum.	(naṉ. 101 caṅkara.)
nāḻimuṉ uri puṇarumāṟu -
nāḻi eṉṉum mukattal aḷavuppeyar muṉṉar, uri eṉṉum mukatta laḷavuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, an nāḻi eṉṉum colliṉ iṟutiyil niṉṟa ikaram tāṉēṟiya meyyoṭum keṭum. avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum. (iraṇṭu uri koṇṭatu oru nāḻi. nāḻiyum uriyum eṉa ummittokiyāy iḵtu alvaḻip puṇarcciyām).
	varumāṟu : nāḻi + uri ḻū nā + uri ḻū nā + ṭ + uri = nāṭuri.		(to. e. 240 nac,  naṉ. 174)
‘nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal toḻilnilikkiḷavi’ puṇarumāṟu -
ikara aikāra makara īṟṟu viṇmīṉ peyarkaḷ nilimoḻi kaḷāka niṟpa, varumoḻikaḷāka valleḻutti mutalākak koṇṭa viṉiccoṟkaḷ varumvaḻi, iṭiyē āṉ cāriyi varum.
	e-ṭu	:	paraṇi + koṇṭāṉ ḻū paraṇi + āṉ + koṇṭāṉ = paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; cittiri + koṇṭāṉ ḻū cittiri + āṉ + koṇṭāṉ = cittiriyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; makam + koṇṭāṉ ḻū makam + attu + āṉ + koṇṭāṉ = makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci; ēḻāvataṉ poruḷ viritturikkappaṭum.	(to. e. 124, 247, 286, 331 nac.)
nāṉkaṉ urupiṟku ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ ātal -
iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉ eṉṟa ṉakarīṟṟuc cāriyikaḷ nāṉkum nilimoḻi nāṉkaṉurupākiya ku eṉpataṉoṭu puṇaruṅ kāl, tām iṭiyē varat tammuṭiya ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tiriyum.
	e-ṭu :	viḷa + iṉ + ku = viḷaviṟku - (to. e. 173 nac.)
		kō + oṉ + ku = kōoṟku - (180)
		iraṇṭu + pattu + ku ḻū irup + āṉ + ku = irupāṟku-				(199)
		atu + aṉ + ku = ataṟku - (176)
nikaḻkāla iṭinili -
āniṉṟu, kiṉṟu, kiṟu - ākiya mūṉṟum  mūviṭattum varum aimpāṟkaṇṇum nikaḻkālam kāṭṭum terinili viṉi muṟṟup pakupata iṭinilikaḷām.
	e-ṭu	:	naṭavāniṉṟāṉ (naṭa + āniṉṟu + āṉ); naṭakkiṉ ṟāṉ (naṭa + k + kiṉṟu + āṉ); naṭakkiṟāṉ (naṭa + k + kiṟu + āṉ)	(naṉ. 143; i. vi. eḻut. 48)
uriyiṟkōṭalāl, uṇṇāniṉṟilaṉ - uṇkiṉṟilaṉa - eṉa etirmaṟikkaṇ āniṉṟu kiṉṟu eṉṉum iṭi nilikaḷ vēṟucila eḻuttoṭu kūṭi nikaḻkālam kāṭṭum eṉavum,
uṇṇākiṭantāṉ -uṇṇāviruntāṉ - eṉak kiṭavum iruvum, urikkiṟṟi - ‘naṉṟumaṉ eṉitu nāṭāy kūṟi’ - eṉa ṟakaramum,
‘kāṉam kaṭattir eṉak kēṭpiṉ’ (kali. 7 : 3) eṉat takaramum,
nōkkuvēṟku, uṇpēṟku eṉa muṟiyē viṉi viṉip peyarkkaṇ vakaramum pakaramum ciṟupāṉmi nikaḻkālam kāṭṭum eṉavum koḷka.	(i. vi. 48 uri)
nilā ‘iṉ’ ṉoṭu varutal -
nilā eṉṉum col iṉcāriyi peṟṟu varum.
	e-ṭu	:	nilā + kānti = nilāviṉ kānti	(mu. vī. puṇa. 93)
nilā eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
nilā eṉpatu kuṟṟeḻutti aṭutta ākārīṟṟup peyar. itu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ vantuḻi eḻuttup pēṟaḷapeṭi māttiram peṟṟum puṇarum. (īṇṭu akaram eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyām.)
	e-ṭu	:	nilāakkatir, nilāamuṟṟam	(to. e. 226 nac.)
nilā eṉṟa col attuccāriyi peṟṟu, nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ, nilāattu ñāṉṟāṉ eṉa varum.	(228 nac.)
ceyyuṭkaṇ nilā eṉpatu nila eṉṟāki ukaraccāriyi peṟṟu nilavu eṉṟāki varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	:	nilavukkatir	(234 nac.)
nilimoḻi yīṟu, mūviṉamey varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ nikaḻumiṭattup puṇarumāṟu -
mey allatu uyirīṟāka niṟkum nilimoḻi mūviṉamey varumiṭattu ēṟkum muṭivu āṟu vakikaḷām. avi iyalpu, mikutal, uṟaḻcci, tirital, keṭutal, nilimāṟutal eṉpaṉa vām. ikkaruttu ilakkaṇakkottiṉiṉṟum koḷḷap paṭṭatu.	(i.ko. 113, 114)
e-ṭu :	nilā + muṟṟam = nilāmuṟṟam; vāḻi + paḻam = vāḻippaḻam; kiḷi + kuṟitu = kiḷikuṟitu, kiḷik kuṟitu; poṉ + kuṭam = poṟkuṭam; maram + vēr = maravēr; nāḷi + kēram = nārikēḷam eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(cuvāmi. eḻut. 28)
nilimoḻi varumoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum puṇartal -
aṭiyāvaṉa nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷi ummit tokiyum irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyumpaṭa ākka vallaṉa ām.
	e-ṭu	:	patiṉāyiram + oṉṟu = patiṉāyirattoṉṟu - nili moḻi aṭi. āyiram + irupaḵtu = āyirat tirupaḵtu - varumoḻi aṭi. patiṉāyiram + iru paḵtu = patiṉāyirattirupaḵtu - irumoḻi aṭi.
vēṟṟumittokiyum uvamattokiyum piḷantu muṭiya, paṇputtokiyum viṉittokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmi yiṉ, oru collēyām.
aṉmoḻittokiyum taṉakku vēṟoru muṭipiṉmiyiṉ orucollēyām. ittokiccoṟkaḷellām aṭi yāy varuṅkālattu oru collāy varum.
uṇṭa cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉap peyareccam aṭutta peyarum, uṇṭu vantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉa viṉiyeccam aṭutta muṟṟum orucollēyām. iṅṅaṉam tokiniliyākavum tokā niliyākavum aṭiyaṭutta coṟkaḷum nilimoḻi varu moḻikaḷākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	paṉṉiraṇṭu + ki = paṉṉiruki - nilimoḻi aṭi yaṭuttu ummittokipaṭa niṉṟatu.
		ōṭiṟṟu + parimā = ōṭiṟṟup parimā - varumoḻi aṭi aṭuttu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki paṭa niṉṟatu.	(to. e. 110 nac. uri)
‘niliyiyalāṉa’ -
niṟṟali ilakkaṇamāka uṭiya eḻuttu. nunti eṉṟa muṟippeyaril niṟṟali ilakkaṇamāka uṭiya (nakaratti ūrntu varukiṉṟa) kuṟṟiyalukaram.	(to. e. 68 nac.)
niliyiṟṟu eṉṟalum, niliyātu eṉṟalum -
niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy varutal nili yiṟṟu eṉṟalām.
	e-ṭu	:	cāttaṉ vantāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ (niṟutta colli muṭittalik kuṟittu varum col ‘kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi’ eṉappaṭum.)
patiṉāyirat toṉṟu, āyirat torupaḵtu, patiṉāyirat torupaḵtu eṉa nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum irumoḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttum vantavāṟu.
muṉṟil, mīkaṇ - ivi ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu.
cōṇāṭu, pāṇṭināṭu - ivi ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntā marūu .
iṉi, niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāyp poruḷiyipu iṉṟi varutal niliyātu eṉṟalām.
e-ṭu :
  ‘karuṅkāl ōmik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉi’  	(akanā. 3:2)
‘irumputirit taṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
  paralaval aṭiya irali teṟippa  	(akanā. 4 : 34)
  ‘teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi aruḷāl’  	(cilap. 3 : 1)
ivvaṭikaḷil muṟiyē ōmicciṉi - maruppiṉ irali - teyva vari - eṉa oṭṭi ōmiyiṉatu ciṉi - maruppiṉi yuṭiya irali - teyvattaṉmiyiyuṭiya vari - eṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟavi, muṟiyē kāṇpu - paral - māl eṉpavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭiṉāṟ  pōla oṭṭi mikkum tirintum keṭṭum niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāyp poruḷiyipiṉṟi varutal kāṇka.	(i. vi. eḻut. 53 uri)
niṟutta col -
puṇarccikkuriya iraṇṭu coṟkaḷil mutalil koḷḷap paṭum col niṟuttacol eṉappaṭum nilimoḻiyām. itu kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyākiya varumoḻiyuṭaṉ kūṭumpōtu iyalpākavum tiripuṟṟum puṇarum. anniṟutta col perum pāṉmiyum peyarākavō viṉiyākavō irukkum; ciṟupāṉmi iṭiccollō uriccollō ātal kūṭum.	(to. e. 107 nac.)
niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyum aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟal -
aṭi eṉṟatu, ummittokiyiṉiyum irupeya roṭṭup paṇput tokiyiṉiyum. (aṭiyoṭu tōṉ ṟiyavaḻi nilimoḻi allatu varumoḻi ummittoki yākavum irupeya roṭṭup paṇputtokiyākavum niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.) avi allāta tokikaḷuḷ viṉittoki yum paṇputtokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ orucol eṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻittokiyum taṉakku vēṟoru muṭipu iṉmiyiṉ oru col eṉappaṭum. iṉi oḻinta vēṟṟumit tokiyum uvamattokiyum taṉṉiṉam muṭittal eṉpata ṉāl orucol eṉappaṭum. uṇṭa cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum avvāṟē oru col eṉappaṭum.
	e-ṭu	:	patiṉāyirat toṉṟu - nilimoḻi aṭi
			āyirat torupaḵtu - varumoḻi aṭi
			patiṉāyirat tirupaḵtu - irumoḻi aṭi
ivvaṭikaḷ orucollēyām.	(to. e. 111 iḷa.)
nī eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
nī eṉṉum muṉṉili orumip potuppeyar eḻuvāyāy varumpōtu nāṟkaṇattoṭum iyalpākavum, urupēṟkum pōtu niṉ eṉat tirintum, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyil varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu	:	nī kuṟiyi, ñāṉṟāy, valiyi, ariyi eṉa nāṟ kaṇattōṭum alvaḻiyil iyalpākap puṇarnta vāṟu.
niṉki, ñāṟci, valimi, aḻaku eṉa nī ‘niṉ’ eṉat tirintu varumoḻi nāṟkaṇattum vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil iyalpāka muṭintavāṟu.
uyir varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumiṭattē, ‘niṉ’ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram (taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟu ātaliṉ) iraṭṭiyatu. (ivviraṭṭutalum iyalpu puṇarcciyē eṉpa)
niṉ + puṟaṅkāppa = niṟpuṟaṅkāppa - eṉa iraṇṭaṉ tokikkaṇ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu puṇarnta vāṟu.	(to. e. 250, 253, 157 nac.)
nīṭṭal vikāram -
ceyyuḷ vikāram āṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ceyyuḷuḷ toṭi nayam nōkkik kuṟṟeḻuttu iṉamotta neṭilāka vikārap paṭuvatu nīṭṭal vikāramām.
	e-ṭu	:	‘pōttaṟār pullaṟivi ṉār’	(nālaṭi. 351)
pottu eṉpatē col. mēlaṭiyuḷ ‘tīttoḻilē’ eṉṟa mutaṟcīri nōkki etukivēṇṭip ‘pottaṟār’ eṉaṟ pālatu okaram nīṇṭu ‘pōttaṟār’ eṉṟu nīṭṭal vikāram āyiṟṟu, etukit toṭikku mutaṟcīrkaḷiṉ mutal eḻuttu aḷavottu niṟṟal vēṇṭutaliṉ.	(naṉ. 155 caṅ.)
nīṭavarutal ‘nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal’ aṉmi -
nīṭa varutalāvatu ceyyuṭkaṇ akara ikara ukarac cuṭṭukkaḷ nīṇṭu varumoḻiyōṭu ‘āyiṭi’ ‘īvayiṉāṉa’ ‘ūvayi ṉāṉa’ eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarvatākum. ‘nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal’ vikāram, (niḻal - nīḻal eṉṟāṟpōla) orumoḻik kaṇ nikaḻvatām ceyyuḷvikāram. ātaliṉ ivi tammuḷ vēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa.	(to. e. 208 nac. uri)
nīroṭu kūṭiya pāl -
nilimoḻip puḷḷiyīṟṟoṭu varumoḻi mutal uyir kūṭi (uyirmeyyāka) niṟṟal nīroṭu kūṭiya pālpōl niṟṟal eṉṟu oṟṟuminayam kūṟiṉār. uyirmey meyyiṉ māttiri tōṉṟātu uyireḻuttiṉ māttiriyē taṉ māttiriyāka niṟkum nayam oṟṟuminayamām. (mey muṉṉarum uyir piṉṉarumāka uccarikkappaṭuvatu ōcipaṟṟi varum vēṟṟumi nayam).	(i. vi. eḻut. 64 uri)
nunti  ‘nu’ kuṟṟiyalukaram aṉmi -
‘kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ, oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum’ eṉat tol. kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalām eṉṟāreṉiṉ,
‘nunti ukaram kuṟuki moḻimutaṟkaṇ
 vanta teṉiṉuyirmey yāmaṉittum - cantikku
 uyirmutalā vantaṇiyum meyppuṇarcci yiṉṟi
 mayalaṇiyum eṉṟataṉi māṟṟu’
eṉpatāl vitiyum vilakkum aṟintukoḷka.			(naṉ. 105 mayili. uri)

nunti  ‘nu’ kuṟṟiyalukaram ātal -
nakaram mēṟpal mutaliṭattu nānuṉi parantu oṟṟap piṟatta lāṉum, ukaram iraṇṭu itaḻum kuvittuk kūṟap piṟappatu ātalāṉum, iraṇṭu nakara olikaḷukku iṭiyē naṉku itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟum muyaṟci niliyāmiyāṉ, ukaram ari māttiri aḷavāka olikkum eṉpatu uyttuṇarattakkatu.
nunti ukaram kuṟuki moḻimutaṟkaṇ vantatu eṉṟāl, cantiyil, moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum uyirmeykaḷ ellām uṇmiyāṉa puṇarccinili māṟum eṉṟu koṇṭu, mayilinātar nunti kuṟṟiyalukaram aṉṟu eṉṟār.
ivaṉ + nunti = ivaṉunti;  ṉu:  orumāttiri yuṭiyatu.		(e. ā. pak. 68, 69)
nunti eṉpatiluḷḷa ukaram kuṟṟiyalukaramākavum muṟṟiyalu karamākavum olikkappaṭṭati nōkki iccūttiram kūṟiṉār.		(e. ku. pak. 76)
nuntāy eṉpatu nuntiyiṉ viḷiyākavum, num tāy eṉṟu poruḷ paṭavum eṉa irutiṟampaṭa varutaliṉ muṟṟiyalu karamām. (e.ku. pak. 77)	(to. e. 68 nac.)
‘num’ annili tiriyāmi -
num eṉṟa muṉṉilip paṉmiccol nāṉkaṉ urupoṭum āṟaṉ urupoṭum puṇaruṅkāl, taṉ - eṉ - niṉ - mutaliya neṭu mutal kuṟukum coṟkaḷip pōlavē, tāṉum kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāmiyum, īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum uṭiyatu.
	varumāṟu :	num + ku ḻū num + a + ku = numakku;
		num + atu ḻū num + a + atu ḻū num + a + tu = numatu;
		num + a ḻū num + a + a ḻū num + a + v + a = numava (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)	(to. e. 162 nac.)
num eṉṟa peyarp puṇarcci -
tolkāppiyaṉār num eṉpataṉip peyarākak koṇṭu aḵtu alvaḻippuṇarccikkaṇ nīir eṉattirintu varu moḻiyoṭu puṇarum eṉavum, vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇ īṟukeṭṭu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukkēṟṟa meli mikkum, meṉkaṇam variṉ am melleḻuttē mikkum puṇarum eṉavum, ai urupu ēṟkumiṭattu īṟṟu mey (m) iraṭṭiyum, nāṉkaṉurupum āṟaṉurupum ēṟkumiṭattu akaraccāriyi peṟṟum, nāṉkaṉurupiṟku varumoḻi valloṟṟu mikkum. ‘atu’ urupu ēṟkumiṭattu avvurupiṉ akaram keṭṭum, ‘kaṇ’ urupu ēṟkumiṭattu makaram keṭṭu varumoḻikkēṟpa ṅakara melloṟṟu mikkum puṇarum eṉavum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
	varumāṟu :	nīir kaṭiyīr, nīir nallīr - alvaḻi
		nuṅki, numcevi, nuntali (numpuṟam : iyalpu);
		nuññāṇ, nunnaṉmi, nummāṭci - vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci
		num + ai = nummi; num + a + k + ku = numakku; num + a + (a)tu = numatu; num + kaṇ = nuṅkaṇ - urupu puṇarcci	(to. e. 114, 115, 187, 325, 326 nac.)
num tam em nam īṟṟu makaram tiriyumāṟu -
nīr tām yām nām eṉṟa iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷākiya num tam em nam eṉpaṉavaṟṟu muṉ ñakara nakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattē, nilimoḻi īṟṟu makaram muṟiyē ñakara nakaraṅkaḷākat tiriyum. (nīr eṉpataṉ tiripākiya um eṉpataṟkum iḵtu okkum). itu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.
	e-ṭu	:	num, tam, em, nam + ñāṇ = nuññāṇ, taññāṇ, eññāṇ, naññāṇ; num, tam, em, nam, + nāṇ = nunnāṇ, tannāṇ, ennāṇ, nannāṇ
			um + ñāṇ, nāṇ = uññāṇ, unnāṇ
makara mutalmoḻi variṉ num+maṇi = nummaṇi eṉa iyal pākap puṇarum. piṟavaṟṟukkum koḷka.	(naṉ. 221)
num ‘nīir’ ātal -
nīir eṉpataṉit tol. ‘nummiṉ tiripeyar’ eṉṟār. num eṉpatu peyar vērccol; nīir eṉpatu ataṉ mutal vēṟṟumi ēṟṟa vaṭivam eṉṟu tol. kūṟiyuḷḷār.
num  eṉpatu ukaram keṭṭu īkāram peṟṟu nī eṉa ākum; num eṉpataṉ īṟṟu makaram rakara oṟṟāka, ‘nīr’ eṉa varum; atuvē iṭiyē ikaram vara ‘nīir’ eṉṟākum.
ākavē, num eṉpatu nīm nīr nīir eṉa muṟiyē tirintu mutalvēṟṟumi vaṭivam peṟum eṉpatu.	(to. e. 326 nac.)
piṟkālattār ‘nīir’ eṉpataṉ ikaratti aḷapeṭi yākak koṇṭu nīkki ‘nīr’ eṉpatē muṉṉilippaṉmip peyar eṉak koḷḷalāyiṉar. cilar nīir eṉpataṉi nīyir, nīvir eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkalāyiṉar.


nūlmarapiṉ peyarkkāraṇam -
nūlmarapu tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutal iya lākum. itu 33 nūṟpākkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itaṉ peyark kāraṇam paṟṟik karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa.
ivvatikārattāṉ collappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉi ōrāṟṟāṉ tokuttu uṇarttaliṉ nūlmarapu eṉṉum peyarpeṟṟatu ivviyal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
inta iyalil kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷ mūṉṟu atikārattiṟkum potuvātaliṉ nūlmarapu eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu iv viyal eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
eḻuttatikārattuḷ kūṟappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉit tokuttuṇarttutalāṉ atikāra marapu eṉappaṭuvataṉṟi nūlmarapu eṉappaṭātu eṉavum, inta iyalil kūṟappaṭuvaṉa ceyki iyalkaḷukkum poruḷatikārattil ceyyuḷiyal oṉṟaṟkumē karuviyāvataṉṟi, mūṉṟu atikārattukkum potu ākāmiyiṉ akkaruttup paṟṟi nūlmarapu eṉap peyariṭavilli eṉavum muṟiyē iḷampūraṇar uriyi yum nac. uriyiyum maṟuttuc civañāṉa muṉivar,
“nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyarkaḷ kūṟaliṉ nūlmarapu eṉṉum peyarttu. mali kaṭal yāṟu kuḷam eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu ulakamarapu paṟṟiya peyarkaḷ pōlātu, īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum eḻuttu kuṟil neṭil uyir uyirmey eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup peyarkaḷ nūliṉkaṇ āḷutaṟ poruṭṭu mutaṉūl āciriya ṉāl ceytukoḷḷappaṭṭamiyiṉ, ivi nūlmarapu paṟṟiya peyarāyiṉa. ēṉi ōttukkaḷuḷ vitikkappaṭum peyarkaḷum nūlmarapu paṟṟi varum peyarātal uṇarntu kōṭaṟku itu muṉ vikkappaṭṭatu” eṉṟār.(cū.vi. pak. 18)
‘nūl marapu ’ eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa nūl, nūl eḻututaṟku aṭippaṭik kāraṇamāya eḻuttu eṉṟa poruḷi uṇarttiṟṟup pōlum.	(e. ā. pak. 3)
nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyā marapu paṟṟiya kuṟikaḷi vitikkum iyal nūlmarapu.	(e. ku. pak. 3)
nūlmarapu kūṟum meymmayakkam -
k c t p eṉpaṉa nāṉku meykaḷum tammoṭu tāmē mayaṅkum.
r ḻ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭu meykaḷum tammoṭu piṟavē mayaṅkum.
ēṉiya paṉṉiraṇṭu meykaḷum tammoṭu tāmum tam moṭu piṟavum mayaṅkum.
ṭ ṟ l ḷ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉ k c p eṉpaṉa mayaṅkum.
avaṟṟuḷ, l ḷ - eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉmuṉ y v - eṉpaṉavum mayaṅkum.
ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa melliṉappuḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉ k c ṭ t p ṟ - eṉṟa valliṉappuḷḷikaḷ muṟiyē mayaṅkum.
avaṟṟuḷ, ṇ ṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉ k c ñ p m y v eṉṟa meykaḷum mayaṅkum.
ñ n m v eṉṟa meykaḷiṉ muṉ yakaramum mayaṅkum;  avaṟṟuḷ makarattiṉ muṉ vakaramum mayaṅkum.
y r ḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉmuṉ k c t n p m y v eṉpaṉavum ṅakaramum mayaṅkum - eṉṟu meymmayakkam kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
immeymayakkatti nac. orumoḻikkaṇṇatu eṉpar; ēṉiyōr irumoḻikkaṇṇatu eṉpar.	(to.e. 22-30 nac).
nūlmarapu: coṟporuḷ -
icippatum olippatumākiya oliyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ marapu kaḷi uṇarttum pakuti- eṉpatu ittoṭariṉ poruḷ. iḵtu āṟaṉ toki;  aṉmoḻit tokiyāṉ,  eḻut tolikaḷip paṟṟiya ilakkaṇam kūṟum iyali uṇartti niṉṟatu. nūl eṉpataṉ iyaṟporuḷ oliyeḻuttu. atu ‘ciṉiyiṟ kūṟum mutalaṟikiḷavi’ eṉṉum ākupeyarāṉ, puttakamākiya nūliṉi vaḻakkiṉkaṇ uṇarttalāyiṟṟu.  marapu eṉpatu maruvutal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyariṉ aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟiyīṭṭuc col; toṉṟutoṭṭu niyatiyāka varutal eṉṉum poruṭṭu. aḵtu īṇṭut tamiḻneṟi uṇarnta cāṉṟōrāṉ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkappeṟṟu varum ilakkaṇa neṟiyi uṇartti niṉṟatu eṉka. eṉavē, ivviya luḷ kūṟap peṟum eḻuttoli marapukaḷ tamiḻmoḻikkē uriyavi eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. 	(to. e. pak.  72 ca. pāla.)
nūlmarapu taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ iyalpē kūṟutal -
mutal nūṟpā - mutaleḻuttu, cārpeḻuttu: toki ; 2 - cār peḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar;3 - 13 - eḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttiri aḷavu mutalāyiṉa; 14 - 18 - cila eḻuttukkaḷiṉ varivarivu; 19 - 21 - meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaki; 22 - 30 - eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mayaṅkumāṟu;  31, 32 - cila uyireḻuttuk kaḷukku vēṟu peyar; 33 - eḻuttukkaḷiṉ aḷaviṉik kuṟittup piṟanūṟ koḷki;
ivvāṟu nūl eḻututaṟku aṭippaṭik kāraṇamākiya taṉi yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ iyalpē nūlmarapil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.			(e. ā. pak. 3)
nūlmarapu nuvalvaṉa -
eḻuttu 30, cārntu varum eḻuttu 3, kuṟṟuyir 5, neṭṭuyir 7, māttiri nīḷumāṟu, māttirikku aḷavu, uyir 12, mey 18, uyirmeykku aḷavu, mey cārpeḻuttu ivaṟṟiṉ aḷavu, makarak kuṟukkam, ataṉ vaṭivu, meyyiṉ iyaṟki, ekara okara iyaṟki, uyirmey iyalpu, uyirmey olikku māṟu, valliṉam melliṉam iṭiyiṉam eṉṟa meyvaki, meymmayakkam paṟṟiya marapu, cuṭṭu, viṉā, uyirum meyyum varampumīṟi olikkum iṭam - eṉpaṉa nūlmarapiṉuḷ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.	(to. e. 1 - 33 nac.)
nūlmarapu mūṉṟatikārak karuttum koṇṭami -
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum eḻuttatikārattiṟkum ceyyuḷiyaṟkum oppak kūṟiyaṉa. eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiya muppattumūṉṟaṉip patiṉintu ākki āṇṭut toki kōṭaliṉ, toki vēṟām. aḷavu, ceyyuḷiyaṟkum eḻuttikārattiṟkum otta aḷavum ovvā aḷavum uḷavākak kūṟiyatu. kuṟiṟkum neṭiṟkum kūṟiya māttiri iraṇṭiṭat tiṟkum otta aḷavu; āṇṭuk kūṟum ceyyuṭku aḷavu kōṭaṟku eḻuttatikārattukkup payaṉ tārāta aḷapeṭi kūṟiyatu ovvā aḷavu. aḵtu ‘aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum’ (e. 33) eṉṉum cūttirattōṭu āṇṭu māṭṭeṟintoḻuku māṟṟāṉ uṇarappaṭum. iṉṉum kuṟilum neṭilum mūvaki iṉamum āytamum vaṇṇattiṟkum eḻuttatikārattiṟkum oppak kūṟiyaṉa. muṟiyum eḻuttatikārattiṟkum ceyyu ḷiyaṟkum okkum. kūṭṭamum pirivum mayakkamum eḻuttati kārattiṟkē uriyaṉavākak kūṟiyaṉa. ‘ammūvāṟum’ (e. 22) eṉṉum cūttiram mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic collāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, collatikārattiṟkum ilakkaṇam īṇṭuk kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu atikārattiṟkum ilakkaṇam kūṟaliṉ ivvōttu nūliṉatu ilakkaṇam kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)

nūṟṟoṉṟu..... nūṟṟoṉpatu: puṇarnili -
nūṟu nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻikkaṇ oṉṟumutal oṉpatu īṟākiya eṇṇuppeyar variṉ, iṭiyē ṟakaroṟṟu mika nilimoḻi ‘nūṟṟu’ eṉṟu niṟpa, oṉṟu mutaliya uyir mutal moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyākumiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum. koṭuppavē, nūṟu + oṉṟu = nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉappuṇarum. nūṟṟu + mūṉṟu, nūṟṟu + nāṉku ivi iyalpākap puṇarum.
nūṟpāvuḷ ‘īṟu ciṉi oḻiya’ eṉpatu iṟuticciṉiyākiya ‘ṟu’ eṉṟa eḻuttuk keṭātu niṟpa eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.
‘oḻiyā’ eṉpatu pālacuntaraṉār pāṭam. ataṉ nuṭpat tiṉiyum uṇarka.	(e. ā. pak. 176, to. e. 472 nac.)
nūṟāyiram, nilimoḻikaḷāṉa oṉṟu mutal oṉpatukāṟum uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu puṇartal -
oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ nilimoḻikaḷāka, varumoḻiyāka ‘nūṟāyiram’ varumvaḻi, nūṟu eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu muṭintāṟ pōlavē vikāram eyti muṭiyum.
	varumāṟu :	oṉṟu + nūṟāyiram = orunūṟāyiram; iraṇṭu + nūṟāyiram = irunūṟāyiram; mūṉṟu + nūṟāyiram = munnūṟāyiram; nāṉku + nāṟāyiram = nāṉū ṟāyiram; aintu + nūṟāyiram = ainnūṟā yiram; āṟu + nūṟāyiram = aṟunūṟāyiram; ēḻ + nūṟāyiram = eḻunūṟāyiram; eṭṭu + nūṟā yiram = eṇṇūṟāyiram; oṉpatu + nūṟāyiram = oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram.
nūṟu, nilimoḻikaḷāṉa oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkāṟum uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu puṇartal -
	oṉṟu + nūṟu	=	orunūṟu	-	(to. e. 460 nac.)
	iraṇṭu + nūṟu	=	irunūṟu		-	(to. e. 460 nac.)
	mūṉṟu + nūṟu	=	munnūṟu		-	(to. e. 461 nac.)
	nāṉku + nūṟu	=	nāṉūṟu		-	(to. e. 462 nac.)
	aintu + nūṟu	=	ainnūṟu		-	(to. e. 462 nac.)
	āṟu + nūṟu		=	aṟunūṟu		-	(to. e. 460 nac.)
	ēḻ + nūṟu		=	eḻunūṟu		-	(to. e. 392 nac.)
	eṭṭu + nūṟu	=	eṇṇūṟu		-	(to. e. 460 nac.)
	oṉpatu + nūṟu	=	toḷḷāyiram	-	(to. e. 463 nac.)
neṭṭeḻuttimpark kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟiyaṟkiḻami yātal -
toḻiṟpaṇpum kuṇappaṇpum oruporuḷiṉiṉṟum pirikka muṭiyāta toṭarpuṭiyaṉa ātaliṉ, ivi āṟām vēṟṟumit taṟkiḻamip poruḷil oṉṟiyaṟkiḻami eṉṟa pakuppiṉpāṟ paṭṭaṉa.
neṭṭeḻuttimpark kuṟṟiyalukaram accolloṭu vēṟallatāka, accolloṭu pirikkamuṭiyāta toṭar puṭiyatu ātaliṉ aḵtu oṉṟiyaṟ kiḻamittāyiṟṟu, nilattatu akalam eṉpuḻi akalam nilattiṉiṉṟu pirikka muṭiyātu oṉṟupaṭṭiruppatu pōla.
kāṭu - neṭṭeḻuttimpark kuṟṟiyalukaram (ṭakaram paṟṟuk kōṭu)
iccolluḷ īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu iṇintuḷḷami kāṇka.	(to. e. 36 nac. uri)
neṭṭeḻuttu vēṟu peyarkaḷ -
neṭumi eṉiṉum, tīrkkam eṉiṉum neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum oruporuṭkiḷavi.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 11)
‘neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutal’ -
ṇ ṉ m l ḷ eṉṟa aintu oṟṟukkaḷum taṉineṭili aṭuttu nilimoḻi iṟutiyil vara, varumoḻikkaṇ mutalil nakaram variṉ, nilimoḻi īṟṟu oṟṟuk keṭum. varumoḻikkaṇ mutalil takaram variṉ, l ḷ oṟṟukkaḷ keṭum.
	e-ṭu :	kōṇ + nimirntatu = kōṇimirntatu; tāṉ + nallaṉ = tāṉallaṉ; tām + nallar = tānallar; pāl + naṉṟu = pāṉaṉṟu; kōḷ + naṉṟu = kōṇaṉṟu; pāl + tītu = pāṟītu; kōḷ + tītu = kōṭītu.
neṭilukkuk kūṟiya viti kuṟiliṇi, kuṟil neṭil ivaṟṟukkum okkum.
	e-ṭu :	viral + tītu = viraṟītu,	kuṟaḷ + naṉṟu = kuṟaṇaṉṟu
		varāl + tītu = varāṟītu, paraṇ + naṉṟu = paraṇaṉṟu
(to. e. 160 nac.)
neṭil -
ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṟa ēḻum uyirneṭilām. ivi 18 meyyuṭaṉ kūṭa 18 o 7 = 126 uyirmey neṭilām. ivi taṉittaṉi iraṇṭumāttiri peṟum. aḷapeṭikkaṇ aikāra neṭilukku ikaramum, oḷakāra neṭilukku ukaramum iṉakkuṟilākak koḷḷappaṭum.
	e-ṭu	:	vilii, kuu.
neṭil, neṭumi, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉa oruporuḷaṉa.			(naṉ. 65)


neṭil uyirttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcciyuḷ cila -
neṭil uyirttoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ cila varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu vēṟṟumiyil iṉoṟṟu (ṭ, ṟ) mikāmiyum, alvaḻiyil mikutalum uḷavām.
	e-ṭu :
‘kāṭakam iṟantārkkē’	(yā.vi. mēṟ.)
‘nāṭu kiḻavōṉē’	(poruna. 248
‘kaṟi miṭaṟaṇiyalum aṇintaṉṟu’	(puṟanā. kaṭavuḷ.)
ivi vēṟṟumikkaṇ mikāvāyiṉa.
(mikutalāvatu ṭaṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutalāṉ varu mikuti.)
kāṭṭaraṇ, kuruṭṭuk kōḻi, muruṭṭup puliyaṉ; kaḷiṟṟi yāṉi, veḷiṟṟup paṉi, eyiṟṟuppal; ivi alvaḻik kaṇ mikkaṉa.  verukkukkaṇ, eruttukkāl (cevi tali puṟam) - eṉap piṟa meykaḷ mikkaṉa.	(naṉ. 182 mayili.)
neṭiltoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram -
moḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttu ukaram ēṟiya valloṟṟu āṟaṉuḷ (ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu) oṉṟāka, ayaleḻuttut taṉineṭilāka iruppiṉ, avvīṟṟu ukaram neṭiltoṭark kuṟṟiya lukaram eṉappaṭum. itaṉit tol. īreḻuttoru moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṉum.
	e-ṭu	:	nāku, kācu, kāṭu, kātu, veḷapu, yāṟu	(naṉ. 94)
neṭilōṭu uyirttoṭar iraṭṭal -
ṭakara ṟakara meykaḷi ūrntu varum kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟkum neṭiltoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukarac col muṉṉum uyirt toṭarkkuṟṟiyalukarac col muṉṉum nāṟkaṇamum varu miṭattu, vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ  ṭakara ṟakara oṟṟukkaḷ perumpāṉmiyum iraṭṭum.
	e-ṭu	:	āṭu + kāl, mayir, valimi, aṭi = āṭṭukkāl, 					āṭṭumayir, āṭṭu valimi, āṭṭaṭi
			pāṟu + kāl, mayir, valimi, aṭi = pāṟṟukkāl, 					pāṟṟumayir, pāṟṟuvalimi, pāṟṟaṭi
			muruṭu + kāl, niṟam, valimi, aṭi = 							muruṭṭukkāl, muruṭṭuniṟam, muruṭṭuvalimi,					 muruṭṭaṭi.
			muyiṟu + kāl, niṟam, valimi, aṭi = 							muyiṟṟukkāl, muyiṟṟuniṟam, muyiṟṟuvalimi, 					muyiṟṟaṭi.
ciṟupāṉmi vēṟṟumikkaṇ iraṭṭāmiyum, ciṟu pāṉmi alvaḻikkaṇ iraṭṭutalum, ciṟupāṉmi iruvaḻi yilum piṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum koḷka.
	e-ṭu	:	nāṭu kiḻavōṉ, ‘kaṟimiṭa ṟaṇiyalum aṇin					 taṉṟu’ (puṟanā. kaṭavuḷ.) - vēṟṟumiyil iraṭṭā					 vāyiṉa.
			kāṭṭaraṇ, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi - alvaḻiyil iraṭṭiṉa.
			veruku + kaṇ = verukkuk kaṇ - vēṟṟumi
			erutu + māṭu = eruttumāṭu - alvaḻi  piṟa 						oṟṟukkaḷ iraṭṭiṉa.
neṭilōṭu uyirttoṭarkkaṇ ṭakara ṟakara oṟṟu iraṭṭāmi -
	e-ṭu :
‘kāviri purakkum nāṭukiḻa vōṉē’	(poruna. īṟṟaṭi)
‘kāṭakam iṟantārkkē ōṭumeṉ maṉaṉēkāṇ’	(yā. vi. mēṟ.)
‘kaṟimiṭaṟu aṇiyalum aṇintaṉṟu’	(puṟanā. kaṭavuḷ.)
ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.	(naṉ. 183 caṅkara.)
neṭuṅkaṇakkil āytattiṉ iṭam -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷuḷ āytam akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāl piṟattalāṉum, uyir ēṟātu ōcivikāramāy niṟpatu oṉṟēṉum eḻuttiyal taḻā ōci pōlak koḷḷiṉum koḷḷaṟka, eḻuttēyām eṉṟu oṟṟiṉpāṟpaṭuttaṟkup ‘puḷḷi’ eṉap peyar peṟutalā ṉum, uyirum oṟṟumākiya iraṇṭiṟkumiṭiyē vikkap paṭṭatu.	(i. vi. 8 uri)
neṭuṅkaṇakkil āytattiṉ iṭam paṟṟi vīracōḻiyattiṟ kaṇṭatu -
āytam uyiriyum meyyiyum cārntu iṭiyē varutaliṉ, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil uyir paṉṉiraṇṭiyum aṭuttu mey patiṉeṭṭaṉ muṉṉar uyirkkum meykkum naṭuvē vikkap paṭṭatu. (āytam uyirpōla alaku peṟṟum mey pōla alaku peṟāmalum ceyyuḷuḷ vaḻaṅkum iru nili miyum uṭimiyāl, aḵtu uyirum meyyumākiya avaṟṟiṭiyē vikkappaṭṭatu eṉalām)	(vī. cō. cantip. 1)

neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ amippu muṟi -
eḻuttukkaḷukku ellām akaram mutalātaliṉ mutaṟkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu. vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya āṇmakaṉi uṇarttum ciṟappāṉ ṉakaram piṉvittār. kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi muṉ ṉākak kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉam otta neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟiṉār, oru māttiri kūṟiyē iraṇṭu māttiri kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ.
akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaram eṇṇum piṟappum poruḷum ottaliṉ vittār. ikarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaram vittār, piṟappu ovvātēṉum cuṭṭupporuṭṭāy niṟkiṉṟa iṉam karuti. ekaram ataṉ piṉ vittār, akara ikaraṅkaḷoṭu piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi. aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ iṉamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttu ilavēṉum, piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ vittār. okaram no eṉa meyyōṭu kūṭiniṉṟallatu tāṉāka ōreḻut torumoḻi ākāta ciṟappiṉmi nōkki aikārattiṉ piṉvittār. iṉṉum aā, uū, oō oḷa - ivi tammuḷ vaṭivu okkum; i ī ai vaṭivu ovvā.
iṉi, kakāraṅakāramum, cakāra ñakāramum, ṭakāra ṇakāramum, takāra nakāramum, pakāra makāramum tamakkup piṟappum ceykiyum ottaliṉ, valloṟṟiṭiyē melloṟṟuk kalantu vittār. mutalnāvum mutalaṇṇamum, iṭinā vum iṭiyaṇṇamum, nuṉināvum nuṉiaṇṇamum, itaḻ iyitalum ākip piṟakkiṉṟa iṭattiṉ muṟimi nōkki avveḻuttukkaḷik ka ca ṭa ta pa, ṅa ña ṇa na ma eṉa immuṟiyē vittār. piṟappuoppumiyāṉum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tiritalāṉum ṟakāramum ṉakāramum cēravittār. ivi tamiḻ eḻuttu  eṉṟu aṟivittaṟkup piṉṉar vittār.
iṉi iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷil yakaram muṉ vittār, aḵtu uyir pōla miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷi aṇṇam kaṇṇuṟṟa aṭiyap piṟattaliṉ. rakaram ataṉoṭu piṟappu ovvātē ṉum ceyki ottaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. lakāramum vakāramum tammuḷ piṟappum ceykiyum ovvāvēṉum, kāl valitu - col valitu eṉṟāṟ pōlat tammil cērntu varum coṟkaḷ perumpāṉmi eṉpatu paṟṟi, lakāramum vakāramum cēra vittār. ḻakāramum ḷakāramum iyipila vēṉum, ‘iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa’ eṉṟāl canta iṉpattiṟku iyipuṭimi paṟṟic cēravittār pōlum.		(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
(eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṟi vippu (2) - kāṇka.)
‘neṭunīrmi aḷapu’ eṉa vīracōḻiyam kuṟippiṭuvatu -
vaṭamoḻi marapi oṭṭi oli nīṇṭa neṭṭeḻuttiyē aḷapeṭi eṉkiṟatu vīracōḻiyam. neṭunīrmiyuṭiya tāvatu uyiraḷapeṭi. (āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārkku neṭilic cārntolikkum kuṟilē aḷapeṭi yeḻut tām).	(vī. cō. cantip. 2)
neṭumutal kuṟukāta iyalpāṉa cāriyikaḷ puṇarumāṟu -
tam nam num - eṉṟa cāriyi iṭiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāka amintavi. avi tām nām nīir eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripu alla.
ellām ellīr ellār - eṉpaṉa urupēṟkumiṭattu iṭiyē varum nam num tam eṉpaṉa, nām nīir tām eṉṟa peyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu nam num tam eṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki akaraccāriyi peṟumāṟu pōla, akaraccāriyi peṟṟu, varumoḻiyāka varum urupoṭu puṇarum.
(īṇṭu urupukaḷ kuvvum atuvum eṉka.)
	varumāṟu :	ellām + ku ḻū ellām + nam + a + ku + um = ellānamakkum; ellīr + ku ḻū ellīr + num + a + ku + um = ellīr numakkum; ellār + ku ḻū ellār + tam + a + ku + um = ellār tamakkum eṉṟu, iṟutiyil ummuccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum.
eṉavē, nam num tam eṉṟa cāriyi iṭiccoṟkaḷ vēṟu; nām nīir tām eṉpaṉa mutal kuṟuki varum nam num tam eṉpaṉa vēṟu eṉpatu.	(to. e. 161 nac. uri)
neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaḷ -
yāṉ nī tāṉ yām nām tām eṉpaṉa urupēṟkumiṭattu neṭumutal kuṟuki eṉ niṉ taṉ em nam tam eṉṟu niṟpaṉavām.
	e-ṭu	:	yāṉ + ku ḻū eṉ + a + ku = eṉakku
			nī + ku ḻū niṉ + a + ku = niṉakku	(to. e. 161 nac.)
yāṉ yām nām nī nīr tāṉ tām eṉpaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa. ivi muṟiyē neṭumutal kuṟuki, eṉ em nam niṉ num taṉ tam eṉṟāki vēṟṟumiyurupu ēṟkum. eṉṉi eṉṉāl eṉakku eṉṉiṉ eṉatu eṉkaṇ eṉa āṟurupukaḷōṭum piṟavaṟṟiyum oṭṭik kāṇka. (nāṉkāvatum āṟāvatum varuvaḻi, neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟavi akaraccāriyi peṟum eṉka.)	(naṉ. 247)
nel eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
nel eṉṟa poruṭpeyar varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum lakaram ṟakaramākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	nel + kaṭitu = neṟkaṭitu - alvaḻi
			nel + kaṭumi = neṟkaṭumi - vēṟṟumi
(to. e. 371 nac.)
nel cel kol col : muṭiyumāṟu -
innāṉku peyarkaḷum vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻip potuvitiyāṉ (iyalpātalum lakaram ṟakaramākat tiritalum ākiya) uṟaḻcci peṟātu, alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumip puṇarcci pōla lakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu muṭiyum.
	varumāṟu :	neṟkaṭitu ceṟkaṭitu koṟkaṭitu coṟ kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu.
(cel - mēkam; kol - kolluttoḻil; aṉṟik kollaṉumām).	(naṉ. 232 caṅkara.)
‘neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷavi’ -
oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coltaṉmi kuṟinta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷ. ivi uyirīṟṟavākavum, puḷḷiyīṟṟavākavum varum; kuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ici paṟṟiyum, paṇpu paṟṟiyum varum. ivi ulakavaḻakkil maruvi varuvaṉa. ivaṟṟi nili moḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarcciviti kūṟavēṇṭuvatu iṉṟu.
	e-ṭu	:	kaṇ viṇṇa viṇittatu, viṇ viṇittatu - kuṟippu; āṭi veḷḷa viḷarttatu, veḷ viḷart tatu - paṇpu; kaṭal olla olittatu, ol olittatu - ici; viṇṇa, veḷḷa, olla : uyirīṟu; viṇ, veḷ, ol : puḷḷiyīṟu.
viṇṇa viṇ, veḷḷa veḷ, olla ol eṉa ovvōr uriccollum uyirīṟākavum puḷḷiyīṟākavum varutaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakkalākāmiyiṉ, ‘neṟippaṭa vārā’ vāyiṉa.	(to. e. 482 nac. uri)
nēmināta eḻuttatikāram -
nēminātam eṉṉum ciṉṉūlil eḻuttatikāram iyalpakup piṉṟi uḷḷatu. itaṉkaṇ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutalvippu, tuṇivippu, piṟappu, mutalnili, iṟutinili, vaṭamoḻi yākkam, vaṭamoḻi etirmaṟi muṭipu, potuppuṇarcci, uṭampaṭumey, uyirīṟṟuc cantimuṭivu, oṟṟiṟṟuc canti muṭivu, kuṟṟiyalukarac cantimuṭivu, eṇṇuppeyarp puṇarcci, pakupata amippu mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṇalām. iḵtu irupattu nāṉku veṇpāc cūttiraṅkaḷāl ākiyatu.
nēminātam -
tiripuvaṉa tēvaṉ eṉṟa peyariya mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉ (ki.pi. 1070 - 1118) kālattil toṇṭināṭṭup poṉviḷinta kaḷattūr eṉappaṭṭa kaḷanti eṉṉum ūril tōṉṟiya kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar eṉṉum camaṇac cāṉṟavar, teṉ mayilā puri nīlniṟakkaṭavuḷ nēminātar peyarāl, tolkāppiyatti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭu eḻuttu - col - eṉṟa iraṇṭu atikāraṅkaḷip pāyirattoṭu 97 veṇpākka ḷāl nēmi nātam eṉṉum ilakkaṇanūlāka iyaṟṟiṉār. innūl ciṉṉūl eṉṟa peyarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ eḻuttatikārattē iyalpakuppu illi; collatikāram tolkāppiyac collatikāram pōla oṉpatu iyal kaḷik koṇṭatu. innūṟku ‘vayiramēka virutti’ eṉṟa aḻakiya curukkamāṉa uri uḷḷatu.
no, tu mūviṉattoṭum puṇarumāṟu -
no, tu ivviraṇṭum (muṉṉili orumi ēval) viṉi yātaliṉ, nokkoṟṟā noccāttā nottēvā nop pūtā - eṉavum, noññeḷḷā nonnākā nom māṭā eṉavum, noyyavaṉā novvaḷavā - eṉavum, mūviṉam varuvaḻiyum avvammeyyē mikkaṉa. tu eṉpataṉoṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka.	(naṉ. 158, 165 caṅkara.)


pa
prakruti pāva santi -
tirinta puṇarccikaḷ mūṉṟu. avi mey piṟitātal (ātēcam), kuṉṟal (lōpam), mikutal (ākamam) eṉpaṉa.
mūṉṟu tiripukaḷuḷ, yātāṉum oṉṟu varavēṇṭiya iṭattil atu varappeṟātē iyalpākap puṇarvatu iyalpupuṇarcci eṉappaṭum. itu vaṭamoḻiyil pirakiruti pāva santi eṉappaṭum.
meypiṟitātalit tirital eṉavum, kuṉṟalik keṭutal eṉavum, mikutalit tōṉṟal eṉavum, tirinta puṇarcciyi vikārap puṇarcci eṉavum naṉṉūl kūṟum.	(e. ā. pak. 92)
pakāppatam -
pakupatam pōlap pakuttup pārttāl pakuti vikuti mutaliya payaṉ viḷiviṉṟi, iṭukuṟiyāyp pakukkappaṭāmal pakuti māttiramēyāy niṟkum peyar - viṉi - iṭi - uri - eṉṟa nālvakic collum pakāppatamām.
	e-ṭu	:	nilam, nīr; naṭa, vā; maṉ, koṉ; uṟu, kaḻi	(naṉ. 131)
pakāppatam, pakupatam taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum puṇartal -
	e-ṭu	:	poṉmuṭi, poṉkoḷ - peyaroṭu peyar, peyaroṭu viṉi; vā pō, uṇ cāttā - viṉi yoṭu viṉi, viṉiyoṭu peyar; ‘atu maṟṟamma tāṉē’ (cīvaka. 2790) - iṭiyōṭu iṭi; ‘atu kol tōḻi kāma nōyē’ (kuṟun. 5) - peyarōṭu iṭi; ‘kommik kuḻaku āṭum’ - uriyōṭu uri; ‘mallaṟ celvamoṭu’ - uriyoṭu peyar;
patam nāṉkum taṉittaṉiyē taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum puṇarntaṉa.
	maliyaṉ maṉṉavaṉ, maliyaṉ maṉ - peyarp pakupatattoṭu peyarppakupatamum peyarppakāppatamum.
uṇṭāṉ tiṉṟāṉ, uṇṭāṉ cāttaṉ - viṉip pakupatat toṭu viṉippakupatamum peyarp pakāp patamum.
pakupatam taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum taṉittaṉiyē puṇarntaṉa.
(naṉ. 150 mayili.)
pakāppatam pōlvaṉa -
pakāppatam pakukkappaṭāta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē mikkuc cellum eṉavē, piṉvarum pataṅkaḷ muṭinta iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟuvaraviṟṟākap pakāppatam koḷḷappaṭum. avi pakukkappaṭiṉum pakāppatam pōlvaṉavē eṉṟavāṟu. avi varumāṟu :
avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, tamaṉ, tamaḷ, tamar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. ivi īṟu pakukkappaṭiṉum pakuti vēṟu poruḷpaṭātaṉa.
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa cāttiyuṭiyāṉ cāttaṉ, koṟṟi yuṭiyāṉ koṟṟaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla īṟu pakukkappaṭṭup pakuti vēṟu poruḷpaṭum; iṭukuṟimāttiri yāyē niṟkumiṭattu avvāṟu poruḷpaṭā.
kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻapuram, cōḻaṉalaṅkiḷḷi, pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa nilimeḻi varumoḻiyākap pakukkappaṭṭup pala poruḷ uṇarttiṉum, toṉṟutoṭṭu  orupiṇṭamāya oru poruḷiyē uṇartti niṟpaṉa.
mēlum, cāttaṉ cātti muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉṟāṟpōlum viravuppeyarkaḷ pakutivikutiyākap pakukkappaṭiṉum, vikuti vakiyāṉ uyartiṇi orumiyē yaṉṟi aḵṟiṇi orumiyum ivi uṇarttum eṉṟaṟku viti illāmiyāl, uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravi varum viravuppeyar kaḷum ciṟupāṉmi pakāppatam pōlvaṉavām.			(i. vi. eḻut. 40)
pakutipporuḷ vikuti -
tamakkeṉa oruporuḷiṉṟip pakutiyiṉ poruḷē tamporu ḷāyp pakutiyōṭu iṇintu pakutittaṉmip paṭṭup piṉ iṭinili vikutikaḷōṭu iṇintu col lākkattukku utavuvaṉa. iru, iṭu mutaliyaṉa pakutipporuḷ vikuti. eḻuntiruntāṉ, eḻuntiṭṭāṉ eṉpuḻi, iru iṭu eṉpaṉa pakutipporuḷ vikutiyāy, mutaṉiliyōṭu iyitaṟkuriya tuccāriyi peṟṟu eḻuntiru eḻuntiṭu - eṉṟu niṉṟavaḻi, aviyum mutaṉilit taṉmippaṭṭu mēlvarum vikuti mutaliyaṉa ēṟkum. eḻuntirukkiṉṟāṉ eḻuntiruppāṉ eḻuntiṭukiṉṟāṉ eḻuntiṭuvāṉ eṉpaṉa muṟiyē nikaḻkālam etirkālam kāṭṭum. coṟkaḷil iru iṭu - eṉṟa pakutipporuḷ vikuti vantamikku eṭuttukkāṭṭām.			(cū. vi. pak. 41)
pakutiyi iṭippakāppatam eṉal -
‘mutaṉilip peyarviṉi’ eṉṉātu ‘tattam pakāppataṅkaḷ’ eṉṟamiyiṉ, iruvakip pakupatattuḷḷum (peyarp pakupatam, viṉip pakupatam) pakutiyum ōr uṟuppu ātaliṉ vikuti mutaliyavaṟṟoṭu toṭarntu niṟpataṉṟip pirintu nillāmiyāṉum, pirintavaḻip pakutiyāy uṟuppiṉ poruḷ tārāmiyāṉum iruvakip pakuti uṟuppukkaḷum ‘iṭippakāppatam’ eṉpatu peṟṟām.	(naṉ. 134 caṅkara.)
pakuti mutaliya āṟu -
pakuti, perumpālum vēṟupaṭātu pakupatattiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṟpatu; vikuti, vēṟupaṭṭu iṟutikkaṇ niṟpatu. iṭinili, perumpālum iṉiyatu ittuṇiyatu eṉa aḷattaṟku aritāyp patam muṭippuḻik kālamum poruṇmiyum kāṭṭi āṇṭē kāṇappaṭuvatu. cāriyi, aṉ āṉ mutalāka eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ellāp puṇarccikkum potuvākac ciṟupāṉmi poruḷnilikku utaviceytu perumpālum iṉṉoliyē payaṉāka varuvatu. canti, iṉṉatu vantāl iṉṉatu ām eṉap puṇarvaḻit tōṉṟum ceyki. vikāram, ceyyuḷ toṭiyum patattuḷ aṭippāṭum oliyum kāraṇamāka valittal melittal mutalāka ākkuvatu. iḵtu iṭinili mutaliyavaṟṟāl muṭiyātavaḻi varuvatu.			(i. vi. eḻut. 42 uri)
taṉ iyalpiṉāṉ niṟpatu paṟṟip pakuti eṉṟum, avvāṟu niṟkum pakutipporuḷit taṉṉakappaṭap piṉṉiṉṟu vikārappaṭuttalāl vikuti eṉṟum, pakutivikutikaḷiṉ iṭi niṟpatāl iṭinili eṉṟum, pakuti vikutikaḷic cārntu iyintu niṟṟalāl cāriyi eṉṟum, nilimoḻi varu moḻi cantittatāl uṇṭākiya tōṉṟal tirital keṭutal kaḷic canti eṉṟum, ceyyuḷ aṭi toṭi mutaliya vaṟṟāl valittal melittal mutaliya vikārappaṭutalāl vikāram eṉṟum kāraṇakkuṟiyāy vantaṉa.	(naṉ. 133 irāmā.)
pakupatam -
iṭukuṟiyāy niṟkum pakāppatam pōlātu, pakuti vikuti yākavum avaṟṟōṭu ēṉiya uṟuppukkaḷākavum pakukkap paṭum, poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉṉu mivi paṟṟi ivi kāraṇamāka varum peyarkaḷum, terinili yāyum kuṟippāyum kālattik koḷḷum muṟṟu viṉi yeccam peyareccam eṉṉum viṉiccoṟkaḷum pakupata mām. viṉimuṟṟup pakupatam eṉavē, ammuṟṟu vēṟṟumi koḷḷa varum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum pakupatamām. puḷi, kaṭu mutaliya ākupeyarkaḷ kāraṇattāṉ varumēṉum vikuti mutaliya uṟuppu iṉmiyiṉ avi pakupatam ākā. naṭa, vā mutaliyaṉa vikutiyoṭu puṇarāmiyiṉ pakupatam ākā eṉak koḷka.
	e-ṭu	:	poṉṉaṉ, akattaṉ, ātiriyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, 						kariyaṉ, ūṇaṉ - peyarp pakupatam āṟu.
			naṭantāṉ, naṭakkiṉṟāṉ, naṭappāṉ - terinili 					uṭaṉpāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu.
			naṭanta, naṭakkiṉṟa, naṭakkum - terinili uṭaṉ 					pāṭṭup peyareccam.
			naṭantu, naṭakka, naṭakkiṉ - terinili uṭaṉpāṭṭu 					viṉiyeccam.
			naṭavāṉ - etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟu
			naṭavāta; naṭavāmal - etirmaṟip peyarecca 						viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ
			poṉṉaṉ, akattaṉ, ātiriyaṉ - kuṟippu 						viṉimuṟṟu
			kariya, periya; aṉṟi, iṉṟi - kuṟippup peyarecca 					viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ
			naṭantāṉ, naṭantavaṉ - terinili viṉiyā 						laṇiyum peyar
			poṉṉaṉ.... ūṇaṉ - kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum 				peyar.	(naṉ. 132 caṅ.)
pakuti vikuti iṭinilikaḷ -
poruḷ āti aṟuvakip pakāppataṅkaḷē pakupataṅkaṭkup pakutikaḷām. pōl, nikar - ivi iṭip  pakutikaḷām. cāl, māṇ - ivi urip pakutikaḷām. cemmi, ciṟumi - ivi paṇpup peyarākiya vikārap pakutikaḷām. pukkāṉ, peṟṟāṉ, viṭṭāṉ eṉṟum (pakuti vikārappaṭṭu) varum.
kēḷ, koḷ, cel, tā, cā, vā, kal, col - iviyum vikārappakutikaḷām. (viṉiccollāṅkāl, ivi vikārappaṭuvaṉa.)
uḻu, toḻu, uṇ, tiṉ - ivi iyalpup pakutikaḷām.
aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ, ar, ār, tu, ai, a - piṟavum aimpāṟ peyarp pakupata vikutiyē.
kālam kāṭṭā iṭinilikaḷ āmāṟu uṇarttutum:
peyarppakāppatamum viṉippakāppatamum ākiya pakuti niṟutti naccāriyiyum ñaccāriyiyum iṭiniliyāka vittu avvappālukkuriya vikutiyi īṟṟiṉkaṇṇē tantatu pakupatamām.
kiḷi iḷi kaṭi naṭi - eṉap peyarppakutiyum, aṟi tuṇi kuṟi moḻi - eṉa viṉippakutiyum niṟutti, n ñ - eṉa iṭiniliyum ar eṉa iṟutiniliyum kūṭṭik kiḷinar iḷinar kaṭinar naṭinar - eṉṟum, kiḷiñar iḷiñar kaṭiñar naṭiñar - eṉṟum, aṟinar tuṇinar kuṟinar moḻinar - eṉṟum, aṟiñar tuṇiñar kuṟiñar moḻiñar - eṉṟum puṇarntu varutal kāṇka.
‘naṇṇalum neṟiyē’ eṉṟa mikiyāl, valicci vaṇṇātti - ivaṟṟuḷ cakarameyyum takarameyyum iṭiniliyāyiṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(to. vi. 83 - 85 uri)
paṭuttal ōciyāl peyarātal -
tāḻnta ōciyāṉ kūṟuvatu paṭuttalōciyām. viṉiyip peyarākka vēṇṭiya iṭattum uriccollip peyarākka vēṇṭiya iṭattum paṭuttalōciyāl kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
evaṉ eṉpatu paṭuttalōciyāl peyarāyiṟṟu.		(to. co. 221 nac. uri)
aḵtu iyalpāka viṉāviṉikkuṟippuc col.
tev eṉpatu uriccollāyiṉum, paṭuttalōciyāl peyarāyiṟṟu.	(to. e. 184 nac. uri)
‘paṭu’ vikuti ceyappaṭuporuḷ uṇarttal -
‘eṉappaṭupa’ eṉpatu ‘eṉ’ eṉṉum mutaṉilimītu ceyappaṭu poruḷ uṇarttum ‘paṭu’ vikutiyum akarac cāriyiyum vantu puṇarntu ‘eṉappaṭu’ eṉa niṉṟavaḻi, atuvum mutaṉilit taṉmippaṭṭu, mēl varum akaravikutiyum pakariṭi niliyum peṟṟu ‘eṉappaṭupa’ eṉa muṭinta palavaṟicol.
‘ilvāḻvāṉ eṉpāṉ’ (kuṟaḷ 41) eṉpataṉic ceyappaṭu poruḷ uṇarttum paṭu vikutiyi virittē ‘eṉappaṭuvāṉ’ eṉṟu poruḷ ceytalāṉum paṭu vikuti ceyappaṭuporuḷ uṇarttum iyalpiṟṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. (cū.vi. pak. 40,41)
paṇputtoki iruvaki -
paṇpu tokka tokiyum, peyar tokka tokiyum eṉap paṇputtoki iruvakippaṭum. karuṅkuvaḷi eṉpatu paṇpu tokka tokiyām; āyaṉ cāttaṉ eṉpatu peyar tokka tokiyām.	(naṉ. 152 irāmā.)
paṇpup pakāppataṅkaḷ -
cemmi, ciṟumi. cēymi, tīmi, vemmi, putumi, meṉmi, mēṉmi, tiṇmi, uṇmi, nuṇmi eṉpaṉa vum, ivaṟṟukku maṟutaliyāṉa veṇmi karumi poṉmi pacumikaḷ, perumi, aṇimi, naṉmi, taṇmi, paḻimi, vaṉmi, kīḻmi, noymmi, iṉmi, parumi eṉpaṉavum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum paṇpup pakāppatam.			(naṉ.134 mayili.)
ikkūṟiya vāypāṭukaḷ ellām col niliyāl pakupata māyiṉum, mivikutikkup pakutipporuḷaṉṟi vēṟuporu ḷiṉmiyiṉ, poruḷniliyāl pakukkappaṭā eṉpār ‘paṇpiṟ pakā nilippatam’ eṉṟār.	(naṉ. 135 caṅkara.)
paṇpup pakutikkuc ciṟappuviti -
paṇpup pakutikaḷākiya karu, cev mutaliyaṉa mivikutiyoṭu cērntu karumi, cemmi mutaliyaṉavākip paṇpup poruḷi ṉiṉṟum vēṟuporuḷ vakukkappaṭāta nilippatam ākum. mivikutikkup paṇpupporuḷaṉṟi vēṟuporuḷ iṉmi yiṉ, cemmi karumi mutaliyaṉa colniliyāl pakupatam pōṉṟu iruppiṉum poruḷniliyāl pakāppatamēyām.		(naṉ. 135 caṅkara.)
paṇpuppakuti puṇarumāṟu -
cemmi ciṟumi mutaliya paṇpuccoṟkaḷ varumoḻiyāka nikaḻum vikutiyō aṉṟip patamō puṇarumvaḻi, iṟuti vikutiyākiya mi pōtalum, paṇpuccolliṉ iṭiyē niṉṟa ukaram ikaramātalum, ataṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa kuṟil neṭilātalum, mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa akaram aikāramātalum, iṭiyē niṉṟa valloṟṟu iraṭṭutalum, muṉniṉṟa mey tiritalum, varumoḻi valleḻuttiṟku iṉamāṉa mel loṟṟu mikutalum, piṟavum uriyaṉavām. īṟupōtal ellā vikārattukkum koḷka.
	e-ṭu	:	naṉmi + aṉ ḻū nal + aṉ = nallaṉ - īṟu pōtal
			karumi + aṉ ḻū kari + aṉ = kariyaṉ - 							īṟupōtalum iṭi ukaram ikaram ātalum
			pacumi + aṭi ḻū pācu + aṭi = pācaṭi - īṟu 					pōtalum, āti nīṭalum
			ciṟumi + uyir ḻū ciṟṟ + uyir = ciṟṟuyir - īṟu 					pōtalum, taṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭalum
			pacumi + tār ḻū pacun + tār = pacuntār - īṟu 						pōtalum, iṉa oṟṟu mikutalum
		 	pacumi + tār ḻū pin + tār = pintār - īṟupōta 					lum, īṟṟayal uyirmey keṭutalum, mutal 						akaram ai ātalum, iṉa oṟṟu mikutalum
			cemmi + ā ḻū cem + ā ḻū  cet + ā ḻū cēt + 					ā = cētā - īṟu pōtalum, īṟṟayal makaram 						takaramākat tiritalum, mutal uyir nīṭalum
			cemmi + aṉ = cemmiyaṉ - yakara uṭampaṭu 					mey peṟṟu iyalpāka muṭintatu. 	(naṉ. 136)
karuṅkutiri mutalāyiṉa (paṇputtoki ātaliṉ) pakupatam allavēṉum, paṇpu atikārappaṭṭamiyāl patappuṇarc- cikkum īṇṭē coṉṉār eṉka. (paṇputtokiyel lām paṇpuppeyarp puṇarcci peṟumāṟu koḷka).			(naṉ. 135 mayili.)
pattiṉmuṉ iraṇṭu puṇarumāṟu -
‘pattu’ nilimoḻi; ‘iraṇṭu’ varumoḻi. nilimoḻi yīṟṟu uyirmeyyām takara ukaram keṭa, īṟṟayal takara oṟṟu ṉakara oṟṟāka, ‘taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟu uyirvariṉ iraṭṭum’ eṉṟa vitippaṭi,
pattu + iraṇṭu ḻū pat + iraṇṭu ḻū paṉ + iraṇṭu = paṉṉi raṇṭu eṉṟākum.	(naṉ. 198)
pattiṉ muṉ eṇ niṟi aḷavup peyarkaḷum piṟa peyarkaḷum puṇarumāṟu -
pattu eṉpataṉmuṉ oṉṟu mutal pattu īṟākiya eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, āyiram kōṭi eṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, niṟippeyar aḷavuppeyarkaḷum, piṟa peyarkaḷum vantu puṇarumiṭattu, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu uyirmey keṭa, iṉṉum iṟṟum ākiya cāriyikaḷuḷ ēṟṟatoṉṟu iṭiyē varum.
	varumāṟu :	patiṉoṉṟu, patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉāyiram, patiṉ kaḻañcu, patiṉkalam, patiṉmaṭaṅku - iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa.
		patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟiraṇṭu, patiṟṟumūṉṟu, patiṟṟukkōṭi, patiṟṟuttūṇi - iṟṟuc cāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa.
ivviti oṉpatu eṉṉum nilimoḻikkum poruntum.
	varumāṟu :	oṉpatiṉāyiram, oṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu, oṉpatiṉ kalam, oṉpatiṉ maṭaṅku; oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu, oṉpatiṟṟiraṇṭu, oṉpatiṟṟuk kuṟuṇi
pattukkōṭi, oṉpatu kōṭi eṉac cāriyi peṟātu iyal pāka muṭitalum koḷka.	(naṉ. 197)
‘pattu’ āyirattoṭu puṇartal
pattu eṉpataṉōṭu āyiram puṇarumvaḻiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum keṭṭu iṉcāriyi peṟṟup patiṉāyiram eṉa 
muṭiyum.	(to. e. 435 nac)
‘pattu’ oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷoṭu puṇartal -
pattu eṉpataṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum keṭa, iṉcāriyi iṭiyē vara, pattu + iṉ + oṉṟu = patiṉoṉṟu eṉa varum.
patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉintu, patiṉāṟu, patiṉēḻ, patiṉeṭṭu eṉpaṉavum aṉṉa.
‘patiṉ nāṉku’ - iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram keṭa, varumoḻi nakaram ṉakaramāyt tiriya, patiṉāṉku eṉa muṭiyum.
pattu + iraṇṭu : ‘pattiṉ muṉ iraṇṭu puṇarumāṟu’ kāṇka.
pattu + iraṇṭu eṉpuḻi, nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum keṭa, takara oṟṟu ṉakaramāki iraṭṭippa, paṉ + ṉ + iraṇṭu = paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiṟṟu.	(to. e. 433, 434nac.)
‘pattu’ niṟippeyar aḷavuppeyar ivaṟṟoṭu puṇartal -
pattu nilimoḻiyāka, varumoḻikkaṇ niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkāl, iṭiyē iṉcāriyi varum.
	e-ṭu	:	patiṉkaḻañcu, patiṉmā, patiṉkalam, patiṉcāṭi
(to. e. 436 nac.)
varumoḻi uyirāyiṉ iṉcāriyi ‘iṟṟu’ ākum.
	e-ṭu	:	pattu + iṉ + akal = patiṟṟakal; pattu + iṉ + uḻakku = patiṟṟuḻakku	(121 nac. uri)
pattu ‘paḵtu’ ātal -
aṭippaṭiccol pattu eṉpatē. pattu eṉpataṉ iṭiyē takaroṟṟuk keṭa avviṭattu āytam varap paḵtu eṉṟākum eṉpar tol.
paḵtu eṉpataṉ āytoli īṟṟeḻuttākiya takaroliyi oṭṭit tirintu olippatākum. atuvē nāḷaṭivil takaram pōla olikkappaṭat tol. kālattukku muṉṉarēyē pattu eṉpatu iyaṟcol pōla vaḻakkattil mikuntuviṭṭatu eṉpatum, paḵtu ataṉ tiripākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉpatum pōtarum.	(e. ā. pak. 172)
‘pattu’ piṟapeyaroṭu puṇarumāṟu -
niṟiyum aḷavum allāta piṟapeyar vantuḻi, iṉcāriyi ‘iṟṟu’ eṉat tiriya, patiṟṟuvēli, patiṟṟiyāṇṭu, patiṟṟaṭukku, patiṟṟumuḻam, patiṟṟoṉṟu,patiṟṟiraṇṭu, patiṟṟunāṉku, patiṟṟintu, patiṟṟāṟu, patiṟṟēḻ, patiṟṟeṭṭu, patiṟ ṟoṉpatu, patiṟṟuppattu eṉap paṇput tokikkaṇ muṭintavāṟu.
patiṉmuḻam mutalāka iṉcāriyi tiriyāmiyum koḷka.
(to. e. 436 nac. uri)
‘pattu’ varumoḻiyāka, oṉṟu mutal eṭṭaṉoṭu puṇartal -
	varumāṟu :	oṉṟu + pattu ḻū oṉṟu + paḵtu = orupaḵtu, orupatu 	(to. e. 438, 439)
		iraṇṭu + pattu ḻū iraṇṭu + paḵtu = irupaḵtu, irupatu 				(to. e. 438, 439)
		mūṉṟu + pattu ḻū mūṉṟu + paḵtu = muppaḵtu, muppatu	(to. 			e. 440, 441)
		nāṉku + pattu ḻū nāṉku + paḵtu = nāṟpaḵtu, nāṟpatu	(to				. e. 442)
		aintu + pattu ḻū aintu + paḵtu = aimpaḵtu, aimpatu		 			(to. e. 443)
		āṟu + pattu ḻū āṟu + paḵtu = aṟupaḵtu, aṟupatu 					(to. e. 440 nac.)
		ēḻ + pattu ḻū ēḻ + paḵtu = eḻupaḵtu, 							eḻupatu	(to. e. 389 nac.)
		eṭṭu + pattu ḻū eṭṭu + paḵtu = eṇpaḵtu, eṇpatu	(to. e. 444 nac.)
pattoṉpatu -
‘āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār pattoṉpatu eṉpataṟkup puṇarcci viti kūṟāmi’ kāṇka. (nilimoḻik kuṟṟukarīṟu varumoḻi mutal okaravuyir ēṟimuṭiyum eṉpatu.)
patam eṉappaṭuvatu -
eḻuttu ōreḻuttākat taṉittō iraṇṭu mutalākat toṭarntō poruḷ taruvatu patam eṉappaṭum. (patam, moḻi, col ivi oru poruṭ kiḷavikaḷ).	(naṉ. 128)
iṟiñi - miṟiñi eṉil eḻuttut toṭarumēṉum taṉṉi yuṇarttu maṉṟip piṟaporuḷ tārāmiyiṉ patam ākātu eṉpār ‘poruḷ tariṉ’ eṉṟār. ‘taṉṉi uṇarttiṉ eḻuttām; piṟaporuḷic, cuṭṭutaṟkaṇṇēyām col’ eṉpa ātaliṉ, poruḷ eṉṟatu piṟa poruḷ eṉpār ‘uṇart tiṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tariṉ’ eṉṟār. aṇu veṉṉum oli nuṭpat tāl eḻuttāṉāṟpōla, eḻutteṉṉum oli nuṭpattāl moḻiyām eṉpār ‘patam’ eṉṟār. ip pataviyalukku mēṟkōḷ āriyamē eṉpār moḻi eṉṉātu ‘patam’ eṉṟār.	(naṉ. 128 caṅkara.)


pataviyal, puṇariyal  toṭarpu -
pataviyaluḷ pataṅkaḷ āmāṟu uṇartti, puṇariyaluḷ pataṅkaḷ tammuḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ivi tammuḷ iyipuṭiyaṉa.	(naṉ. 150 mayili.)
paracu, iṅku, ētu muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa vātal -
maḻu eṉṟa paṭikkalattik kuṟikkum paracu eṉpatum, peruṅ kāyam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum iṅku eṉpatum, kāraṇam eṉṟa poruḷil varum ētu eṉpatum vaṭacoṟkaḷ ātaliṉ, āriyac coṟkaḷukkuk kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu iṉmiyiṉ, āriyaccoṟkaḷ tirinta vaṭacoṟkaḷākiya ivi kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu ākātu muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟa āyiṉa.			(to. e. 36, 152 nac. uri)
paracu - vaḻipaṭu; iṅku - taṅku - eṉṟu (tamiḻil) ēvalorumi viṉiyāṉālum, muṉṉilikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu vārātu eṉṟa karuttāṉ ivi muṟṟiyalukara īṟukaḷēyām.
āriyaccollukkuk kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu illāmal irukka lām. āṉāl avi tamiḻiṟkēṟpa vaṭacollākat tirikkap paṭṭa niliyil, ēṉiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ pōla, iṅku ētu tātu eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa eṉṟu kōṭalē poruntum.	(e. ku. pak. 46)
paripāṭi -
tamiḻil uttivakikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil paripāṭi eṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
‘iyaṟkip poruḷi iṟṟeṉak kiḷattal’ eṉṟāṟpōlac cevvaṉam collutal uttiyām. cevvaṉam collātu vēṟoruvāṟṟāṉ poruntum vaki urippatu utti vakiyām. utti ‘yukti’ (poruntum vitam) eṉṟa āriyac colliṉ tiripākum.	(to. po. 665 pērā.)
civañāṉamuṉivar utti eṉpatu paripāṭi ākum eṉpar.			(civañā. pā. vi. pak. 9)
paruntiṉ viḻukkāṭu -
paruntu naṭuvē viḻuntu tāṉ karutiya poruḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu pōvatu pōla muṭikkappaṭum poruḷi muṭittuppōm iyalpiṉatākiya cūttiranili paruntiṉ viḻukkāṭu eṉappaṭum. (‘paruntiṉ vīḻvu’ eṉpatum atu.)			(naṉ. 18 mayili.)
	e-ṭu	:	‘ṇaṉamuṉṉum vaḵkāṉ miciyum mak kuṟukum’
(naṉ. 96)
eṉṉum iccūttiram naṉ. 341, 114, 227, 120 eṉa mēlvarum cūttiraṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum vēṇṭi niṉṟavāṟu.
‘pallavi nutaliya akara iṟupeyar’ -
palla, pala, cila, uḷḷa, illa eṉṟa aintum palaviṉpālik kāṭṭum akara īṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷām.			(to. co. 168 cēṉā.)
‘palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi’ (to. e. 210 nac.) kāṇka.
palvakip peyariṉ eccam muṉ vali iyalpātal -
	e-ṭu	:	uṇṭa, uṇṇāniṉṟa + kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉat toḻilkoḷ peyareccam muṉ iyalpāyiṉa.
poṉṉaṉṉa, poṉpōṉṟa, poṉṉaṉiya, poṉṉi- kartta, poṉṉotta + kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉa uvamippaṇpu koḷ peyareccam muṉ iyalpāyiṉa.
periya, ciṟiya, ceyya, iṉiya, taṇṇiya + kamalam - carōrukam - tāmari - paṅkayam eṉap palavakip paṇpukoḷ peya reccam muṉ iyalpāyiṉa. 
uṇṇāta, tiṉṉāta + kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉap peyarecca etirmaṟi muṉ iyalpāyiṉa.	(naṉ. 166 mayili.)
pala eṉpatu ēṉiya peyarkaḷoṭu puṇartal -
pala eṉpatu taṉmuṉ piṟapeyar variṉ, lakaram ṟakara oṟṟākātu, akaram keṭṭup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	palkaṭal, cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi, yāṉi, vēḷvi eṉa varum.
uyir varuvaḻit taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭip pallaṇi, pallāṇṭu, pallili eṉṟāṟpōla varum.
‘palvēṟu kavarporuḷ’	(to. po. 114 nac.)
‘eṇṇarum paṉṉaki’	(114 nac.)
‘pallāṟṟāṉum’	(168 nac.)
‘palkuṟippiṉavē’	(286 pērā.)
‘paṉmuṟiyāṉum’	(to. co. 396 nac.)
	(to. e. 214 nac.)

pala, cila  tammuḷ puṇarumāṟu -
pala cila eṉpaṉa tammoṭu tām puṇariṉ iyalpum, valliṉam mikutalum, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram keṭutalum, anta lakaramey ṟakaramey ātalum, ēṉiya peyar varu moḻiyāka varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram vikaṟpa mātalum, (lakaramey ṟakaramākac ciṟupāṉmi tiritalum) uḷa.
	varumāṟu :	palapala, palappala, palpala, paṟpala
		cilacila, cilaccila, cilcila, ciṟcila
		palaporuḷ, palporuḷ; cilaporuḷ, cil poruḷ; paṟpakal, ciṟkali	(naṉ. 170)
pala : collilakkaṇam -
pala eṉṉum col peyareccak kuṟippāyum (pala kutirikaḷ ōṭiṉa), kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟāyum (kutirikaḷ pala), kuṟippu muṟṟu īreccam ākum peyareccak kuṟippāyum viṉi yeccak kuṟippāyum sa(pala kutirikaḷ ōṭiṉa (palavākiya), kutirikaḷ, pala ōṭiṉa (palavāy)], kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum aḵṟiṇip paṉmip peyarāyum sapala ōṭiṉa (palavākiya kutirikaḷ)], poruḷāti āṟaṉuḷ paṇpu kāraṇamāka varum aḵṟiṇip paṉmip peyarāyum (kutirikaḷ oṉṟalla, pala)  eṉa aṟuvakippaṭṭup poruḷnōkkam mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṉṉateṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.	(naṉ. 167 caṅkara.)
pala : paṇpu aṉṟu, peyar eṉpatu -
pala, cila - eṉa akara īṟṟaṉavāka vittup puṇartta kāraṇam yāteṉil, appaṇpukaḷ paṉmi ciṉmi - eṉap pakutip poruḷ vikuti (mi) yōṭēṉum, palar - pala, cilar - cila - eṉa vikutip poruḷ vikutiyōṭēṉum (ar, a) aṉṟit taṉittunillā oṟṟumi nayam paṟṟi eṉpatu.	(naṉ. 170 caṅkara.)
palamoḻikaḷilum cilavitikaḷ orutaṉmiyavāy iruttal -
kuṟili aṭutta oṟṟu varumoḻi mutalil uyir variṉ iraṭṭum.
	e-ṭu	:	col + aritu = collaritu
‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭal’	(to. e. 160 nac.)
‘motaloḷ hras vikasvara
 motave param svaramatāke naṇalayaḷaṅkaḷ
 kutayisukum tvitvam’	(karuṇāṭaka ™ptamaṇi 79)
eṉpatu kaṉṉaṭa ilakkaṇam.
‘ṅamō hrasvātaci ṅamuṉ nityam’	(pāṇiṉī. 8 : 3 : 32)
eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam.
vaṭamoḻiyil cila eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉ kuṟili aṭuttu niṟkum meyyum iraṭṭukiṟatu.
	e-ṭu	:	pratyaṅṅātmā, sukaṇṇī™ :  - eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
(e. ā. muṉṉuri pak. iii)
palavaki eḻuttukkaḷ -
‘uruvē uṇarvē oliyē taṉmi
 eṉīr eḻuttum īriru pakutiya’
1.	kāṇap paṭṭa uruvam ellām
	māṇak kāṭṭum vakimi nāṭi
	vaḻuvil ōviyaṉ kiviṉi pōla
	eḻutap paṭuvatu uruveḻut tākum’
2.	 koṇṭōr kuṟiyāl koṇṭa ataṉi
	 uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarvatu uṇarveḻuttu ākum’
3.	‘icippaṭu puḷḷiṉ eḻāal pōla
	 cevippulaṉ āvatu oliyeḻuttu ākum’
4.	‘mutaṟkā raṇamum tuṇikkā raṇamum
	 tuṇikkā raṇattoṭu toṭariya uṇarvum
	 avaṟṟoṭu puṇarnta akatteḻu vaḷiyiṉ
	 miṭaṟṟuppiṟantu icippatu taṉmi yeḻuttē’
eṉṉum innāṉku pakutiyuḷ oliyeḻuttē cevippula ṉāyp poruḷtarum ciṟappunōkki ataṉi eṭuttōti ṉār.	(naṉ. 256 mayili.)
‘palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi’ (1) -
‘palāañ cilām’ : am eṉpatu ummiṉ tiripē. palavum cilavum eṉpatu poruḷ. am, īṇṭu eṇṇupporuḷil vantatākum.
kaṉṉaṭamoḻiyil eṇṇupporuḷil am, um eṉṟa īriṭic coṟkaḷum varukiṉṟaṉa.
pala + am = palāam eṉṟākum. varumoḻi amcāriyi eṉpataṉik kuṟikkavē akaram iṭiyē vantuḷḷatu. ‘palāam’ eṉpataṉkaṇ akaram aṟikuṟi aḷaviṉatē.	(e. ā. pak. 135, 136)
‘palavaṟṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi’ (2) -
ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṉiyum ōciyiṉiyum karutivarum toṭar moḻikkaṇ, palavaṟṟi uṇarttivarum iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu varum coṟkaḷum uḷa.
palla - pala - cilla - cila - eṉpavaṟṟuḷ palla - cilla - eṉṉum toṭarmoḻikaḷ viṉiyoṭu toṭaruṅkāl niṉṟavāṟē toṭarumāyiṉ ōcinayam iṉmi karutic cāṉṟōr tam ceyyuḷuḷ ‘pallā kūṟiṉum pataṭikaḷ uṇarār, cillā koḷkeṉac ceppunar uḷarē’ eṉṟāṟ pōla vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭu meṉat terikiṟatu.
uriyāciriyaṉmār kāṭṭum palāam- cilāam- eṉpaṉa palavum cilavum eṉpavaṟṟiṉ vikāram ām. avvāṟāyiṉ accoṟkaḷ makara īṟṟiṉavēyaṉṟi akara īṟāmāṟilli.			(to.e.213 ca.pāla.)
‘palavaṟṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi’ -
paṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum akara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ aintaṉiyum, ‘aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamakkiḷavi’ mutaliyavaṟ ṟoṭu cērttuk koṭuttal.
aḵtāvatu paṉmip poruḷ uṇarttum palla - pala - cila - uḷḷa - illa - eṉṟa aintu peyarkaḷiyum, akara īṟṟuḷ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum aṉṉa - aṇmi viḷi - ceymmaṉa - ēvalviṉi - ceyta eṉṉum peyareccam - ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeccam - amma eṉṉum uri yaci - eṉpaṉavaṟṟoṭu cērttu eṇṇutal. (to. e. 210 nac.)
palavaṟṟi uṇarttum akarīṟṟup peyarkaḷ ellāvaṟṟi yum koḷka. (e-ṭu : kariyaṉa, ceyyaṉa, nallaṉa, tīyaṉa)			(e. ku. pak. 209)
palavaṟṟiṟuti puṇarumāṟu -
palavaṟṟik kuṟikkum palla - pala - cila -uḷḷa - illa - eṉṉum coṟkaḷ urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟuccāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	pallavaṟṟi, palavaṟṟi, cilavaṟṟi, uḷḷavaṟṟi, illavaṟṟi.... pallavaṟṟukkaṇ,
			pallavaṟṟuk kōṭu, palavaṟṟukkōṭu, cilavaṟṟuk kōṭu, uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu, illavaṟṟuk kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū	(to. e. 174, 220 nac.)
alvaḻikkaṇ, palakutiri - eṉṟāṟpōla iyalpākap puṇarum.	(to. e. 210 nac.)
‘palāañ cilāam’ -
‘palavaṟṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi’ kāṇka.
palacila eṉpaṉa ummit tokiyākap puṇarntavaḻic ceyyuṭkaṇ ‘palāañ cilāam’ eṉat tirintu puṇartalu muṇṭu. it toṭarukkup palavum cilavum eṉpatu poruḷ. ippuṇarcci ceyyuṭkē uriyatu. itaṉ colnili palacila eṉṉum cevveṇ.	(to. e. 213 nac. uri)
pavvī, nī, mī muṉ valliṉam vantu puṇarumāṟu -
‘pī’ eṉṉum iṭakkarppeyar, nī eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyar, mēlāya paṇpiyum mēliṭattiyum uṇarttum mī eṉṉum col - ivaṟṟiṉmuṉ varumoḻi mutal vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpupuṇarcciyām. mī iyal pākap puṇartalēyaṉṟi valiyum meliyum mikkup puṇartalu muṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	:	pīkuṟitu, nī kuṟiyi, mīkaṇ; mīkkūṟṟu, mīntōl
			mīkaṇ eṉpatu kaṇmī eṉpataṉ ilakkaṇappōli yāy alvaḻi āyiṟṟu. mīkkūṟṟu - mēlāya col lāṟ piṟanta pukaḻ eṉap paṇputtoki aṉmoḻi. mīntōl - mēlāya tōl eṉap paṇputtoki.	(naṉ. 178 caṅ.)
pa, va nikaḻkālam kāṭṭutal -
uṇṇākiṭantāṉ - uṇṇāviruntāṉ eṉa, ākiṭantu - āviruntu eṉpaṉavum, varuti - peyarti - varuntuti - poruti - pulamputi - eṉat takaroṟṟum, uṇpal - varuval - eṉavum nōkkuvēṟku - uṇpēṟku - eṉavum muṟiyē viṉiyum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum ākiya ivaṟṟil pakara vakara oṟṟukkaḷum, ciṟupāṉmi nikaḻkālam kāṭṭum.			(naṉ. 143 irāmā.)
paḷ, kaḷ  puṇarumāṟu -
paḷ, kaḷ eṉpaṉa ‘puḷ’ eṉṟa poruṭpeyar pōla iru vaḻiyum ukarac cāriyiyum peṟṟup puṇarum. iruvaḻiyum ḷakaram ṭakaramāyt tirintu muṭitalum ām.
	varumāṟu :	paḷḷukkaṭitu, paḷḷukkaṭumi; paṭkaṭitu, paṭ kaṭumi; kaḷḷukkaṭitu, kaḷḷukkaṭumi; kaṭ kaṭitu, kaṭkaṭumi,	(404 iḷa. uri.)
paṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
paṉ eṉpatu pañciṉip piritteṭukkum toḻiliyum pañciṉiyum kuṟikkum.
aḵtu alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭik kaṇattu vakaramum vantuḻi ukaramum, yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpum, uyir varuvaḻi oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	paṉṉuk kaṭitu, paṉṉu neṭitu, paṉṉu valitu, paṉ yātu, paṉ ṉaritu; paṉṉuk kaṭumi, paṉṉu neṭumi, paṉṉuvalimi, paṉyāppu, paṉṉarumi.				(to. e. 345 nac.)
paṉṉiraṇṭu : puṇarnili -
pattu + iraṇṭu : pattu eṉpataṉ īṟṟuyirmey keṭa niṉṟa ‘pat’ eṉpataṉ takara oṟṟuk keṭa, pa eṉṟa taṉikkuṟili aṭuttu ṉakaroṟṟu iraṭṭittup ‘paṉṉ’ eṉṟu niṟpa, iraṇṭu eṉṟa varumoḻi puṇarap paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa muṭintatu eṉpar tol.
pattu eṉpataṟkup pāṉ eṉpatu pariyāyap peyar. pāṉ + iraṇṭu : nilimoḻi mutaleḻuttuk kuṟukip ‘paṉ’ eṉṟāki niṟka, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟiraṭṭip paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉap puṇarntatu eṉalām.
iṅṅaṉamē paṉṉoṉṟu, paṉmūṉṟu, paṉṉāṉku, paṉṉāṟu eṉak kaṉṉaṭamoḻikkaṇ vaḻaṅkutal kāṇattakkatu.		(e. ā. pak. 171)
paṉāaṭṭu : collamippu -
paṉi + aṭṭu : īṟṟu aikāram taṉakku muṉ uyirmutal moḻi vara, oru col taṉmippaṭap puṇarumiṭattuc colliṉ iṭiyatu ātaliṉ, aḵtu orumāttiri aḷavu olikkum. ‘paṉiaṭṭu’ paṉaaṭṭu eṉṟolikkum. paṉa eṉpataṉ īṟṟu akaramum aṭṭu eṉpataṉ mutal akaramum cērntu ōr ākāramākip paṉāṭṭu eṉa olikkum. varu moḻi ‘aṭṭu’ eṉpataṉi aṟivikka, ākārattiṉ muṉṉar akaram aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭum; eḻutappaṭutalaṉṟi akaram olikkappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉam paṉāaṭṭu eṉṟa col amiyum.
naṉṉūlār akaratti viṭuttup paṉāṭṭu eṉṟē kūṟiṉār. (paṉāṭṭu - paṉiyiṉatu tīṅkaṭṭi)	(e. ā. pak. 146)
paṉi eṉṟa kālappeyarp puṇarcci -
paṉi eṉṟa kālappeyar nilimoḻiyāy varumoḻi vaṉ kaṇattoṭu puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyi yum peṟum.
	e-ṭu	:	paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ - paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ, 					ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ.
			paṉikkālattil koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla ēḻaṉ 					poruḷ virikkappaṭum.	(to. e. 241 nac.)
paṉuval -
pala ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakki iyaṟṟappaṭum ilak kaṇanūl potuvākap paṉuval eṉṟē kuṟippiṭappaṭum.			(to. e. pāyiram)
paṉi eṉpataṉ puṇarcci -
paṉi eṉpatu ammup peṟṟu ai keṭṭup paṉaṅkāy, paṉañcetiḷ, paṉantōl, paṉampū eṉa varum. paṉi īṇṭup pulliṉam.
paṉi + aṭṭu = paṉāaṭṭu eṉa ai keṭṭu ākāram puṇarntu muṭiyum.
itu pōlavē, ōri + nayam = ōrānayam, vicci + vāti = viccāvāti, kēṭṭi + mūlam = kēṭṭā mūlam, pāṟi + kal = pāṟāṅkal, pāṟaṅkal eṉa muṭiyum.
paṉi + koṭi = paṉikkoṭi	(to. e. 283 - 285 nac.)
paṉiyiṉ kuṟi eṉṟāṟpōla urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum varum. (paṉiyiṉatu kuṟi mutalāka āṟaṉ poruḷpaṭa urikka).	(285 nac. uri)
paṉi eṉṟa (marap) peyar nilimoḻiyāka niṟpa, koṭi varumoḻi yāyiṉ iṭiyē kakaramey mikum. piṟa vaṉkaṇam variṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu aikāram keṭa iṭiyē ammuc cāriyi mikum. ‘tiraḷ’ varumoḻiyāyiṉ vali mikutalum ‘ai’ pōy ammuc cāriyi peṟutalumām. ‘aṭṭu’ varumoḻi yāyiṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ai keṭa varumoḻi mutal akaram nīḷum. iviyellām vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.
	varumāṟu :	paṉi + koṭi = paṉikkoṭi (paṉi uruvatti eḻutiya koṭi); paṉi + kāy, ceṟumpu, tūṇ, paḻam ḻū paṉ + am + kāy, ceṟumpu..... = paṉaṅkāy, paṉañceṟumpu, paṉantūṇ, paṉampaḻam; paṉi + tiraḷ = paṉittiraḷ, paṉantiraḷ; paṉi + aṭṭu ḻū paṉ + āṭṭu = paṉāṭṭu.
(paṉantūṇ - paṉiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatūṇ; paṉāṭṭu - paṉiyiṉatu tīṅkaṭṭi)	(naṉ. 203)
paṉi eṉṉum aḷavup peyarp puṇarcci -
paṉi eṉṉum aḷavup peyar kuṟi eṉṉum varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarumvaḻi iṉcāriyi peṟum; ciṟupāṉmi iṉcāriyi peṟātu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.
	varumāṟu :	paṉiyiṉ kuṟi, paṉikkuṟi
itaṟkup paṉiyum atiṟ kuṟintatum eṉa ummit tokippaṭap poruḷ kūṟuka.	(to. e. 169 nac.)
pākatam -
nāṭukaḷellām vaḻaṅkum moḻi pākatamām. (mu. vī. moḻi. 30)
pākatavaki -
taṟpavam, tēcikam, taṟcamam eṉa pākatam mūvakippaṭum.
(mu. vī. moḻi. 31)
pāricēṭam -
pāricēṭam - eñciyatu. itu piramāṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. pakupatattuḷ pakuti vikuti mutaliya uṟuppellām pakāppatam āyiṉum, vikuti mutaliyavaṟṟi mēl vitantu kūṟaliṉ, īṇṭut ‘tattam pakāppataṅkaḷ’ eṉṟatu mutaṉilikaḷiyē eṉpatu pāricēṭattāl peṟṟām. (piramāṇam - aḷavi)		(naṉ. 134 caṅkara.)
pālvari kiḷavi -
oruporuḷiṉ pakutiyi uṇarttum coṟkaḷākiya ari, kāl, mukkāl, arikkāl mutaliyaṉa.
cempāl - camapāti	(to. po. 463 pērā.)
pāl - oṉṟu pirintu palavākiya kūṟṟiṉmēṟṟātal.	
(to. po. 13 nac. uri)
pālum nīrum pōla -
meyyum uyirum uyirmeykkaṇ muṉṉum piṉṉum peṟa niṟkum eṉṟamiyāl, akkūṭṭam pālum nīrum pōla uṭaṉkalantatākum; viralnuṉikaḷ talippeytāṟ pōla vēṟu niṉṟu kalantaṉa alla eṉpatu.	(to. e. 18 iḷa. uri)
pāḻ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
pāḻ eṉṟa col vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ avvalleḻuttiṉōṭu otta mel leḻuttum mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu	:	pāḻ + kiṇaṟu = pāḻkkiṇaṟu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu
itu pāḻuṭ kiṇaṟu eṉa ēḻāvatu viriyum. viṉittoki yāyiṉ, pāḻkiṇaṟu eṉa iyalpākavē puṇarum.			(to. e. 387 nac. uri)
piṭṭaṉ + koṟṟaṉ : puṇarumāṟu -
piṭṭaṉukku makaṉ koṟṟaṉ eṉṟa karuttil, nilimoḻi ‘aṉ’ keṭṭu ‘am’ puṇarntu, atu varumoḻik kēṟpat tirintatu eṉṟu kūṟuvativiṭa, nilimoḻi īṟṟu ṉakaram varumoḻikkēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tiriyum eṉṟal ēṟṟatu.
piṭṭaṉ + koṟṟaṉ = piṭṭaṅ koṟṟaṉ; antuvaṉ + cāttaṉ = antuvañ cāttaṉ; viṇṇaṉ + tāyaṉ = viṇṇantāyaṉ; koṟṟaṉ + piṭṭaṉ = koṟṟam piṭṭaṉ  	(e. ā. pak. 158)
piṭā eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
piṭā eṉṟa marappeyar varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi, akara eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭiyōṭu, iṉamelleḻuttu mikuta lum, avvalleḻuttē mikutalum, urupiṟkuc cellum iṉcāriyi peṟutalum ām.
	e-ṭu	:	piṭā + kōṭu = piṭāaṅkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, piṭāviṉkōṭu; piṭāattuk kōṭu eṉa attup peṟutalumuṇṭu.	(to. e. 229, 230 nac. uri)
pirakiruti -
itu tamiḻil mutaṉili eṉavum, pakuti eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. itu pakupatattiṉatu mutal pirivāy niṟṟaliṉ mutaṉili (mutal nili) eṉappaṭṭatu. pakāppatattiṉ col muḻutum pakutiyām. ippakuti, iṭinili vikuti mutaliya ēṉi uṟuppukkaḷoṭu cērumiṭattu vikārappaṭutalumuṇṭu. iru, iṭu mutaliya vikutikaḷ taṉṉoṭu cērumpōtu avaṟṟit taṉṉuḷ aṭakkiya pakutiyātalumuṇṭu. pakuti illāta collē illi eṉalām.
	e-ṭu :	uṇṭāṉ	-	uṇ : pakuti
		yāṉi	-	ippakāppatam muḻutum pakuti.
		vantāṉ	-	vā eṉṟa pakuti kuṟuki va eṉa 					niṉṟatu.
		eḻuntiruntāṉ	-	eḻuntiru : pakuti
		naṭa (ēvalorumi) - naṭa : pakuti; ‘āy’ vikuti 					     kuṉṟi vantatu.    (cū. vi. pak. 41)
pirattiyayam -
vikuti iṭinili vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ ākiya iṭic coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil pirattiyayam eṉappaṭum. vaṭamoḻi yil vikutiyē kālam kāṭṭaliṉ, iṭinili viṉimutaṟ poruṇmi mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi varum.	(cū. vi. pak. 55)
pirātipatikam -
poruḷuṭiyatāy, viṉimutaṉili allātatāy, iṭic col allātatāy varum, peyar aṭippaṭic col vaṭamoḻiyil pirātipatikam eṉappaṭum. tamiḻil pirāti patikamē mutalvēṟṟumi ākum. vaṭamoḻiyil pirāti patikam vēṟu, mutal vēṟṟumi ēṟṟa colvaṭivam vēṟu.
urupum viḷiyum ēlātu piṟitoṉṟaṉoṭu toṭarātu paṭṭāṅku niṟkum peyarkaḷ pirātipatikam ām.
	e-ṭu	:	ā, avaṉ	(pi. vi. 7)
pirittup puṇarkkappaṭāta toṭarkaḷ -
‘puṇariyal niliyiṭi yuṇarat tōṉṟātaṉa’ - kāṇka.
‘piṟappiyal’ nuvalvatu -
untiyiliruntu piṟakkum kāṟṟu, tali - miṭaṟu - neñcu-ivaṟṟil nilipeṟṟup pal - itaḻ - nā - mūkku - mēlvāy - eṉṟa aintaṉ muyaṟcip piṟappāṉ vevvēṟu eḻuttoli yāyt tōṉṟum.
paṉṉīruyirum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ icikkum. a ā - aṅkāppāṉum, i ī e ē ai - aṅkāppōṭu aṇpal mutali nāviḷimpu uṟutalāṉum, u ū o ō oḷa - itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟutalāṉum piṟakkum. (aṅkāttali ellāvaṟ ṟukkum koḷka.)
k ṅ, cñ, ṭṇ - muṟiyē mutalnā aṇṇam, iṭinā aṇṇam, nuṉinā aṇṇam uṟap piṟakkum. t n - aṇpal aṭiyi nuṉināp parantu oṟṟalāṉum, ṟ ṉ - nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟalāṉum, r ḻ - nuṉinā aṇṇam varuṭalā ṉum, l - nāviḷimpu vīṅki aṇṇatti oṟṟalāṉum, ḷ - nāviḷimpu aṇṇatti varuṭalāṉum, p m - iraṇṭu itaḻkaḷum iyitalāṉum, v - mēṟpallik kīḻitaḻ iyi talāṉum, y - aṭinā aṇṇam kaṇṇuṟṟu aṭitalāṉum, melleḻuttu āṟum tam piṟappiṭam muyaṟci ivaṟṟoṭu mūkkoli poruntalāṉum piṟakkum. ivaṟṟiṉ oli kaḷiṭiyē ciṟu vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa.
cārpeḻuttukkaḷ tattam aṭippaṭi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamum muyaṟciyum tamakkum urimiyavākap piṟakkum. āytam taṉakkup poruntiya neñcuvaḷiyāl piṟakkum eṉpar nac.
untiyil tōṉṟum eḻuttu miṭaṟṟi aṭiyum vari nikaḻum tiritaru kūṟukaḷ ākiya pari, picanti, matyamā eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku ēlā. veḷippaṭum oliyāṉa vikari eṉpatē ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷāṉ uṇart tappaṭum. iṉṉa ceytikaḷ tolkāppiya eḻuttuppaṭala mūṉṟām iyalākiya piṟappiyalil 20 nūṟpākkaḷāṉ viḷakkap paṭṭuḷa.	(to. e. 83 - 102 nac.)
piṟappiṉ puṟaṉaṭi -
pala eḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappu oṉṟākac collappaṭiṉum, eṭuttal, paṭuttal, nalital eṉṟa eḻuttukkuriya olimuyaṟci- yāṉ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu piṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷ avvaṟṟuḷ ciṟitu ciṟitu uḷavām. ‘tantatu’ eṉpataṉkaṇuḷḷa takaraṅ kaḷiṉ olivēṟupāṭu pōlvaṉa kāṇka.(naṉ. 88)

piṟappukku mutaltuṇi iṭavaki -
eḻuttukkaḷ neñcum taliyum miṭaṟum eṉṉum mutal iṭavakiyiṉum, nāvum aṇṇamum itaḻum eyiṟum mūkkum eṉṉum tuṇi iṭa vakiyiṉum pulappaṭum.			(nēmi. eḻut. 6)
piṟappoliyil tiripu -
ovvoru tāṉattup piṟakkiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭṭik kūṟap paṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyumiṭattut tammuṭiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ ciṟiyaṉa ciṟiyaṉavāka uḷa. avi eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital vilaṅkal - eṉṟa vakiyāṉum, taliyici miṭaṟṟici neñcici mūkkici - eṉṟa vaki yāṉum piṟavakiyāṉum vēṟupaṭutal. ‘ai’ vilaṅkalōci uṭiyatu.	(i. vi. eḻut. 14 uri.)
piṟa mēl toṭartal -
vaṉmi ūr ukaram taṉ māttiriyiṉ kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkum kāraṇam, avvōreḻuttut toṭartal māttiriyiṉ amiyātu, piṟa eḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum mēl toṭaravum peṟutal.
‘pakāppatam ēḻum pakupatam oṉpatum’ (130) eṉṟu vari yaṟuttavaṟṟil iṅṅaṉam īṟṟayalum īṟumākak kūṟiya iraṇṭum oḻittu oḻinta ēḻum aintum ākiya eḻuttuk kaḷip ‘piṟamēl toṭaravum peṟumē’ eṉṟār. ‘toṭara vum’ eṉṟa ummi iṟantatu taḻīi niṉṟatu. ‘peṟumē’ eṉṟatu, taṉineṭil oḻinta aintu toṭarum; vaṉmi ūr ukaram kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkum mēl toṭar talum iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ.	(naṉ. 94 civañā.)
piṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
piṉ eṉṟa col piṉṉutal toḻiliyum, kūntali vārip piṉṉiya piṉṉaliyum uṇarttum. iḵtu alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum vantuḻi ukaramum, uyir vantuḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum peṟṟup puṇarum.

eṭu :	piṉṉukkaṭitu; piṉṉu naṉṟu, piṉṉu valitu, piṉ yātu, piṉ ṉaritu; piṉṉukkaṭumi; piṉṉunaṉmi, piṉṉuvalimi, piṉyāppu, piṉṉarumi.			(to. e. 345 nac.)
piṉ eṉpatu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itu peyarākavum viṉi yeccamākavum niṟkum. iṭiccollum viṉiyeccamum iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu	:	eṉpiṉ ceṉṟāṉ, uṇṭapiṉ ceṉṟāṉ
piṉ eṉṟa peyar vaṉkaṇam varumoḻiyāka vantuḻi, piṟ koṇṭāṉ - eṉa ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum, piṉkoṇ ṭāṉ - eṉa iyalpākavum puṇarum.	(to. e. 333 nac.)

piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpam kūṟal -
‘paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum vaḻuvala’ eṉṟavaṟṟi muṟiyē iṟantatu vilakkal, etiratu pōṟṟal - eṉṉum uttikaḷāl vilakkiyum pōṟṟiyum kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa (vaḻinūṟkup) piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpam kūṟalām.			(naṉ. 7 caṅkara.)
pī eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
pī eṉpatu malattik kuṟikkum iṭakkarp peyar. atu pī kuṟitu eṉṟāṟ pōla varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭum iyal pākap puṇarum.
ā eṉṟa nilimoḻimuṉ pī eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyp puṇarumiṭattu ikaramākak kuṟuki valimikku āppi eṉap puṇarum.	(to. e. 250, 233 nac.)
pīr eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
pīr eṉpatu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi melleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum. pīr attup peṟutalum, ammup peṟuta lum, iyalpātalum ām.
	pīrṅ koṭi - melleḻuttu mikkatu; ‘pīrattalar’ - attup peṟṟatu; ‘pīramoṭu pūtta’ - ammup peṟṟatu; ‘pīrvāyp pirinta’ - iyalpāyiṟṟu.	(to. e. 363, 364 nac. uri)
puṭi nūl -
puṭi nūlāvatu cārpunūl. mutaṉūl vaḻinūlkaḷukkuc ciṟu pāṉmi ottup perumpālum vēṟupaṭṭiruppatu cārpu nūlāyiṟṟu.	(naṉ. 8 irāmā.)
puṭipeyarcci vikuti puṇarntu keṭṭavi, keṭṭup piṉ mutal nīṇṭavi  -
‘keṭuvāka viyātu’ (kuṟaḷ 117) eṉpuḻik keṭu eṉpatu puṭipeyarcciyi uṇarttum talvikuti keṭṭu niṉṟatu. (‘aṟi koṉṟu’ kuṟaḷ 638 - ‘kaḷiṟukaḷam paṭutta peruñcey āṭavar’ neṭunal. 171 eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavum aṉṉa.)
kēṭu eṉpatu puṭipeyarcciyi uṇarttum talvikuti keṭṭup piṉṉar mutal ekaram nīṇṭatu. (kōḷ, ūṇ eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavum aṉṉa)	(cū. vi. pak. 33)
puṇarcci -
meyyiyum uyiriyum mutalum iṟutiyumāka uṭiya pakāppatam pakupatam eṉṉum iruvakic coṟkaḷum taṉṉoṭu tāṉum taṉṉoṭu piṟitumāy vēṟṟumip poruḷilāvatu alvaḻip poruḷilāvatu poruntu miṭattu, nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum iyalpākavum mūvakit tiripu koṇṭum poruntuvatu puṇarcciyām. uyirmeyyi meymutal eṉavum uyirīṟu eṉavum pakuttuk koḷka. ticiccollum vaṭacollum peyarviṉi iṭi uri nāṉkum pakāppatam pakupatam eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum.	(naṉ. 151)
puṇarccikkaṇ ka ca ta pa-kkuriya iyaṟki melleḻuttu -
puṇarccikkaṇ ka ca ta pa -kkaḷukkuriya iyaṟki mel leḻuttukkaḷ, avi tōṉṟum iṭattilēyē tōṉṟi mūkkiṉ vaḷiyiciyāṉ caṟṟu vēṟupaṭṭolikkum ṅa ña na ma -kkaḷām.
e-ṭu :	viḷa + kōṭu = viḷaṅkōṭu; viḷa + cetiḷ = viḷañ cetiḷ; viḷa + tōl = viḷantōl; viḷa + pū = viḷampū	(to. e. 143 nac.)
puṇarccikkuriya nāṟkaṇam -
vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam uyirkkaṇam eṉṟa nāṉkum puṇarccikkaṇ varumoḻi mutaleḻuttiṟkuriya nāṟkaṇaṅkaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṉkaṇam nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya mūṉṟu kaṇaṅkaḷum perumpālum iyalpākap puṇartaliṉ iyalpukaṇam eṉappaṭum.	(to. e. 203 - 207 nac.)
puṇarcci nilipeṟṟu amital -
puṇarcciyāvatu nilimoḻi īṟṟeḻuttoṭu varumoḻi mutaleḻuttup puṇarvatu. nilimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉarppiṟantu keṭṭuppōka, varumoḻiyatu muta leḻuttup piṉṉarp piṟantu keṭṭamiyāṉ, muṟiyē piṟantu keṭuvaṉa oruṅkuniṉṟu puṇarumāṟu iṉmiyiṉ puṇarcci eṉpatoṉṟu iṉṟāmpiṟa eṉiṉ, accoṟ kaḷik kūṟukiṉṟōrum kēṭkiṉṟōrum av vōciyi iṭiyaṟavupaṭāmi uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇē uṇarvarākaliṉ, avvōcikaḷ uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇē nilipeṟṟup puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē, piṉṉark kaṇkūṭākap puṇark kiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyām eṉpatu.			(to. e. 108 nac. uri)
puṇarcciyil vikāraṅkaḷ -
puṇarcciyil vikāram ēḻām.
mutalāvatu tōṉṟal.
	e-ṭu	:	kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam, celuḻi - celvuḻi. muṟiyē am, v - tōṉṟiṉa.
iraṇṭāvatu tirital.
	e-ṭu	:	māki - māci (kakaram cakaramākat tirintatu)
mūṉṟāvatu keṭutal.
	e-ṭu	:	yār - ār, yāvar - yār (muṟiyē yakara meyyum vakara uyirmeyyum keṭṭaṉa)

nāṉkāvatu nīḷal.
	e-ṭu	:	poḻutu - pōtu, peyar - pēr (okaramum ekaramum muṟiyē nīṇṭaṉa)
aintāvatu nilimāṟutal.
	e-ṭu	:	vicāki - vikāci (cakaram kakaram nilimāṟiṉa). nāḷikēram - nārikēḷam, taci - cati, ñimiṟu - miñiṟu, civiṟi - viciṟi.
āṟāvatu maruvi vaḻaṅkutal.
	e-ṭu	:	eṉṟanti - enti
ēḻāvatu ottu naṭattal.
	e-ṭu	:	naṇṭu - ñaṇṭu, neṇṭu - ñeṇṭu, namaṉ - ñamaṉ (nakarattiṟku ñakaram pōliyāka vantatu).		(to. vi. 37 uri)
puṇarcciyum mayakkamum -
