puṇarcciyum mayakkamum vevvēṟē. puṇarcci eṉpatu nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷ tattam taṉmi tiriyāmal ceppiṉ puṇarcci pōla niṟpatu. mayakkam eṉpatu maṇiyuḷ kōtta nūlpōlat tiripuṟṟu niṟpatu. nilimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram varumoḻi nāṟkaṇaṅkaḷoṭum puṇarumiṭattu eytum tiripukaḷit taṉakku muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷē peṟat tāṉ tiripuṟutal iṉmiyiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukara mayaṅkiyal eṉṉātu puṇariyal eṉṟār. taṉittu varal marapiṉiyuṭiya uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ aṉṉa allavām.	(to.e.pak.311 ca.pāla.)
puṇarcciyi eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyami -
puṇarcciyāvatu coṟkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu alvaḻip poruḷilō vēṟṟumipporuḷilō puṇarvataṉik kūṟutaliṉ collatikārattuk kūṟaṟpālatu eṉiṉum, nilimoḻiyīṟ ṟeḻuttiṉoṭu varumoḻi mutaleḻuttu iyitalē puṇarcci eṉappaṭutaliṉ, eḻuttiṉōṭu eḻuttup puṇarum puṇariyali eḻuttatikārattil kūṟutalē muṟiyākum. ‘niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku eḻuttoṭu, kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyiṉ mutaleḻuttu iyi’talē puṇarcci eṉpatu tol. eḻuttatikāra 108 ām nūṟpāvāl pulaṉām. (e.ā. pak. 1)
puṇarcci vikāraṅkaḷ -
patattoṭu patam puṇaruṅkāl, canti kāraṇamākap palamuṟi nilippata īṟṟeḻuttāyiṉum varumpata mutaleḻuttāyiṉum palaviṭattu oruppaṭa iraṇṭum vēṟeḻuttākat tiritalum, muṟṟum keṭutalum ākum. palamuṟi irupata naṭuvē ākkamāka mikalum ām. immūvikāram aṉṟiyum, cilamuṟi irupatam orupatamākat tiraṇṭu kalappuḻic cila eḻuttu avvaḻi vikārappaṭum eṉak koḷka.
avi tiripu (mey piṟitu ākal), aḻivu (kuṉṟal), ākkam (mikutal), tiraṭṭu (vēta + ākamam = vētākamam pōṉṟa vaṭamoḻip puṇarcci) eṉpaṉavām.	(to. vi. 21 uri)
puṇarcci vikāramum puṇarcci il vikāramum -
ilakkaṇak kottiṉit taḻuvi urikkappaṭum ivvikāraṅ kaḷuḷ, puṇarcci vikāram nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarcci yāl nikaḻum tōṉṟal - tirital - keṭutal - immūṉṟumām.
	e-ṭu	:	ūr + kuruvi = ūrkkuruvi; vēl + tali = vēṟ ṟali; paḻam + naṉṟu = paḻanaṉṟu eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.
irumoḻip puṇarcciyāṉaṉṟi orumoḻikkaṇṇēyē nikaḻum tōṉṟal - tirital - keṭutal - nilimāṟutal - virittal - nīṭṭal - kuṟukkal - valittal - melittal - tokuttal - mutal iṭik kaṭik kuṟikaḷ - eṉpaṉa puṇarcci il vikāramām.
	e-ṭu	:	yātu - yāvatu; aḻuntupaṭu - āḻntupaṭu; yāṭu - āṭu; miñiṟu - ñimiṟu, viḷiyumē - viḷiyummē; itu - ītu; tīyēṉ - tiyēṉ; kuṟuntāḷ - kuṟuttāḷ; taṭṭiyiṉ - taṇṭiyiṉ; ciṟiyavili - ciṟiyili; tāmari - mari; ōnti - ōti; nīlam - nīl	(cuvāmi. eḻut. 27)
puṇarnilic cuṭṭu -
collappaṭṭa oru collōṭu aṭutta col kūṭum nilimiyākiya karuttu. inniliyil mutalil niṟkum colli ‘niṟutta col’ eṉṟum, aṭuttu varum collik ‘kuṟittu varu kiḷavi’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. niṟutta colloṭu kuṟittu varu kiḷavi kūṭum nilimikkaṇ, niṟutta colliṉ iṟuti eḻuttum, kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyiṉ mutaleḻuttum tammuḷ puṇartalē puṇarcciyām.	(to. e. 107 nac.)
puṇarmoḻi āytam -
nilimoḻi taṉikkuṟili aṭutta lakaroṟṟu īṟṟatākavō  ḷakaroṟṟu īṟṟatākavō iruppa, alvaḻip puṇarcciyil varumoḻikkaṇ takaram mutaleḻuttāka variṉ, lakaramum ḷakaramum āytamākat tirintu puṇarum niliyumuṇṭu. aṅ ṅaṉam tiriyum niliyil āytam puṇarmoḻikkaṇ varum.
e-ṭu :	kal + tītu = kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu; muḷ + tītu = muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu
ittiripukaḷi nōkka, āytam ṟakaratti aṭuttapōtu ataṉ oliyatākavum ṭakaratti aṭuttapōtu ataṉ oliya tākavum paṇṭu olikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu.	(to. e. 39, 369, 399 nac.) (e. ā. pak. 85)
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram -
nilimoḻi iṟutiyil kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟpa, varumoḻi mutalil yakaram variṉ, avvukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātāka ataṉiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalikaram varum.
	e-ṭu	:	nāku + yātu = nākiyātu; varaku + yātu = varakiyātu; eḵku + yātu = eḵkiyātu; kokku + yātu = kokki yātu; kuraṅku + yātu = kuraṅkiyātu; pōḻtu + yātu = pōḻtiyātu.	(to. e. 410 nac)
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram -
nilimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukara īṟāy varumoḻiyoṭu puṇaru miṭattu alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruniliyilum kuṟṟiya lukaram nilipeṟṟirukkum.
	e-ṭu	:	nāku kaṭitu, nāku kaṭumi	(to. e. 408 nac. uri)
alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉṟa iruvakip puṇarccikkaṇṇum nilimoḻiyiṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāy niṟintuviṭum. āyiṉ nilimoḻiyiṟuti valloṟṟut toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramāka, atuvum kakara ukaramāka, varu moḻiyākak kakara mutal moḻi variṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaram ākātu kuṟṟiyalukaramākavē  niṟkum.
	e-ṭu	:	nāku kaṭitu, varaku kaṭitu; nākukaṭumi, varakukaṭumi eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāyiṟṟu.
cekkukkaṇi, cukkukkoṭu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇar moḻikkaṇ muṟṟiyalukaram ākātu kuṟṟiyalukaramākavē niṉṟatu.	(to. e. 409, 410 iḷa. uri)
puṇariyal -
itu tolkāppiya eḻuttuppaṭalattu nāṉkām iyal; 40 nūṟpākkaḷi uṭiyatu. itaṉkaṇ, ellāc coṟkaḷum uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatum, kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu puḷḷi yīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatum, uyirmeyyīṟu uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatum, meyyīṟukaḷ ellām puḷḷi peṟum eṉpatum, meym mutalkaḷ ellām uyiroṭu kūṭi uyirmey mutalākum eṉpatum, meymmutal uyir mutal eṉpaṉa meyyīṟu uyirīṟu eṉpavaṟṟoṭu puṇarum eṉpatum, peyaroṭu peyarum toḻilum toḻiloṭu peyarum toḻilum puṇarumiṭattu iyalpē yaṉṟit tiripum nikaḻum eṉpatum, attiripāṉatu meypiṟi tātal - mikutal - kuṉṟal - eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatum, nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum aṭiyoṭu kūṭiyum puṇarum eṉpatum, puṇarcci vēṟṟumippuṇarcci yum alvaḻippuṇarcciyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉpatum, eḻuvāyttoṭarum viḷittoṭarum alvaḻiyākum, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumittoṭar mutal ēḻām vēṟṟumit toṭar īṟāka āṟumē vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyākum eṉpa tum, puṇarccikkaṇ ku - kaṇ - atu - eṉṟa urupukaḷ peṟum vikāram ivi eṉpatum, vēṟṟumip puṇarccikku nilimoḻi peyarē eṉpatum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi ākiya iruvakip peyari aṭuttum cāriyi varum eṉpatum, cāriyikaḷ ivi eṉpatum, nilimoḻi varu moḻikaḷukku iṭiyē varum cāriyikaḷuḷ tiripuṭiyaṉa uḷa eṉpatum, cāriyi peṟātaṉavum uḷa eṉpatum, eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷ ivi eṉpatum, uṭalmēl uyir vantu oṉṟuvatu iyalpupuṇarcci eṉpatum, meyyīṟu pōlvatu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟup puṇarcci eṉpatum, uṭampaṭu mey uyirīṟṟiyum uyirmutaliyum iṇikka iṭiyē varum eṉpatum, eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal oliyāṉēyē teḷivāka uṇarappaṭum eṉpatum innāṟpatu nuṟpākka ḷāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.	(to.e. 103-142.)
‘puṇariyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā’taṉa -
puṇarccikkaṇ nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷākap pakut tuṇarappaṭātaṉa nāṉku. avi varumāṟu:
1.	kuṟippu, paṇpu, ici - eṉṟa poruṇmikkaṇ uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟumāy niṟkum collāki oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmi kuṟinta coṟ kaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷ:
e-ṭu :	kaṇ viṇṇa viṇittatu, kaṇ viṇ viṇittatu - kuṟippu uriccol; āṭi veḷḷa viḷarttatu, āṭi. veḷ viḷarttatu - paṇpuuriccol; kaṭal olla olittatu, kaṭal ol olittatu - ici uric col
ivi uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉuḷ aṭakkalākāmiyiṉ neṟippaṭa vārātaṉa.
2. 	uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟa īriṭattum uḷavākiya oruvaṉ - orutti- palar- oṉṟu- pala - eṉṉum aimpā liṉiyum aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa paṇpukoḷ peyar tokum tokiccol: karumpārppāṉ, karumpārppaṉi, karumpārppār, karuṅkutiri, karuṅkutirikaḷ eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ, kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār, kariyatākiya kutiri, kariyavākiya kutirikaḷ - eṉa aimpāliṉiyum uṇarttum paṇpu koḷ peyar tokkavāṟu.
	karu eṉṟa paṇpu irutiṇi aimpālukkum potu ātaliṉ, orupālukkuriya collāṉ virittal kūṭāmiyiṉ, karumpārppāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟik karu + pārppāṉ mutaliyaṉavākap pirittuk kāṇṭal kūṭātu.
3. 	ceyyum, ceyta - eṉṟa peyareccac coṟkaḷi ṉuṭiya kālam kāṭṭum ummum akaramum oru coṟkaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭipeyarcci tokku niṟkum coṟkaḷ:
e-ṭu :	āṭaraṅku, ceykuṉṟu, puṇarpoḻutu, arivāḷ, kolyāṉi, celcelavu - eṉa nilaṉ - poruḷ - kālam - karuvi - viṉimutaṟkiḷavi - viṉi- ivaṟṟik koṇṭu muṭiyum peyareccattoki yām viṉittokikaḷi virikkuṅkāl, ceyta eṉṟa peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, (āṭāniṉṟa araṅku- āṭum araṅku- eṉac) ceyyum eṉṟa peyareccattu īṟu virinta um īṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum avaṟṟiṉā ṉāya puṭipeyarcciyit tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ, avaṟṟi oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmiyiṉ, āṭaraṅku mutaliya vaṟṟi āṭu+ araṅku mutalavākap pirittup puṇarkkalākātu eṉṟār.
4.	tamatu taṉmiyik kūṟumiṭattu, niṟutta collum kuṟittu varum kiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇup peyariṉatu tokuti:
pattu eṉa nilimoḻi amittup pattu eṉṟa varumoḻi yik koṇarntu puṇarkkappaṭātu, pappattu eṉavum paḵpattu eṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarappaṭum. 
orōvoṉṟu eṉpatum atu. ‘ōroṉṟu ōroṉṟāk koṭu’ eṉṟu aṭukkutal poruntum.
pattu+ pattu - eṉṟa niṟuttip puṇarppiṉ, aḵtu ummit tokiyō paṇputtokiyō ākumaṉṟip ‘pattup pattākat taṉittaṉi’ - eṉṟu poruḷ tārāmiyiṉ,  pappattu eṉpataṉi nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pirittuk kāṇṭal kūṭātu eṉpatu.	(to. e. 482 nac. uri)
a) 	koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ - eṉpuḻik ‘koḷ’ eṉṟa nilimoḻiyōṭu ‘eṉa’ eṉṟa varumoḻi iṭiccollip pirittup puṇarkkappaṭātu, ‘eṉa’ eṉṟa iṭiccollip pirittāl atu poruḷ tārātu ātaliṉ.
uṟuppilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟip pakupatattip pakuti vikuti iṭi nili cāriyi canti vikāram - eṉap piṉṉuḷḷōr pirittup puṇarttalum iḷampūraṇarkkum nacciṉārkkiṉi yarkkum pērāciriyarkkum uṭaṉpāṭu aṉṟu.			(e. 482 iḷa. uri)  (po. 663 pērā. uri)
putiyaṉa pukutal -
muṟkālattu illāta cila piṟkālattu ilakkaṇamāka varutal. avi varumāṟu: 
taṉmi orumikkaṇ aṉvikutiyum, paṉmikkaṇ ōm vikutiyum, nīr uṇṇum - nīr tiṉṉum - eṉa um īṟṟavāy varum paṉmi ēvalum, alīṟṟu viyaṅkōḷ eṭuttōtāta piṟa īṟṟavāy irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻttu mutaliya ēvaṟporuḷavāy nikaḻvaṉavuḷavēl aviyum, 
uṇṭu eṉpatu aḵṟiṇi orumikkēyaṉṟip piṟavaṟṟiṟkum potuviṉiyāy varutalum, 
yār eṉṉum viṉā viṉikkuṟippumuṟṟu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ varutalum, yār eṉpatu ār eṉa marīi varutalum, ī-tā-koṭu - eṉṉum mūṉṟum iḻintōṉ - oppōṉ - mikkōṉ - irappuriyāka muṟiyē vārāmal mayaṅki varuvaṉa uḷavēl aviyum,
maṉ - kol- eṉaṟpāla iṭiccoṟkaḷ maṉṉi - koṉṉi - eṉa varuvaṉavum, 
‘avaṉē koṇṭāṉ’ eṉpuḻi ēkāram orōvaḻi ‘avaṉ koḷkilaṉ’ eṉa etirmaṟipporuḷ tarutalum, (iṉṉō raṉṉavum) putiyaṉa pukutalām.			(naṉ. 332, 337-339, 349, 407, 420, 422 caṅkara.)
purōvātam -
yātāṉum oru nimittattāṉ oṉṟaṉi muṟkūṟi vēṟoru nimittattāṉ ataṉiyē piṉṉum kūṟutal anuvātam. anuvātattiṟku muṉṉark kuṟippiṭappaṭuvatu purōvātam. purōvātatti uṇarntāltāṉ anuvātatti uṇara muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : ‘aṉ āṉ  aḷ āḷ  ār ār  pa(m) mār
			a ā  kuṭutuṟu  eṉ ēṉ  al aṉ’ (naṉ. 140)
mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa ‘aṉ’ vikuti purōvātam; piṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa ‘aṉ’ anuvātam. iraṇṭāvatu aṉvikutiyi nōkka mutalāvatu purōvātam ām.	(cū. vi. pak. 19)
puḻaṉ : urupoṭu puṇarumāṟu -
puḻaṉ eṉpatu urupēṟkumiṭattu attum iṉṉum māṟi varap peṟum.
varumāṟu: puḻatti, puḻaṉiṉi 
irucāriyiyum oruṅkē peṟṟup puḻattiṉi eṉavum varum.
(to.e.193 nac. uri)
puḷ: puṇarumāṟu -
paṟaviyi uṇarttum puḷ eṉṟa poruṭpeyar iruvaḻi yum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻit toḻiṟpeyar pōla ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟuvatu perumpāṉmi;  meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukarap pēṟum, yakaram variṉ iyalpum, uyir variṉ ḷakaroṟṟu iraṭṭutalum eytalām.
e-ṭu :	puḷḷuk kaṭitu, puḷḷukkaṭumi; puḷḷu ñāṉṟatu, puḷḷuñāṟci; puḷḷu valitu, puḷḷuvalimi; puḷ yātu, puḷyāppu; puḷḷaritu, puḷḷarumi.
	puḷ eṉpataṉ ḷakaram vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṭakaramākavum, meṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṇakaramākavum tiritalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	puṭ kaṭitu, puṭkaṭumi; puṇ ñāṉṟatu, puṇñāṟci. puḷ eṉpataṉmuṉ iṭikkaṇam varumoḻiyā yiṉ ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum puṇarum. 
e-ṭu :	puḷḷu valitu, puḷḷu valimi; puḷ valitu, puḷ valimi	(to. e. 403 nac.)
puḷḷi iṟuti -
meyyiṉi īṟāka uṭiya coṟkaḷ. meyyīṟukaḷāvaṉa, ñ ṇ n m ṉ y rl v ḻ ḷ eṉṟa patiṉoṉṟām.
	e-ṭu	:	uriñ, uṇ, porun, maram, poṉ, tēy, pār, cel, tev, vāḻ, kēḷ.	(to.e.78. nac.)
‘puḷḷi iṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷavi’ -
mey īṟāka amiyum muṉṉilic coṟkaḷ. muṉṉilic coṟkaḷ, muṉṉiṉṟāṉ ceyali uṇarttuvaṉavum, muṉṉiṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavum eṉa iruvakiya. muṉṉiṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa ēvalviṉi eṉavum paṭum.
yakara rakara īṟukaḷ muṉṉiṉṟāṉ niṉṟārtam toḻil uṇarttuvaṉa.
	e-ṭu :	uṇpāy - yakara īṟu; uṇpīr  - rakara īṟu
ṉakara īṟu muṉṉiṉṟārit toḻiṟpaṭukkum ēval poruḷ tarum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇmiṉ koṟṟīr - ṉakarīṟu
			ñṇ n m l v ḷ ṉ - īṟṟu ēvalviṉikaḷ ukaram peṟum.
	e-ṭu :	uriñu koṟṟā, uṇṇu koṟṟā, porunu koṟṟā, tirumu koṟṟā, kollu koṟṟā, vavvu koṟṟā, koḷḷu koṟṟā, paṉṉu koṟṟā.
ivaṟṟuḷ ṇ l ḷ ṉ - eṉpaṉa ukaram peṟāmalum varum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇ koṟṟā, kol koṟṟā, koḷ koṟṟā, paṉ koṟṟā.
y r - eṉṟa īṟṟu ēvalviṉikaḷ uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : 	ey koṟṟā, eyk koṟṟā; īr  koṟṟā, īrk koṟṟā
ḻakara īṟṟu ēvalviṉi iyalpākum.
	e-ṭu :	vāḻ koṟṟā
eṉavē, muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻil  uṇarttum muṉṉili īṟukaḷ  y r - eṉpaṉa;  muṉṉiṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuppaṉa ñ ṇ n m ṉ y r l v ḻḷ - eṉṟa patiṉō rīṟukaḷām.			(to.  e. 151 co.225, 226 nac.)
puḷḷi īṟṟu muṉ uyir -
nilimoḻi puḷḷiyīṟṟatāka, varumoḻi mutalil uyir variṉ, av varumoḻi uyir nilimoḻiyīṟṟup puḷḷi yeḻuttoṭu kūṭi uyirmeyyāyviṭum. 
e-ṭu :	āl + ili = ālili; nāku + aritu = nākaritu
meyyīṟu puḷḷi peṟuvatu pōlak kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum puḷḷi peṟum eṉavē, puḷḷiyīṟu eṉpataṉuḷ meyyīṟu kuṟṟukara īṟu eṉa iraṇṭum aṭaṅkum. uyir, meyyīṟ ṟoṭum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟoṭum cērntu iyalum.
uyir, iyalpāya meyyīṟṟoṭum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟoṭum cērntu iyalutal pōlavē, vitiyāṉ vanta meyyīṟu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu ivaṟṟoṭum cērntu iyalum.
	e-ṭu : ataṉi - at + aṉ + ai		avaṟṟoṭum - av + vaṟṟu + oṭu +  um
atu eṉpatu ukaram keṭṭu ‘at’ āyiṟṟu. muṟṟiyalukara mākiya oṭu eṉpatu avaṟṟu eṉpataṉoṭu cērntu avaṟṟoṭu - eṉak kuṟṟiyalukara īṟāki ‘um’ puṇarntu, kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ mītu ukaruyir ūra avaṟṟoṭum eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
uyirmutalmoḻi vara, nilimoḻiyīṟṟumuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṟu kūṟiya tol. kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṉā rāy iyalpākap puṇarum eṉṟē yāṇṭum kūṟaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṉ mēlum uyir ūrntu varum eṉpatē avar karuttu.	(e. ā. pak. 105)
puḷḷiyīṟākiya kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟṟa eḻuttil kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭa eñciniṟkum meyyiṉmītu uyir ēṟi muṭiyum. itu civañāṉa. karuttu. pi.cā. cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār karuttum atuvē.
	e-ṭu :	nāku + aritu ḻū nāk + aritu = nākaritu				(e. ku. pak. 142)
puḷḷi peṟum eḻuttukkaḷ -
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷoṭu varivaṭivil vēṟupāṭillāta ekara okaraṅkaḷ, meyyeḻuttukkaḷ, īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ, āytam - eṉpaṉa puḷḷi peṟum. 
makarakkuṟukkam mēṟpuḷḷiyōṭu uṭpuḷḷiyum peṟum. (m¶)
puḷḷi, eḻuttiṉ iyalpāṉa māttiriyip  pātiyākak kuṟittatit terivippataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka varum.				(to. e. 15, 16, 14, 105, nac.)  (po. 320 pērā.)
puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal -
puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal tolkāppiya eḻuttup paṭalattiṉ eṭṭāvatu iyalākum. itu 110 nuṟpākkaḷi uṭiyatu.
ñakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci; nakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci; ṇakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci; makāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; yakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; rakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; lakārīṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; vakārīṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; ḻakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; ḷakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarcci; ōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭi - eṉpaṉa itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkiyuḷa. ip puṟaṉaṭi ciṅkanōkkāka muṉṉuḷḷa uyir mayaṅkiyalukkum piṉṉuḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalukkum okkum.	(to.e.296- 405 nac.)
puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal puṟaṉaṭic ceyti -
maṇṇap pattam - alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu.
poṉṉappattam - alvaḻikkaṇ ṉakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu.
maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi - alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu ammup peṟṟatu.
poṉṉaṅkaṭṭi - alvaḻikkaṇ ṉakara īṟu ammup peṟṟatu.
maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi - alvaḻikkaṇ marūu muṭivu.
kāṉāṅkōḻi - alvaḻikkaṇ marūu muṭipu. 
vēyiṉtali - yakarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟatu.
nīr kuṟitu - rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ  iyalpu; vērkuṟitu, vērkkuṟitu - rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ uṟaḻcci; vaṭacārk kūri, mēlcārk kūri - rakara īṟu valleḻuttup peṟṟa marūumuṭipu; ampark koṇṭāṉ, impark koṇṭāṉ, umpark koṇṭāṉ, empark koṇṭāṉ - eṉa ēḻaṉ urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vanta peyarkaḷ valloṟṟup peṟṟaṉa.
takarkkuṭṭi, pukarppōttu - rakarīṟu irupeyaroṭ ṭiṉkaṇ valloṟṟu mikkatu.	
viḻaṉ kāṭu - lakarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṟakaram ākātu ṉakaram āyiṟṟu.
kallampāṟi, ucilaṅkōṭu, eliyālaṅkāy, puṭōlaṅkāy -  lakara īṟu ammup peṟṟatu.
kallāmpāṟi - marūu muṭipu peṟṟatu
aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ - attup peṟṟatu.
aḻuṅkaṟ pōr, puḻuṅkaṟ cōṟu - lakarīṟṟu alvaḻittiripu.
vīḻ kuṟitu,  vīḻk kuṟitu  - ḻakarīṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.
tāḻampāvi - ḻakarīṟu alvaḻiyil ammup peṟṟatu.
yāḻiṉ kōṭu - ḻakarīṟu ‘iṉ’ peṟṟatu.
muṉṉāḷip paricu, orunāḷik kuḻavi  - ḷakarīṟu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟatu.
kuḷattiṉ puṟam	-	makarīṟu attum iṉṉum peṟṟatu.
poṉṉukku, poruḷukku, nellukku, patiṉēḻu, pallukku, collukku - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum ceyyuṭkaṇṇum puḷḷi yīṟukaḷ ukaram peṟṟaṉa.
pal +ku = paṟku - eṉa urupēṟpuḻi, lakaram ṟakaramākat tirintatu.	(to.e.405  nac.)
puḷḷum vaḷḷum puṇarumāṟu -
puḷ, vaḷ - eṉpaṉa toḻiṟpeyar pōla yakaram allāta meymmutalmoḻi varumiṭattē ukaraccāriyi peṟutalu muṇṭu; potuvitippaṭi alvaḻiyil īṟṟu ḷakaram ṭakarat tōṭu uṟaḻntu varutal mikuti; vēṟṟumiyil ḷakaram ṭakaramākat tiritalē mikuti eṉka.
	puḷ + kaṭitu = puḷ kaṭitu, puṭ kaṭitu, puḷḷuk kaṭitu - alvaḻi
	puḷ + kaṭumi = puṭkaṭumi, puḷḷukkaṭumi - vēṟṟumi
	vaḷ + kaṭitu = vaḷ kaṭitu, vaṭ kaṭitu, vaḷḷuk kaṭitu - alvaḻi
vaḷ + kaṭumi = vaṭkaṭumi, vaḷḷukkaṭumi - vēṟṟumi puṇ melitu, puṇmeṉmi; puḷ valitu, puḷvalimi - eṉa ēṉikkaṇattoṭum puṇarttu muṭikka.	(naṉ. 234) 
puḷimarappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
puḷi eṉṟa marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl, puḷiyampū; puḷiyañeri, puḷiyanuṉi, puḷiyamuri; puḷiyayāḻ, puḷiyavaṭṭu -
eṉa vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi ammup peṟṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum varuvaḻi ammiṉ makaram keṭṭum puṇarntavāṟu.	(to. e. 244 nac.)
puṟakkaruvi -
itu karuvivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu.  īṇṭuk karuviyāvatu nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarccikkuk karuviyāka utavu vatu. puṟakkaruviyāvatu nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāy niṟkum moḻikaḷiṉ marapu kūṟuvatu. atu ceykiyākiya puṇarccik kuriya karuvi kūṟātu, puṇarcci nikaḻttuvataṟkuriya moḻikaḷiṉ marapu kūṟutaliṉ puṟakkaruvi āyiṟṟu. ākavē moḻimarapu eṉṉum iyal puṟakkaruviyām.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
puṟacceyki -
itu ceykivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ceykiyāvatu nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarcci. nilimoḻikkup puṟamāy ataṉi aṭuttu vantu puṇarum varumoḻic  ceyki kūṟuvatu puṟac ceykiyām.
lakara ṉakara īṟukaḷiṉ muṉ varum takaram ṟakaramākavum nakaram ṉakaramāka vum tiritalum, ḷakara ṇakara īṟukaḷiṉ muṉ varum takara nakaraṅkaḷ ṭakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākat tiritalum pōlvaṉa puṟac ceykiyām.
e-ṭu :	kal+tītu = kaṟṟītu; poṉ + tītu = poṉṟītu; kal + naṉṟu = kaṉṉaṉṟu; poṉ + naṉṟu = poṉṉaṉṟu; muḷ + tītu = muṭṭītu; muḷ + naṉṟu = muṇṇaṉṟu; kaṇ + tītu = kaṇṭītu; kaṇ + naṉṟu = kaṇṇaṉṟu
ivaṟṟuḷ varumoḻi mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu puṟacceyki yām.	(to. e. 149, 150 nac.)

puṟappuṟak karuvi -
itu karuvivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. moḻikaḷ ātaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum piṟappum kūṟum nulmarapum piṟappiyalum puṟappuṟak karuvikaḷām. avi puṇarccikkuriya puṟakkaruvikaḷākiya, moḻikaḷi ākkum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum piṟappum kūṟutaliṉ puṟappuṟakkaruvikaḷ āyiṉa.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
puṟappuṟac ceyki -
itu ceykivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. nilimoḻi īṟum varumoḻi mutalum ceyki peṟātu niṟpa, avviraṇṭi yum poruttuvataṟku uṭampaṭumey pōṉṟa ōreḻuttu varuvatu puṟappuṟac ceykiyām.
	e-ṭu	:	maṇi+ aḻakitu ḻū maṇi + y + aḻakitu = maṇi 						yaḻakitu
			kō + aḻakitu ḻū kō + v + aḻakitu = kōvaḻakitu
iṭiyē yakaroṟṟum vakaroṟṟum puṟappuṟac ceykiyāṉ vantaṉa.	(to. e. 1, 140 nac. uri)
puṟaṉaṭi -
itu nūṟpā vaki āṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ciṟappu nūṟpākkaḷiṉ puṟattē aṭuttu varutaliṉ (puṟaṉ + aṭi) itu puṟaṉaṭi eṉappaṭum. nuṟpākkaḷiṉ puṟattē naṭattaliṉ ( puṟam+ naṭi) puṟanaṭi eṉiṉum amiyum.	(i.ko. 86)
puṟaṉaṭi nūṟpā, ciṟappu nuṟpākkaḷil kūṟappaṭātu eñci niṟkum ceytikaḷik kōṭaṟku vaḻikōluvatāy uḷḷatu.
piṇṭam, toki, vaki, kuṟi, ceyki, puṟaṉaṭi- eṉa nūṟpā aṟuvakippaṭum. perumpāṉmiyum ovvōriyal iṟutiyilum puṟaṉaṭi nūṟpā avviyaṟ ceytiyi niṟivu ceyvataṟkāka iṭam peṟum.	(naṉ. pāyi. 20)
pū eṉpataṉ muṉ valliṉam -
pū eṉṟa peyar nilimoḻiyāka niṟka, valliṉam varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ nikaḻumāyiṉ, iṭiyē valleḻuttu mikutalē yaṉṟi ataṟku iṉamāṉa melleḻuttum mikum.
	e-ṭu :	pū + koṭi, cōli, taṭam, poḻil = pūkkoṭi, pūṅkoṭi; pūccōli, pūñcōli;  pūttaṭam, pūntaṭam; pūppoḻil, pūmpoḻil.
vaṉmimeṉmi mikutal ceviyiṉpam nōkkik koḷka. pūttoḻil, pūttoṭi eṉavum, pūmpoḻil eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.	(naṉ. 200 caṅ.)
pū : puṇarumāṟu  -
malari uṇarttum pū eṉṟa col alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum. vēṟṟumikkaṇ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum iṉamelleḻuttu mikutalum urittu.
e-ṭu:	pūkkaṭitu; pū naṉṟu, pūvaṟitu, pū vaḻakiyatu (vakaram 
	uṭampaṭumey); pūkkoṭi, pūṅkoṭi			(to. e. 264, 268 nac.)
pūti mutaliyavaṟṟil tīṭṭiya varivaṭivu  -
‘puḷḷiviṭ ṭavvoṭu’ eṉṉumcūttirattil varivaṭivu olivaṭivu eṉṉum eḻuttiṉ irutiṟaṉum viravik kūṟiṉār, tirunīṟu mutaliyavaṟṟil tīṭṭiya varivaṭivam olivaṭivu pōlap payaṉ tarum oṟṟumi kuṟittu.
puḷḷiviṭṭu avvoṭu muṉ uruvātal, ēṉi uyirōṭu uruvu tirital, uyiriṉ vaṭivoḻittal	 - vari vaṭivam;
uyir aḷavātal, oṟṟu muṉṉāy olittal  - olivaṭivam			(naṉ. 89 caṅkara.)
pūl:puṇarumāṟu -
pūl eṉṟa marappeyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ uṟaḻntum, iṭi uyirkkaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum. vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ ammuc cāriyiyum ciṟupāṉmi āmcāriyiyum peṟṟup puṇarum.  meṉkaṇattuḷḷum ñakaramakaraṅkaḷ varuvaḻi lakaram ṉakaramāyt tiriyum eṉka.
	e-ṭu	:	pūlkaṭitu, pūṟkaṭitu - pūl(ṉ) ñāṉṟatu, pūl valitu, pūlaḻakitu - alvaḻi; pūlaṅkōṭu, pūlañeri, pūla viṟaku; pūlāṅkōṭu, pūlāṅkaḻi - vēṟṟumi.				(to. e. 368, 375 nac. uri)
peṇpāṟpeyarp pakupatam cilavaṟṟatu muṭipu -
peṇpāṟpeyarp pakupatam muṭippuḻi iṉṉa kulattār eṉṉum poruṇmi tōṉṟa muṭikka.
araci eṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟu: •‘tattam..... pakutiyākum’ (naṉ. 133) eṉpataṉāṉ aracu eṉṉum pakutiyit tantu, ‘aṉ āṉ’ (139) eṉpataṉāṉ ikara vikutiyi niṟuvi, ‘uyirvariṉ ukkuṟaḷ’ (163) eṉpataṉāṉ ukarattik keṭuttuc cakara oṟṟiṉmēl ‘uṭalmēl’ (203) eṉpataṉāṉ ikaruyiri ēṟṟi muṭikka. 
pārppaṉi: ‘tattam... pakutiyākum’ eṉpataṉāṉ pārppāṉ eṉṉum pakutiyi mutalvittu, ataṉi ‘viḷampiya pakuti’ (138) eṉpata ṉāṉ pārppaṉ- eṉa aṉ īṟākki, ‘aṉ āṉ’ eṉpataṉāṉ ikara vikutiyik koṇarntu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka.
vāṇicci: vāṇikaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyi ‘viḷampiya pakuti’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘kaṉ’ keṭuttu, ikaravikutiyik koṇarntu cakara iṭiniliyi varuvittu ataṉimikuvittu, uyirēṟṟi muṭikka.
uḻatti: uḻavaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyi ‘viḷampiya pakuti’ eṉpata ṉāṉ ‘vaṉ’keṭuttu, ikara vikuti koṇarntu, ‘ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampaliṉ’ (140) eṉpataṉāṉ takara iṭiniliyi varuvittu, ataṉi mikuvittu, uyirēṟṟi muṭikka.
taccicci: taccaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyi ‘aṉ’ keṭuttu niṟuvi, ikara vikutiyik koṇarntu ‘ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘ic’ eṉṉum iṭiniliyi varuvittu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka.	(naṉ. 144 mayili.)
peyariṭinilikaḷ -
vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ, villavaṉ, ellavaṉ, katiravaṉ, cūriyavaṉ  - eṉpaṉa  akara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
cēramāṉ, kaṭṭimāṉ - eṉpaṉa makara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
valicci, pulicci - eṉpaṉa cakara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
pulitti, vaṇṇātti - eṉpaṉa takara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
veḷḷāṭṭi, maliyāṭṭi - eṉpaṉa ṭakara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
kaṇakkicci, taccicci- eṉpaṉa ‘ic’ iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
canti vakiyāṉum potuccāriyi vakiyāṉum muṭiyāvaḻi ivvāṟu varuvaṉa iṭinili eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ. 140 mayili.)
aṟiñaṉ eṉpatu ñakara iṭinili peṟṟatu.
ōtuvāṉ, pāṭuvāṉ - eṉpaṉa vakara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
valicci, pulicci - eṉpaṉa cakara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
vaṇṇātti, pāṇatti; maliyāṭṭi, veḷḷāṭṭi; tanti, enti, nunti- eṉpaṉa takara iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.			(naṉ. 141 caṅkara.)
aṟiñaṉ, viṉiñaṉ, kaviñaṉ - ñakaroṟṟu iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
ōtuvāṉ, pāṭuvāṉ - vakaroṟṟu iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
valicci, iṭicci - cakaroṟṟu iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
ceṭṭicci, taccicci - ‘iccu’ eṉṉum iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
katiravaṉ, ellavaṉ, vāṉavaṉ - akara uyirām iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.
cēramāṉ, kōmāṉ, vaṭamaṉ - makaroṟṟu iṭinili peṟṟaṉa.	(naṉ. 141 irāmā.)
uṇṭavaṉ urittavaṉ uṇṇāniṉṟavaṉ uṇpavaṉ - mutaliya  viṉippeyarkaḷ mukkāla iṭinilikaḷ (ṭ, t, āniṉṟu, p mutaliya) peṟṟavāṟum, vāṉavaṉ mīṉavaṉ villavaṉ ellavaṉ katiravaṉ kariyavaṉ-mutaliyavi‘a’ eṉṉum iṭinili peṟṟavāṟum, cēramāṉ kaṭṭimāṉ-mutaliyavi makara iṭinili peṟṟavāṟum, valicci paṉatti maliyāṭṭi mutaliyavi muṟiyē cakara takara ṭakara iṭi nili peṟṟavāṟum, ceṭṭicci, taccicci - mutaliyavi ‘iccu’ eṉṉum iṭinili peṟṟavāṟum kāṇka. piṟavum aṉṉa.		(i. vi. 52. uri)
peyarniliccuṭṭu -
peyarnilic cuṭṭāvatu cuṭṭunilip peyar eṉṟavāṟu. avi poruḷi oruvar cuṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa niliyiyuṭiya peyarkaḷ. avi uyartiṇip peyarum aḵṟiṇip peyarum eṉa iruvakiya. potuppeyareṉa oṉṟu iṉṟu. aḵtu aḵṟiṇip peyarākavō uyar tiṇip peyarākavō, orunērattil oṉṟākattāṉ iruttal vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ, potuppeyar, uyartiṇip peyar aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum.		 	(to. e. 117nac. uri)
peyarniliccuṭṭu-peyarākiya potunilimiyatu karuttu.		(118 iḷa. uri)
peyar puṇarum niliyākiya karuttiṉkaṇ 	(e. ku. pak. 124)
peyarppakupatam taṉṉoṭumpiṟitoṭum puṇartal -
maliyaṉ maṉṉavaṉ- maliyaṉ maṉ eṉavum, vāṉavaṉ vāḷavaṉ - vāṉavaṉ vāḷ eṉavum, paraṇiyāṉ pāravaṉ - paraṇiyāṉ pār eṉavum, iḷiyaḷ maṭavāḷ - iḷiyaḷ peṇ eṉavum, kariyāṉ maliyaṉ - kariyāṉ kāl eṉavum, ūṇaṉtīṉaṉ - ūṇaṉ uram eṉavum peyarppakupatam taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum vantatu.	(naṉ.150 mayili.)
peyarppakupatam pirippu  -
peyarp pakupatam pirittāl, pakuti pakāppatamum vikuti vēṟu poruḷil iṭiccollumāyt toṭarntu niṉṟu poruḷi viḷakkum. avi ūraṉ, veṟpaṉ, villi, vāḷi- eṉpaṉa. 	(naṉ.131 mayili,)
peyar puṇarnili vēṟṟumi vaḻiya -
peyar puṇarnili - peyarccol iṇiyumvaḻi. peyarc col puṇarum vaḻiyilēyē vēṟṟumip puṇarcci amiyum. nālvakic coṟkaḷum iruvaḻiyum puṇarum eṉa eytiyataṉi maṟuttup peyarccol puṇarum niliyē vēṟṟumiyām eṉavē, viṉiccol mutaliyamūṉṟum puṇarumvaḻi alvaḻiya āvaṉa aṉṟi, vēṟṟumi vaḻiya vārā;peyarccoṟkaḷ, vēṟṟumivaḻi - alvaḻi - eṉa iruvaḻiyum ām eṉpatu. 	(cū. vi. pak. 51) 
	e-ṭu :cāttaṉ makaṉ-peyaroṭu peyar-vēṟṟumip puṇarcci
			nilam kaṭantāṉ-peyaroṭu viṉi – vēṟṟumip					 puṇarcci
			vanta cāttaṉ-viṉiyoṭu peyar							 -alvaḻippuṇarcci
			maṟṟilatu-iṭiyoṭu viṉi - alvaḻippuṇarcci
			naṉipēti- uriyoṭu peyar - alvaḻippuṇarcci
			tavaccēyatu-uriyoṭu viṉi - alvaḻippuṇarcci
			maṟṟip poruḷ- iṭiyoṭu peyar- alvaḻip 					puṇarcci
nālvakip puṇarcciyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇ ṭāy aṭakkaliṉ, viṉivaḻiyum urupu varum eṉpatupaṭa niṉṟataṉi itu vilakkiṟṟu.	(to. e. 117 iḷa. uri)
“āyiṉ ivvilakkutal ‘viṉiyeṉappaṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu’ eṉṟa viṉiyiyal mutal cūttirattāṉ peṟutum eṉiṉ, atu mutaṉiliyik kūṟiyatām.”			(to. e. 116 nac. uri)
peyar vikuti peṟutal -
villaṉ villāṉ, vaḷiyaḷ vaḷiyāḷ, ūrar ūrār, villi vāḷi araci ceṭṭicci, kātaṟi mūkkaṟi -ivi viṉiyiṉ vikutikaḷ cila peyaril vantaṉa. (aṉ āṉ, aḷ āḷ, ar ār, i, ai - eṉpaṉa.)	(naṉ. 140 caṅkara.)
peyar viṉikaṭkē puṇarcci kūṟal -
iṭiyum uriyum peyarviṉikaḷi aṭuttallatu tāmāka nillāmiyiṉ peyarviṉikaḷukkē puṇarcci kūṟap paṭṭatu.	(to.e.109 iḷa. uri)
iṭiccollum uriccollum puṇarkkum ceykip paṭṭuḻip puṇarppu ciṟupāṉmi ātaliṉ avi vitantu kūṟap peṟavilli.	(108 nac. uri)
perunūl -
oru poruḷ kiḷanta cūttiram, iṉamoḻi kiḷanta ōttu, potumoḻi kiḷanta paṭalam - eṉṉum immūṉṟu uṟuppa hṉ iyaṉṟa ilakkaṇam.	(civañā. pā. vi. pak. 9)
	e-ṭu	:	tolkāppiyam, vīracōḻiyam, naṉṉūl, ciṉṉūl, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam, toṉṉūl viḷakkam, muttu vīriyam, cuvāminātam.
pēṉ eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
pēṉ eṉpatu paṇṭikkāla iyaṟpeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. pēṉ eṉṟa iyaṟpeyar tanti eṉṟa muṟippeyarāṭum, makkaḷ muṟimiyil varum iyaṟpeyarkaḷoṭum puṇarumvaḻi, iyaṟpeyarkaḷukkuriya ciṟappup puṇarcci viti peṟātu, aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyariṉ potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	pēṉ+ tanti = pēṉṟanti (pēṉukkut tanti)
	pēṉ + koṟṟaṉ = pēṉkoṟṟaṉ (pēṉ eṉpa vaṉukku makaṉākiya koṟṟaṉ)	(to.e.351 nac.)

potu eḻuttu -
āriya uyir patiṉāṟaṉuḷ, naṭuvilirukkum nāṉkuyirum kaṭiyilirukkum iraṇṭuyirum allāta pattuyirum āriyattiṟkum tamiḻiṟkum potuveḻuttākum. iṉi, āriya mey muppattēḻaṉuḷ, kakaramutal aintu varukkaṅkaḷiṉ mutalil niṟkiṉṟa ka ca ṭa ta pa-kkaḷum,  kaṭiyil niṟkiṉṟa ṅa ña ṇa na ma -kkaḷum, ya ra la va ḷa-kkaḷum irumikkum potu veḻuttukkaḷām.	(mu. vī. moḻi. 11-13)
potuk karuvi -
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattu mutal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷākiya nūlmarapu, moḻimarapu, piṟappiyal eṉpaṉa. mutaleḻuttu cārpeḻuttu, moḻi mutalil varuvaṉa, iṟutiyil varuvaṉa, naṭpeḻuttu, pakiyeḻuttu, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam- ivi paṟṟiyē immūṉṟu iyalkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ceykikku nērāṉa karuvi aṉmiyiṉ potukkaruvi āyiṉa.	(cū. vi. pak. 17)
potup puṇarcci -
irupattunāṉku īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ muṉ varumoḻi mutal ña na ma ya va eṉṉum meykaḷ variṉ iyalpākap puṇarum. taṉikkuṟṟeḻutti aṭutta yakaramey, taṉiyāka niṟkum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya aikāram, no tu eṉṟa uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷ - ivaṟṟu muṉ ña na ma ya va - variṉ, am meykaḷ mikutalumuṇṭu. nilimoḻi īṟṟil ṇa ḷa ṉa la - eṉṟa meykaḷ niṟkumāyiṉ, varumoḻi mutaṟ kaṇuḷḷa nakaram ṇakaramākavum ṉakaramākavum tiriyum.
	e-ṭu	:	viḷa ñāṉṟatu, viḷa nīṇṭatu, viḷa māṇṭatu, viḷa 					yātu, viḷa valitu;
meyñāṉam, meyññāṉam; kinīṭci, kinnīṭci;				no nākā, non nākā; tu nākā, tun nākā					 (ñeḷḷā, māṭā, yavaṉā - eṉpavaṟṟiyum kūṭṭi				 varu meyyeḻuttu mikukka.)
			maṇ, muḷ, poṉ, kal+naṉṟu = maṇṇaṉṟu, 						muṇṇaṉṟu, poṉṉaṉṟu, kaṉṉaṉṟu
(nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum, lakaram karamāka vum 		tiriyum eṉka.)	(naṉ. 158)
potuppeyarkaḷukkum uyartiṇippeyarkaḷukkum īṟāṉa meykaḷ valliṉa mutal moḻi varumiṭattē iyalpām. uyirum yakara rakarameykaḷum ākiya ivvīṟṟu avviruvakip peyarkaḷ muṉṉar varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum ka ca ta pa - eṉṉum valliṉa meykaḷ mikā. uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ cila nāṟkaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattē nili varumoḻikaḷ vikārappaṭuvaṉavum uḷa.
e-ṭu :	cāttaṉ, āṇ + kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ = cāttaṉ kuṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉ ciṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉ ṟīyaṉ, cāttaṉ periyaṉ;āṇ kuṟiyaṉ, āṇ ciṟiyaṉ, āṇ ṭīyaṉ,  āṇ periyaṉ; cāttaṉ, āṇ + ki, cevi, tali, puṟam = cāttaṉki, cāttaṉ cevi, cāttaṉṟali, cāttaṉpuṟam; āṇki, āṇcevi, āṇṭali, āṇpuṟam.
potuppeyar alvaḻi vēṟṟumi iruvaḻiyum iyalpāka muṭintaṉa.
ūraṉ, avaṉ + kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ = ūraṉ kuṟiyaṉ, ūraṉ ciṟiyaṉ, ūraṉ ṟīyaṉ, ūraṉ periyaṉ; avaṉ kuṟiyaṉ, avaṉ ciṟiyaṉ, avaṉ ṟīyaṉ, avaṉ periyaṉ;
	ūraṉ, avaṉ + ki, cevi, tali, puṟam = ūraṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam; avaṉ ki, cevi, tali, puṟam.
uyartiṇippeyar iruvaḻiyum iyalpāka muṭintaṉa.
cātti, tāy +kuṟiyaḷ, ciṟiyaḷ, tīyaḷ, periyaḷ= cātti kuṟiyaḷ, cātti tīyaḷ....., tāy kuṟiyaḷ, tāy tīyaḷ.....
cātti, tāy + ki, cevi, tali puṟam= cāttiki, cātti cevi,..........., tāyki, tāycevi,.............
nampi, cēy + kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ = nampi kuṟiyaṉ, nampi ciṟiyaṉ..., cēy kuṟiyaṉ, cēy ciṟiyaṉ,.....
nampi	+ ki, cevi, tali,  puṟam = nampiki, nampicevi....., cēyki, cēycevi.....
avar, oruvar + kuṟiyar, ciṟiyar, tīyar, periyar = avar kuṟiyar, avar ciṟiyar,............., oruvar kuṟiyar, oruvar ciṟiyar,............ + ki, cevi, tali, puṟam= avarki, avarcevi,.........., oruvarki, oruvarcevi,...........
uyirum yakara rakara meyyum ākiya īṟṟup potuppeyar kaḷum uyartiṇippeyarkaḷum alvaḻi vēṟṟumi eṉa iruvaḻiyilum iyalpāka muṭintaṉa.
virāṭaṉ + aracaṉ =virāṭavaracaṉ; kapilaṉ + paraṇaṉ = kapilaparaṇar; vaṭukaṉ + nātaṉ = vaṭukanātaṉ; aracaṉ + vaḷḷal = aracavaḷḷal - ivi alvaḻip puṇarcci.
ācīvakar + paḷḷi = ācīvakap paḷḷi; kumaraṉ + kōṭṭam = kumara kōṭṭam, kumarak kōṭṭam - ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci
iruvaḻiyum uyartiṇippeyar vikārappaṭṭatu. nili moḻi īṟu keṭutal kāṇka. orōvaḻi varumoḻi mutal valleḻuttu mikutalum kāṇka. varumoḻi mutaleḻuttu mikutal varumoḻic ceyki eṉappaṭum.	(naṉ. 159 caṅ.)
nilimoḻi īṟṟil niṟkum ā ē ō - eṉṉum viṉā iṭiccol muṉṉarum, nilimoḻiyāka niṟkum yā eṉṉum viṉāppeyariṉ muṉṉarum, uyirum meyyumākiya īṟṟiṉiyuṭiya viḷippeyar muṉṉarum vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpu puṇarcciyām.
	e-ṭu	:	uṇkā cāttā? avaṉē koṇṭāṉ? uṇkō  	cāttā? yā kuṟiyaṉa? nampī koḷ, viṭalāy col
(uṇkā - uṇpēṉō eṉṉum poruḷiyuṭiyatu; ākārīṟṟu viṉā)	(naṉ. 160)
uyirum ya ra ḻa - meykaḷum ākiya īṟṟiṉiyuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiyum ēvalviṉiyum, varumoḻi mutal vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum uṟaḻntum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	uṇṭi, uṇṭaṉi, uṇṭāy+ koṟṟā = uṇṭi koṟṟā - mutalāka iyalpāka muṭintaṉa.
			uṇṭīr + koṟṟīr = uṇṭīr koṟṟīr - eṉa iyalpāka muṭintatu.
			muṉṉiliviṉi muṉṉar vaṉkaṇam vantu iyal pāka muṭintavāṟu.
			vā koṟṟā, pāy cāttā, cēr tēvā, vāḻ pulavā - eṉa, uyir ya ra ḻa iṟuti ēvalviṉi muṉ vaṉkaṇam vara iyalpāyiṟṟu
			naṭa koṟṟā, naṭak koṟṟā; ey koṟṟā, eyk koṟṟā; īr koṟṟā, īrk koṟṟā; tāḻ koṟṟā, tāḻk koṟṟā; - eṉa, ivi uṟaḻcci muṭipu.
iyalpātal muṉṉiliviṉikkaṇṇum, uṟaḻtal ēval viṉi cilavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum koḷḷappaṭum.	(naṉ. 161)
poruṭṭaṉmi -
poruṭṭaṉmiyāvatu, oruporuṭkuk kēṭu piṟantālum taṉakkuk kēṭiṉṟit tāṉ oṉṟēyāyp palavakippaṭṭa poruḷtōṟum niṟkum taṉmi.	(naṉ. 287 civañā.)
poruṭpuṇarcci -
vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vēṟṟumiyurupu maṟintu niṟka, avvurupiṉ poruḷ avvurupu maṟintavaḻiyum tiripuṟātu niṟka, vēṟṟumiyurupu tokku niṟkum nilimoḻi, perumpāṉmi varumoḻi mutaleḻuttām vaṉkaṇat toṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum puṇarum puṇarcci.

potuvākap poruṭpuṇarcci eṉpatu aḵṟiṇip peyar kaḷukkē koḷḷappaṭum. tol. uyartiṇippeyar kaḷiyum viravuppeyar kaḷiyum vitantu ōtiyē muṭippār eṉpatu.	(to.e. 153 nac. uri)
urupu tokku niṉṟa poruṭpuṇarcciyē eḻuttatikāra iṟuti iyalkaḷ mūṉṟaṉkaṇṇum perumpālum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷa.	(203 nac. uri)
poruṭpeyarp pakupatam -
kuḻiyaṉ kuḻiyaḷ kuḻiyar kuḻiyatu, kuḻiyaṉa kuḻiyēṉ kuḻiyēm kuḻiyāy kuḻiyīr - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa ‘ipporuḷiṉi uṭiyār’ eṉṉum poruṇmip poruṭ peyarp pakupatam.	(naṉ. 133 mayili.)
porunutal -
oruvar maṟṟoruvar pōla vēṭaṅkoḷḷum porunaratu toḻil porunutal ātaliṉ, aḵtu ottal eṉṉum poruḷuṭiyatu.	(naṉ. 208 caṅkara.)
poruḷ āti āṟum mutal ciṉiyuḷ aṭaṅkutal -
poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉṉum aṟu vakip peyaruḷ, poruḷ mutal mūṉṟum mutalum, ciṉi mutal mūṉṟum ciṉiyumāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku immuṟi vittār eṉka.	(naṉ. 131 mayili., i.vi. 45 uri)
poruḷ teriyā olikaḷ -
kaṭaloli, caṅkoli pōlvaṉa poruḷ pulappaṭa māṭṭāta olikaḷ.	(to.e. 1 nac. uri)
poruḷ varintu icikkum aikāra vēṟṟumi -
ēṉi vēṟṟumikaḷatu poruṭpuṇarcciyatu potumuṭi piṉit tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟākum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi.	(to.e. 157 nac.)
pōli iṭinilimayakkam ākāmi -
pōlikaḷi iṭinili mayakkattiṉ pāṟpaṭuttip poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku uṟuppāki oṉṟu niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu maṟṟoṉṟu atu pōla moḻi nirampa niṟpataṉṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mayaṅki iraṇṭeḻuttum uṭaṉ niṟpatu iṉmiyāṉum, mutal īṟu iṭinilikaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭiyum kūṟik kuṟivaṟuttami yāṉum, ‘uṟaḻā naṭappaṉa’ eṉṟum ‘okkum’ eṉṟum ‘uṟaḻum’ eṉṟum uvamurupu koṭuttu im mūṉṟu cūttiram kūṟuta lāṉum, pōli iṭinili mayakkattiṉpāṟ paṭātu eṉka.		(naṉ. 124 caṅkara.)
pōli aikāra oḷakāram -
a i, ay - eṉpaṉa aikārattukkup pōliyāka varum. aikārac ciṉikkup pōliyāka varum eṉṟu tol. kūṟutaliṉ, ciṉi yeḻuttāvatu moḻiyiṉ mutalil varum eḻuttik kuṟikkāmal iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum varum eḻuttik kuṟittaliṉ, moḻi iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum niṟkum aikāramē ‘ay’ eṉa varum’; moḻi mutaṟkaṇ ‘ai’ aikārattukkup pōliyāka varum.
au eṉpatu oḷakārattukkup pōliyāka varum, oḷakāram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ varumē aṉṟi iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum vārāmiyiṉ, iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum varum aikārat tukku‘ay’ pōliyāvatu pōla, oḷakārattukku ‘av’ pōliyākum eṉṟu tolkāppiṉār kuṟippiṭavilli.
vīracōḻiyamum nēminātamum moḻimūviṭattum aikārat tukku ‘ay’ pōliyākum eṉṟum, oḷakārattukku ‘av’ pōliyākum eṉṟum kūṟiṉa. āyiṉ, “ai-ai,au-oḷa” eṉpataṉi avi kuṟippiṭavilli, 	(e.ā.pak.59,60)
naṉṉūlār ai,ay-eṉpaṉa ai pōlavum, au,av-eṉpaṉa oḷa pōlavum ākum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟkālattu ai, oḷa-eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ay av-eṉṟa pōlikaḷiyum etukikkuk koṇṭaṉar.	(pi.vi.5uri)
pōlimoḻi -
pōlum eṉṉum col itu. iccollil ukaram keṭṭu, eñciya lakāram ṉakāramākip‘pōṉm’eṉac ceyyuḷil īroṟṟu uṭaṉilic collāy varumpōtu īṟṟu makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiriyāy makarak kuṟukkamām. 	(to. e. 51 nac.)
ma
maḵkāṉ puḷḷiyīṟu peṟum cāriyikaḷ -
makarīṟu attuc cāriyiyum iṉ cāriyiyum peṟṟu varumoḻiyākum urupukaḷoṭum peyarkaḷoṭum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	maram+ai ḻūmaram+attu+ai=maratti; maram + attu + iṉ + ai = marattiṉi.
	maram+kōṭu ḻū maram + attu + kōṭu = marattukkōṭu; maram + attu + iṉ + kōṭu = marattukkōṭu.			(to.e. 185, 186 nac.)
makkaḷ eṉṟa potuppeyar puṇarumāṟu -
makkaḷ eṉṟa potuppeyar iruvaḻiyum iyalpātalē yaṉṟic cila viṭaṅkaḷil varumoḻi mutalil vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ḷakaram ṭakaramākat tiritalum urittu.
e-ku:	makkaḷ+tali=makkaṭṭali-vēṟṟumi
	makkaḷ+cuṭṭu=makkaṭcuṭ (to.co. 1) - alvaḻi 		(to.e.404.nac.)
maka eṉṟa peyar puṇarumāṟu -
maka eṉṟa iḷamip peyar iṉcāriyiyum attuc cāriyi yum peṟṟup puṇarum. iṉcāriyi peṟutal perum pāṉmi.
e-ṭu : 	maka+iṉ+ki,ñāṇ,vaṭṭu,āṭi=makaviṉ ki,makaviṉ ñāṇ; makaviṉvaṭṭu, makaviṉāṭi ; maka+attu+ki=makattukki;
	maka+pāl+yāṭu= makappālyāṭu, makampālyāṭu-eṉa valittalum melittalum peṟṟu varutalu muṇṭu.	(to. e. 218, 219 nac. uri)
makara īṟṟu alvaḻip puṇarcci -
makariṟutip peyar alvaḻikkaṇ īṟṟumakaram keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttukku ēṟpa iṉamelleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu	:	maraṅ kuṟitu, marañ ciṟitu,maran tītu, maram peritu-eṉak kāṇka. maram peritu eṉpuḻit tiripuiṉṟu eṉpatu āṇi kūṟalām.	(to. e. 314 nac. uri)
vaṭṭattaṭukku, caturappalaki, āytappuḷḷi, vēḻakkarumpu, nīlakkaṇ-eṉṉum paṇputtokikkaṇ nilimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram keṭṭu varumoḻimutal valleḻuttu mikku muṭintaṉa.
āyta u(vu)lakki, akara mutala - ivi iyalpukaṇattiṉ kaṇ makaram keṭṭu muṭintaṉa.
celluṅ koṟṟaṉ, uṇṇuñ cōṟu; kavaḷamāntu malināṭaṉ, poru māraṉ, tāvu pari, paṟakku nāri, aṭu pōr, varukālam;kollum yāṉi, pāṭum pāṇaṉ - ivi muṟiyē makaram tirintum, keṭṭum, nilipeṟṟum vanta peyareccam (peyareccat toṭar).
kalakkoḷ, kalaccukku, kalattōri kalappayaṟu - ivi aḷavup peyarkkaṇ makaram keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
ellāk kollarum, ellāp pārppārum - eṉa ivi makaram keṭṭu valimikkaṉa.
pavaḷavāy - eṉa uvamattokikkaṇṇum, nilanīr eṉa eṇ ṇiṭattum makaram keṭṭatu.
mara ñāṉṟatu, mara nīṇṭatu, mara māṇṭatu ‘uriyacik kiḷaviyu ñāṅkar’(e. 204 nac.),ataṉkuṇa nutali’ (co. 416)- eṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaram keṭṭatu.	(314 nac.)
maram yātu, maram valitu, maramaṭintatu - eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ makaram keṭātu niṉṟatu.	(to.e. 314)
akam+ ki - kakaram keṭṭu makaramum keṭṭu, melleḻuttu mikku aṅki - eṉa muṭintatu.	- 315
ilam+paṭu =  ilampaṭu - eṉa iyalpākap puṇarntatu - 316
āyiram + oṉṟu = āyirattoṉṟu - eṉa āyiram attup peṟṟatu.	- 317
aḵtu aṭiyaṭutta iṭattum patiṉāyirattoṉṟu - eṉṟāṟ pōla attup peṟṟatu.	- 318
āyiram + kalam, cāṭi = āyirakkalam, āyirac cāṭi - eṉa makaram keṭṭu vali mikkatu.	- 319
num eṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ nīir - eṉat tirintatu.	-326
makara īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar cemmuk kaṭitu, cemmu ñāṉṟatu - eṉṟāṟ pōla ukaram peṟṟatu.	-327
īm, kam, urum - eṉṟa peyarkaḷ ukaram peṟṟu īmukkaṭitu eṉṟāṟ pōla vantaṉa.	- 328
makara īṟṟuc ciṟappu viti -
makara īṟṟu nilimoḻi vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē makarīṟu keṭa, varumoḻi valleḻuttō ataṉ iṉa melleḻuttō mikku muṭiyum. alvaḻikkaṇ uyirum iṭikkaṇamum variṉ, nilimoḻi īṟṟu makaram keṭātu iyalpāka muṭitalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu :	kuḷam+ kari= kuḷakkari, kuḷaṅkari - vēṟṟumi
			kuḷam +aḻakitu, yātu =	kuḷamaḻakitu, kuḷam yātu - alvaḻi	(naṉ. 220)
num tam em nam - eṉpavaṟṟu īṟṟu makaram varumoḻi mutalil ñakaramō nakaramō varumiṭattu avvam meyyākat tiriyum.
num, tam, em, nam +ñāṇ = nuññāṇ, taññāṇ, eññāṇ, naññāṇ
+ nāṇ = nunnāṇ, tannāṇ,ennāṇ, nannāṇ
varumoḻi mutalil makaram varumiṭattu nilimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram iyalpākap puṇarum.
num +maṇi = nummaṇi; piṟavum koḷka. 	(naṉ. 221)
akam eṉṉum nilimoḻi muṉṉar varumoḻiyākac cevi - ki - eṉpaṉa variṉ, akaṅki - akañcevi - eṉap potu vitiyāl muṭitalē aṉṟi, nilimoḻiyiṭiyēyuḷḷa kakara uyirmey keṭa, am+ cevi = añcevi, am+ki = aṅki - eṉa muṭiyum.
añcevi, aṅki - eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇappōliyāyt taḻāat toṭarām.
akam+ciṟi = añciṟi - eṉap puṇarttalum  īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum.	(naṉ. 222 caṅ.)
īm, kam, urum - eṉpaṉa, toḻiṟpeyar pōla, yakaram allāta ēṉiya meykaḷ varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉ ukarac cāriyi peṟum. īmum kammum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukarac cāriyiyē aṉṟi akaraccāriyiyum peṟum.
e-ṭu :		īmuk kaṭitu, īmu nīṇṭatu, īmu valitu; kammuk kaṭitu, kammu nīṇṭatu, kammu valitu; urumuk kaṭitu, urumu nīṇṭatu, urumu valitu - ivi alvaḻi.
	īmukkaṭumi, īmunīṭci, īmuvaṉmi; kammukkaṭumi, kammunīṭci, kammuvaṉmi; urumukkaṭumi, urumu nīṭci, urumuvaṉmi - ivi vēṟṟumi.
īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam- vēṟṟumiyil akaraccāriyip pēṟu.
(īmattukkuriya kuṭam, kammiyaratu toḻilāl camitta kuṭam - eṉap poruḷ ceyka.)	(naṉ. 223)
makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarp puṇarcci -
makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar āṉcāriyi peṟuvataṟku muṉ attuc cāriyiyum peṟṟu varumoḻi viṉi nāṟkaṇat toṭum puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	maka+ attu+ āṉ - makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, makat tāṉ ñāṟṟiṉāṉ, makattāṉ vantāṉ, makattā ṉaṭintāṉ
itaṟku ēḻaṉurupu virittu makattiṉkaṇ eṉṟu poruḷ ceyyappaṭum.	(to.e.331 nac.)
makara īṟṟil aminta nāḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ mirukacīriṭam, puṉarpūcam, pūcam, āyiliyam, makam, pūram, uttaram, attam, vicākam, aṉuṭam, mūlam, pūrāṭam, uttarāṭam, tiru vōṇam, aviṭṭam, catayam eṉap patiṉāṟām. 
ippeyarkaḷ nilimoḻikaḷāka varumoḻikkaṇ viṉic colvariṉ, iṭiyē attuccāriyiyum āṉcāriyiyum varum. varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ, āṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākum.
makara īṟṟup potuviti -
makarīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, iṟuti makaram keṭṭu viti uyirīṟāy niṉṟu, iyalpu uyirīṟu pōl, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ uyirvariṉ uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ avvalliṉamey mikkum, meṉ kaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum; vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē keṭātu vanta valliṉattukku iṉamāṉa melliṉa meyyākat tiritalum ām.
e-ṭu :	vaṭṭam +āḻi ḻū vaṭṭa +āḻi ḻū vaṭṭa+ v  +āḻi = vaṭṭavāḻi; vaṭṭam + kaṭal ḻū  vaṭṭa +kaṭal ḻū vaṭṭa+k + kaṭal = vaṭṭakkaṭal; vaṭṭam + nēmi ḻū vaṭṭa + nēmi = vaṭṭanēmi; vaṭṭam + vāri ḻū vaṭṭa + vāri = vaṭṭavāri
	ivi alvaḻip puṇarcci
			maram+ aṭi ḻū  mara + aṭiḻū mara + v + aṭi = maravaṭi; maram + kāl ḻū mara + kāl ḻū mara + k  +  kāl = marakkāl; maram+  nār ḻū mara + nār = maranār; maram + vēr ḻū mara + vēr = maravēr
	ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci.
			nām + kaṭiyam= nāṅ kaṭiyam ; aṭum + kaḷiṟu= aṭuṅ kaḷiṟu - alvaḻi; nam + ki = naṅki  - vēṟṟumi.	(naṉ. 219)
makarīṟṟu vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ, makara īṟu keṭa varumoḻi mutalil vanta valloṟṟu iṭiyē mikum.
	e-ṭu:	marakkōṭu, marappū, muṇṭakak kōti
iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyartiṇippeyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷḷappaṭum.
	e-ṭu:	marañāṇ, maranūl - makaram keṭṭatu. ivi nāṉkaṉtoki. maramaṇi, marayāḻ, maravaṭṭu, maravural - makaram keṭṭatu. naṅki, eṅki, taṅki - nam em tam - eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ makaram keṭṭu iṉa melleḻuttu mikkatu.	(to .e. 310 nac.)
varumoḻiyil akaramum ākāramum mutalil varumiṭattu makara oṟṟuk keṭṭu īṟṟil niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum nīṭāmiyum uriyatu. ākāram akaramākiviṭum.
	e-ṭu:maram +aṭi= marāaṭi, maravaṭi; kuḷam + āmpal = kuḷāampal,kuḷavāmpal	(to.e.311 nac.)
	kōṇam +kōṇam =kōṇākōṇam; kōṇam + vaṭṭam =  kōṇā vaṭṭam
ivi ēḻaṉtoki.
makara īṟṟup peyar makaram keṭutaloṭu valimeli mikutalum urittu.
	e-ṭu :	kuḷakkari, kuḷaṅkari - valimeli uṟaḻvu.
			kuḷattuk koṇṭāṉ, īḻattuc ceṉṟāṉ, kuṭattu vāy, pilattuvāy - eṉa makaram keṭṭu attup peṟutalumuṇṭu.
			‘pulam pukkaṉaṉē, ‘kalam peṟu kaṇṇuḷar’ - eṉa iyalpātalu muṇṭu.	(312 nac. uri)
illam eṉṟa marappeyar makaram keṭṭu melleḻuttup peṟum.
	e-ṭu:	illaṅkōṭu, illañcetiḷ	(312 nac. uri)
	tām yām nām - eṉpaṉa tam em nam - eṉa mutal kuṟuki īṟu keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikum.
	e-ṭu	:	taṅki, eṅki, naṅki, tañcevi... tantali.....
ellārum ellīrum eṉpaṉavum ellārtaṅkiyum, ellīrnuṅkiyum - eṉa muṭiyum. iḵtu ellārtam maṇiyum eṉpuḻi makaram keṭātu ummup peṟṟatu. ellīrnummaṇiyum eṉpuḻiyum atu.
tamakāṇam, numakāṇam, emakāṇam - eṉa akaraccāriyi peṟutalum, num eṉpatum nuṅki, nuñcevi - eṉṟāṟ pōla makaram keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.	(320, 325 nac. uri)
ellām eṉpatu, ellānaṅkiyum ellānañceviyum - eṉa makaram keṭṭu nammuccāriyi īṟu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukku ēṟpa iṉamelleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum. (324 nac. uri)
īm kam - eṉpaṉa vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇ akkuccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam	(329 nac.)
toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ, vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭik kaṇattu vakaramum vantuḻi ukaramum, yakaramum uyirum vantuḻi iyalpum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	tummuk kaṭitu; tummu nīṇṭatu,tummu valitu, tum yātu, tum mitu.	(327 nac.)
			(taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭutal iyalpām.)
			nāṭṭakkaṭumi, āṭṭakkaṭumi - toḻiṟpeyar makaram keṭṭu	valleḻuttu mikkatu.(327 nac. uri)
nāṭpeyarkaḷ attum āṉum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu 	:	makam+ attu+āṉ+ koṇṭāṉ = makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ (makam eṉṟa nāḷiṉkaṇ koṇṭāṉ - eṉpatu poruḷ.)	(331 nac.)
makarak kuṟukkam -
pattuc cārpeḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ tirinta ṉakara ṇakara meykaḷi aṭuttu varum makaram taṉ iyalpāṉa arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiriyāka olikkum. nilimoḻi īṟṟu makara meyyiṉ muṉṉar vakara mutal moḻi varumāyiṉum avvirumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ nilimoḻi īṟṟu makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiriyām.ivvāṟu iṭavakiyāl makarakkuṟukkam mūṉṟāyiṟṟu.
	varumāṟu :	‘ticiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm’ (pōlum ḻū pōlm = pōṉm) ‘mayiliyal mātar maruṇm’ (maruḷum ḻū maruṇm= maruṇm)
	tarum vaḷavaṉ (ceyyumeṉṉum vāypāṭṭatu nilimoḻi eṉka.)	(naṉ.96)
makarak kuṟukkam cārpeḻuttu ākāmi -
‘ariyaḷavu kuṟukal’ eṉpataṉ poruḷ, (makaram) taṉ iyaṟki (ari) māttiriyiṉ ariyaḷavākak kuṟukutal eṉpatu.	(e.ku.pak. 22)
vēṟōr eḻuttiṉatu ōciyāl makaram kuṟukutal. makaram kuṟukutal iyaṟṟamiḻukkum uriyatātaliṉ, itu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal ākātu. cārpeḻuttāvatu maṟṟoru mutaleḻuttic cārntu ataṉ piṟappiṭamē taṉ piṟappiṭamākak koḷva tām. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam - eṉṟa mūṉṟumē an niliyiṉa ātaliṉ, tamakkeṉat taṉip piṟappiṭatti yuṭiya uyirmey mutaliyaṉa cārpeḻuttākā. (e.ku. pak. 23)
makarakkuṟukkam cārpeḻuttu ātal -
(arimāttiri makaramey kālmāttiriyāka olikkum) aḷavu kuṟukalāṉa makarakkuṟukkam cārpeḻuttu eṉa meyyiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.	(i. vi. 22 uri)
makarakkuṟukkam piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal ākāmi -
‘makarakkuṟukkam iciyiṭaṉ arukum’ (to. e. 13). eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘iciyiṭaṉ’ eṉpataṟku vēṟōr eḻuttiṉ ōciyiṉkaṇ’ eṉpatē poruḷ. pērāciriyar ici nūliṉkaṇ eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu, itaṉip piṟaṉkōṭkūṟal eṉṉum uttikku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi ṉār. āciriyar icinūliṭattiṉkaṇ - eṉat teḷivākak kūṟāmiyāṉum, makarakkuṟukkam iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ kaṇ  varuva tōr ilakkaṇam ākiyāṉum, makarakkuṟukkam paṟṟiya ceyti piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal ākātu.	(e.ā. pak. 19)
makarak kuṟukka varivaṭivam -
ovvōr eḻuttum peṟṟa māttiriyip pātiyākka, ataṉ mēl puḷḷiyiṭuvatu paṇṭi vaḻakkam, paṇṭik kālattil ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷukkum ekara okaraṅkaḷukkum vari vaṭivu oṉṟē. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiliruntu ekara okaraṅkaḷip pirittuk kāṭṭa, avaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivumēl puḷḷiyiṭap paṭṭaṉa.
e - neṭil:iru māttiri;  e)- kuṟil : oru māttiri
o - neṭil:iru māttiri;  o)- kuṟil : oru māttiri
ka - oru māttiri;  k - ari māttiri
naku - orumāttiri;   nāku) - ari māttiri
m - ari māttiri; m¶ - kāl māttiri
ivvāṟu, makarakkuṟukkam varivaṭivil mēlē peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷi peṟṟatu. “makaram kuṟukik kālmāttiriyāy uṭpuḷḷi peṟum” eṉṟu vīracōḻiya uriyāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  (cantip. 19)	(e.ā.pak.20)
makaram  ta va ya ātal -
paṇpāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ iṭiyē niṉṟa makaram tirintu takara vakara  yakaramākavum peṟum eṉṟār nēmināta āciriyar.
varalāṟu: cemmi eṉa niṟutti āmpal eṉa varuvittu, ‘āṅku uyirmey pōm’ eṉpataṉāl makara aikāratti aḻittu, ‘makaram tavaya ām’ eṉpataṉāl  makara oṟṟit takaramākki, mutal uyiri nīṭṭi, ‘cemmi uyir ēṟum ceṟintu’ eṉpataṉāl takara oṟṟilē uyiri (ā)ēṟṟic cētāmpal - eṉa muṭikka.
cemmi eṉa niṟutti alari - āṭi - eṉa varuvittu, makara aikāratti aḻittu, muṉniṉṟa makaroṟṟi vakaram ākkik ‘kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭum’ eṉpataṉāl vakara oṟṟi iraṭṭittu, ‘oṟṟuṇṭēl cemmi uyir ēṟum ceṟintu’ eṉpataṉāl vakara oṟṟil uyiri ēṟṟic cevvalari- cevvāṭi - eṉa muṭikka. 
aimmi eṉa niṟutti ari eṉa varuvittu, makara aikārattik keṭuttu, makara oṟṟi yakaram ākki yakara oṟṟil  uyiri (a) ēṟṟi, aiyari - eṉa muṭikka.
paṇpīṟṟu nilimoḻiyil makaram takara vakara yakaram āvatu varumoḻikku mutalāka uyirvariṉ - eṉa aṟika.
(nēmi. eḻut. 18 uri)
makaram mayaṅkāta ṉakara īṟu oṉpatu eṉṟal poruntāmi -
‘makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa’	(to. moḻimarapu 49)
eṉṟu ekiṉ - cekiṉ - eyiṉ - vayiṉ - kuyiṉ- aḻaṉ- puḻaṉ- pulāṉ- kaṭāṉ- eṉa varum oṉpatum mayaṅkātaṉa eṉak koḷḷiṉ, paliyaṉ - valiyaṉ- vayāṉ - kayavaṉ- alavaṉ- kalavaṉ- kaluḻaṉ - maṟiyavaṉ- cekilaṉ- mutalāyiṉavum mayaṅkappeṟā - eṉa maṟukka.	(naṉ. 121 mayili.)
immayakkam ēṟpuḻikkōṭalāṉ kuṟiliṇiyi aṭutta ṉakaram paṟṟiyatē. 	(ila. cūṟā. pa. 65.)
makaram ṉakarattōṭu ottal -
‘makara iṟuti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ pāl pakā aḵṟiṇippeyar eṉpatu peṟṟām. makaram ṉakarattōṭu ottalāvatu, peya riṟutikkaṇ makaram niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu ṉakaram niṟpiṉum vēṟṟumi iṉṟi ottal.
	e-ṭu	:	‘akaṉ amarntu....mukaṉ amarntu’ (kuṟaḷ 84)				(naṉ. 122 caṅkara.)
makaram ṉakaramōṭu uṟaḻātu naṭappaṉa -
‘uṟaḻā naṭappaṉa uḷavē’ eṉavē, uṟaḻātaṉa perum pāṉmiyavām. avi vaṭṭam paṭṭam kuṭṭam māṭam kūṭam kaṭām paṭām kaṭakam caṭakam nukam makam āram pūram uttaram vīkkam nōkkam ūkkam - eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa.			(naṉ. 121 mayili.)
uṟaḻātaṉa perumpāla eṉka. avi vaṭṭam, kuṭṭam, māṭam, kūṭam- mutalāyiṉa.	(naṉ. 122 caṅkara.)
makaṉ, makaḷ, makkaḷ: tiṇiviḷakkam -
makaṉ, makaḷ, makkaḷ - eṉpaṉa makkaṭkatiyiluḷḷāri uṇartti niṟpiṉ, uyartiṇip peyarkaḷām; muṟiyi uṇartti niṟpiṉ potuppeyarkaḷām.	(naṉ. 158 mayili.)
‘makaṉ viṉi’ kiḷattal -
tāy eṉṉum col taṉakku aṭiyāy muṉvanta makaṉatu viṉiyip piṉṉāka oruvaṉ kūṟumiṭattu, yakara īṟṟup puṇarcciyāy, valleḻuttu varumoḻi mutalil vantuḻi, avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu:	makaṉ ṟāyk kalām - makaṉ tāyoṭu kalāytta kalām
			makaṉ ṟāycceru - makaṉ tāyoṭu ceyta ceru
			makaṉ ṟāyt tuṟattal - makaṉ tāyit tuṟattal
			makaṉ ṟāyp pakittal - makaṉ tāyip pakittal
- eṉa vēṟṟumivaḻip poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum.
viṉi: īṇṭup pakimēṟṟu. nac.	(to. e. 359 nac.)
makaṉviṉi - makaṟkut tāyāṉ payaṉpaṭu nilimiyaṉṟi ava ḷoṭu pakitta nilimiyik kuṟittal.	(360 iḷa. uri)

matam
matam eṉpatu maṉattiṉ koḷki. nūl taḻuviya mataṅkaḷ palavā yirukkat talimi nōkki matam ēḻu eṉpatu nūlvaḻakku.	(naṉ. 11 irāmā.)
marappeyarp puṇarcci ai vaki -
a)	melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum 
(e-ṭu: viḷaṅkōṭu, ataṅkōṭu, māṅkōṭu)
ā)	valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum 
(e-ṭu: palāakkāy, attikkāy, puṉṉikkāy)
i)	ammuccāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum 
(e-ṭu: puḷiyaṅkōṭu, ariyaṅkōṭu, tēkkaṅkōṭu)
ī) 	orukāl melleḻuttup peṟṟum, orukāl val leḻuttup peṟṟum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum
	(e-ṭu: yāaṅ kōṭu, yāakkōṭu; piṭāaṅ kōṭu; piṭāakkōṭu; taḷāaṅkōṭu, taḷāak kōṭu)
 	orukāl melleḻuttup peṟṟum orukāl ammup peṟṟum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum
	(e-ṭu: utiṅkōṭu, utiyaṅkōṭu: oṭuṅkōṭu, oṭuvaṅkōṭu)
u)	orukāl ammup peṟṟum, orukāl ammup peṟātu valleḻuttup peṟṟum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum 
	(e-ṭu: puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal, puṉṉikkāṉal; mulliyan toṭiyal, mullit toṭiyal )-
eṉa marappeyarp puṇarcci aivakiyām. 			(i.vi.eḻut. 83 uri)
marapu eṉpataṉ oruporuṭkiḷavikaḷ -
ilakkaṇam, muṟimi, taṉmi - eṉpaṉa marapu eṉpataṉōṭu oru poruṭkiḷavikaḷ.	(to. e. 1 nac. uri)
maram allāta ekiṉmuṉ valliṉam -
ekiṉ eṉṟa peyar aṉṉappaṟaviyik kuṟikkumvaḻi vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē iyalpāta lum, iruvaḻiyum akarac cāriyi porunta valleḻuttō iṉamelleḻuttō mikutalum ām.
e-ṭu :	ekiṉkāl, ekiṉcevi, 	ekiṉṟali, ekiṉpuṟam eṉa vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil iyalpu. ekiṉappuḷ, ekiṉampuḷ eṉa alvaḻiyil akaram maruva vali meli mikkaṉa. ekiṉakkāl, ekiṉaṅkāl eṉa vēṟṟumiyil akaram maruva valimeli mikkaṉa. ekiṉa māṭci, ekiṉa vāḻkki, ekiṉa vaḻaku eṉa vēṟṟumiyil piṟakaṇam variṉum akaraccāriyi maruviṟṟu.	(naṉ. 215)
marāaṭi: collamippu -
maram+ aṭi ḻū mara + aṭi ḻū marāṭi  ḻū marāaṭi.
makariṟuti keṭa, mara aṭi eṉa nilimoḻi iṟutiyum varumoḻi mutalumākiya iraṇṭu akaraṅkaḷ ākāramāki ‘marāṭi’ eṉṟu muṭiya, varumoḻi aṭi eṉṟa col eṉpa taṉi aṟivikka aṟikuṟiyāka akaram iṭappaṭa, marāaṭi eṉṟāyiṟṟu.	(e. ā. pak. 151)


maruviṉ tokutiyum mayaṅkiyal moḻiyum -
vēṟṟumi mutalāṉa poruḷpaṭac coṟkaḷ tokkut tirintu oru collāy maruvi niṟkum coṟkaḷum, iṭam māṟit tirintu niṟkum coṟkaḷum eṉa ivi. ivvirutiṟac coṟkaḷumpuṇarniliyik karutumiṭattu, niṟutta collum kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyumāy niṉṟu puṇartaṟ kuriyaṉa. iḻiciṉar vaḻakkum piḻipaṭucoṟkaḷum kavar poruḷpaṭu vaṉavum avvāṟu niṉṟu puṇarappeṟā.
	e-ṭu:cōṇāṭu, malāṭu, teṉātu, vaṭātu - ivi marūu moḻikaḷ.
	mīkaṇ, muṉṟil, nuṉinā, puṟanakar - ivi mayaṅkiyal moḻikaḷ.
	vanticci, pōccu, ākaccē, taṅkacci mutaliyaṉa	- ivi iḻiciṉar vaḻakku.
cōḻaṉnāṭu, maliyamāṉnāṭu, teṟkiṉkaṇṇatu, vaṭakkiṉ kaṇṇatu - eṉpaṉa muṟiyē cōṇāṭu mutaliya vākat tirintu oru collāy maruviniṉṟaṉa. kaṇmī, ilmuṉ, nānuṉi, nakarppuṟam - eṉpaṉa muṟiyē mīkaṇ mutaliyavākac coṟkaḷ piṉmuṉ iṭamāṟi niṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 111 ca. pāla.)
maruviṉ pātti -
marumuṭipiṉ pakuti. marumuṭipu ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maru, ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntā maru - eṉa irupakutip paṭum.
yāvar eṉṉum palarpāl paṭarkkip peyar iṭiyē vakaram keṭṭu uyartiṇi muppāṟkum potuvāṉa ‘yār’ eṉṟa viṉikkuṟippup pōla vaṭivu koṇṭu varutal, yātu eṉṉum aḵṟiṇi orumi viṉāppeyar iṭiyē vakara uyirmey vara ‘yāvatu’ eṉa varutal - pōlvaṉa ulaka vaḻak kiṉum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṉum maruvi vanta ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maruvām. muṉ + il = muṉṟil, mēl + kaṇ  = mīkaṇ- mutaliyaṉavum ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maruvām.
arumaruntaṉṉa - arumanta, cōḻaṉāṭu- cōṇāṭu, āṟṟūr - āṟi mutaliyaṉa ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntā maru.			(to. e. 172, 250, 355; 483 nac. uri)
marūuc coṟkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ puṇarumpōtu iṭam māṟum.
	e-ṭu	:	nuṉinā, muṉṟil	(e. ku. pak. 118)
marūu, mayaṅkiyal moḻi taḻāttoṭar -
maruviya coṟkaḷum, mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum puṇarum nilimikkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷavām.
	e-ṭu:	muṉṟil, mīkaṇ (ivi marūu);
		‘teyva māl vari’ (mayaṅkiyal moḻi)
ivi nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷ taḻāat toṭarākap puṇarntaṉa.	(mu. vī. puṇa. 5)
marūumoḻi -
‘maruviṉ pātti’ kāṇka.
maḻi eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
maḻi eṉṟa aikāra īṟṟuc col, vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi alvaḻip puṇarccikkaṇ iyalpākavum, vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ attum iṉṉum peṟṟum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	maḻi kaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu, peritu  - alvaḻi; maḻiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ, maḻiyiṟ ceṉṟāṉ -vēṟṟumi; maḻiyiṉkaṇ ceṉṟāṉ -  eṉpatu poruḷ.			(to. e. 287 nac.)
maṉ:puṇarumāṟu -
maṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟu iṭiccol, vēṟṟumip puṇarcci pōl, īṟṟu ṉakaram ṟakaramāyttirintu vaṉkaṇat toṭu puṇarum. e-ṭu: ‘atumaṟ koṇkaṉ tērē’			(to. e. 333 nac.)
mā eṉṟa peyarccol puṇarumāṟu -
mā eṉṟa peyar vilaṅkukaḷiyum māmarattiyum uṇarttum. iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻiyum iyalpām.
e-ṭu :	mā +kuṟitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu = mā kuṟitu, mā ciṟitu, mā tītu, mā peritu,
vēṟṟumikkaṇ, mā eṉpatu maramāyiṉ, akara eḻuttup pēṟaḷapeṭiyum iṉamelleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum; vilaṅkik kuṟikkumāyiṉ ṉakarac cāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu:	māantaḷir, māaṅkōṭu; māṉkōṭu
	māṅkōṭu eṉa, māmarattik kuṟikkum col akaram peṟātu melleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu; māviṉ kōṭu - eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉcāriyi peṟutalumām.   	(to.e.231 nac. uri)
māttiri -
iḵtu oru kālaaḷaviṉ peyarām. iyalpākak kaṇṇi imittal nēramum, kiyi noṭittal nēramum oru māttiriyām. ki noṭittaliṉkaṇ, niṉitta aḷavil kālmāttiriyum, ki noṭittaṟkuk kaṭṭivirali naṭu viraloṭu cērtta aḷavil arimāttiriyum, avviru viralkaḷiyum muṟukkum aḷavil mukkālmāttiriyum, viṭuttu olitta aḷavil orumāttiriyum ākiya kālam kaḻiyum eṉpar.
eḻuttoliyi māttiri eṉṟa kālaaḷavu koṇṭu kaṇakkiṭuvar.	(to. e. 7 nac.  mu. vī. eḻut. 98)
māttiri aḷavukaḷ -
kaṇṇimittal nēramum kinoṭittal nēramum oru māttiri nērattip potuvākak kuṟippaṉa. ōr akaṅ kikku mēl nāṉku aṅkulam iṭikiṭappa maṟṟōr akaṅkiyi vittuk koṇṭu mellavum viriyavum iṉṟi aṭittal, viritalum nīṭṭittalu miṉṟi muḻaṅ kālik kiyāl cuṟṟutal - mutaliyaṉavum oru māttiri aḷaviṉa. kuruvi kūvutal oru māttirikkum, kākam karital iraṇṭu māttirikkum, mayil akavutal mūṉṟu māttirik kum, kīriyiṉ kural ari māttirikkum aḷavu. nōyillāta iḷiyōṉ kuṟṟeḻuttiṉik kuṟinta aḷavil ettuṇi nēram olippāṉō attuṇi nēram oru māttiri eṉappaṭum.	(e. ā. pak. 18)
māttiri aḷavukaḷ paṟṟiya virivāṉa aṭṭavaṇi -
aṟuvaki ilakkaṇa nūlār ā ī ū ē ō - eṉpaṉa neṭil eṉṟum, ai oḷa- eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum taṉittaṉi oṉṟari māttiri peṟuvaṉa eṉṟum, ākavē avaṟṟik kuṟil neṭil eṉṟum koḷvar.



nūlkaḷ

  viḷakkam	1	2	1	1	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅū	ṅu	ṅu	3	1
