māttirikku imi, noṭi īraḷavu kōṭal -
kaṭpulaṉākiya imikkālamum, cevippulaṉākiya noṭik kālamum karutik kōṭaṟku iraṇṭu ōtiṉār.			(naṉ. 99 mayili.)

mār, al, ai vikutikaḷ -
‘kāṇaṉmār emar’ eṉa mārīṟu etirmaṟi viyaṅkō ḷiṭattum, ṭu tu ṟu - eṉpaṉa oṉṟaṉ paṭarkkiyiṭattum, alvikuti ‘makaṉeṉal makkaṭ pataṭieṉal’ (kuṟaḷ 196) eṉa viyaṅkōḷ etirmaṟiyilum uṭaṉpāṭṭilum, ai īṟu ‘añcāmi añcuva toṉṟiṉ’ eṉa viyaṅkōḷ etirmaṟiyilum varum.	(naṉ. 140 irāmā.)
‘māṉam illi’ -
ittoṭar tolkāppiyattil payila vaḻaṅkukiṟatu ‘māṉam’ eṉṟa iccol vaḻaṅkappaṭumiṭattu muṉmoḻi perum pāṉmiyum makaramey īṟṟatāy uḷḷatu. cantiyil makaram keṭa ‘mikiṉu māṉa milli’ eṉṟē toṭar amikiṟatu. māṉam eṉṟum āṉam eṉṟum appuṇarmoḻiyil collip pirikkalām. iṉampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiya rum māṉam eṉpatē collākakkoṇṭu ‘māṉamilli’ eṉṟa toṭarkkuk kuṟṟamilli’ eṉṟu poruḷ ceytaṉar.		(to.e. 199, 271 nac. mutaliyaṉa.)
‘āṉam’ eṉpatē collākak koṇṭu (āṉam - hāṉam) ‘kuṟṟam’ eṉṟu karuttuk koḷvār cilar. (e.ku.pak.190) āyiṉ, to.col. 111āmnūṟpāvil (cēṉā.) ippirippup poruntāmi kāṇka.
māṉam illi - varivu (nīkkutal) illi - eṉpar cilar.
māṉam kuṟṟam eṉṟa poruḷil ‘māṉamilli maṟṟavaṉ māṭṭeṉa’ peruṅkati -ai : 47: 225 eṉṟa toṭaril vantuḷḷatu.
mikkup puṇarum puṇarcciyiṉ iruvaki -
mikkup puṇarum puṇarcci, eḻuttu mikutalum, cāriyi mikutalum eṉa iru vakittu.
	e-ṭu :	viḷa+kōṭu = viḷaṅkōṭu - ṅakaramey mikkatu.
			maka+ ki = makaviṉki - iṉcāriyi mikkatu.  
(to.e. 112 nac.)
mikaṟki -
mikaṟki - mikutal; iyalpākap puṇara vēṇṭiya iṭattil valleḻuttu mikutalākiya nili tōṉṟutal.
tāy eṉpatu vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi iyalpākap puṇarum eṉṟavitikku māṟāka iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyil,  tāy+ koli = tāyk koli - eṉṟu mikutali ‘mikaṟki’ eṉṟār.	(to.e.157. nac.)
miṉ piṉ paṉ kaṉ:puṇarcci -
miṉ mutaliya innāṉku coṟkaḷum, toḻiṟpeyar pōla, yakaram nīṅkiya piṟameykaḷ varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉ ukarac cāriyi peṟum. kaṉ eṉpatoṉṟum akaraccāriyi peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttum iṉamelleḻuttum mikku uṟaḻum.
	e-ṭu:	miṉṉuk kaṭitu, miṉṉu nīṇṭatu, miṉṉu valitu; miṉṉuk kaṭumi, miṉṉunīṭci, miṉṉuvalimi - eṉa alvaḻi vēṟṟumi iruvaḻiyum ukaraccāriyi peṟṟatu. ēṉiya mūṉṟoṭum ivvāṟē porunta ōṭṭuka. kaṉṉuk kaṭitu, kaṉṉuk kaṭumi - mēṟkūṟiya potuvāṉa muṭipu. kaṉṉat taṭṭu, kaṉṉan taṭṭu - ‘kaṉ’ akaram peṟṟu melliṉattōṭu  uṟaḻntatu.
			(kaṉ - ciṟutarācut taṭṭu)	(naṉ. 217)
miṉ: puṇarumāṟu -
miṉ eṉṟa col miṉṉutal toḻiliyum miṉṉaliyum kuṟikkum. iḵtu alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumiyilum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum, yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpum, uyir varuvaḻi īṟṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	miṉṉuk kaṭitu; miṉṉu naṉṟu,  miṉṉu valitu, miṉ yātu; miṉṉaritu -alvaḻi. miṉṉukkaṭumi; miṉṉu naṉmi, miṉyāppu, miṉṉuvalimi; miṉṉarumi - vēṟṟumi	(to.e.345 nac.)
‘mī eṉa marīiya iṭam vari kiḷavi’ -
mī eṉpatu iyalpāṉa col aṉṟu; mēṟku eṉṟa col mī eṉa   marīiyiṟṟu eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
mēl eṉṟa col īṟṟumey keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāka maruvutal iyalpu ātaliṉ, mēl eṉpatu mī eṉa maruviṟṟu eṉpatu ciṟakkum.	(to.po.pak. 742 pērā.)
iṭam varital - mēliṭatti variyaṟuttuc cuṭṭutal.			(e.ā.pak. 142 to.e. 250 nac.)
mīkaṇ, mīkkūṟṟu, mīkkōḷ, mīntōl col muṭivu -
mīkaṇ eṉpatu kaṇṇiṉatu mēliṭam eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟkumēṉum, āṟaṉurupiṉ payaṉiliyām mī eṉṉum varumoḻi nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu valleḻuttu mikātu puṇarntamiyiṉ ilakkaṇap pōliyāy alvaḻiyāyiṟṟu.
mīkkūṟṟu eṉpatu pukaḻ. atu mēlāya collāṉ piṟanta pukaḻ eṉṉum mēmpāṭu eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ, paṇput tokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittoki.
mīkkōḷ eṉpatu mēṟpōrvi. atu yākkiyiṉmēl koḷḷutaliyuṭiya pōrvi - eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumittokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit toki. īṇṭu mī eṉṟatu iṭapporuḷōṭu ēḻām vēṟṟumiyurupiṉ poruḷ paṭa niṉṟatēṉum, kaṇāti urupu vēṇṭāmiyiṉ, vēṟṟumit toki yāyiṟṟu.
mīntōl eṉpatu mēṟṟōl. atu mēlāya tōl - eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ paṇputtoki. iḵtu ik kālattup pīntōl eṉa marīiyiṟṟu.	(naṉ. 178 caṅkara.)
mī : puṇarumāṟu -
mī eṉpatu mēl eṉṟa colliṉ marūu. atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkum, varumoḻi valleḻuttukku iṉamāṉa mel leḻuttup peṟṟum puṇarum. iḵtu alvaḻi muṭivu.		 	(e.ā. pak. 142)
	e-ṭu :	mīkaṇ, mīcevi, mītali, mīpuṟam;mīkkōḷ, mīppal;
			mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl - mēlākiya kaṇ mutalākap poruḷ ceyka.
mīkaṇ eṉpataṟku mēliṭattukkaṇ eṉṟu vēṟṟumip poruḷpaṭa uri kūṟuvar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉi yarum. kaṇ eṉpataṉi iṭappeyarākak koṇṭu mī ākiya kaṇ - mēliṭam- eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar.			(e.ku.pak. 229)
mīṉ puṇarumāṟu -
mīṉ eṉṟa col alvaḻip puṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum; vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarckikkaṇ valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu	:	mīṉ karitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu : alvaḻi; mīṉkaṇ, mīṟkaṇ; mīṉciṉi, mīṟciṉi; mīṉṟali, mīṟṟali; mīṉpuṟam, mīṟpuṟam - vēṟṟumi uṟaḻcci muṭipu.				(to.e.339 nac.)
‘mīṉ’ vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇartal -
mīṉ eṉṉum nilimoḻi īṟṟu ṉakaram vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē ṟakarattōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum; alvaḻikkaṇ  iyalpupuṇarcciyām.
	e-ṭu	:	mīṉ + kaṇ =mīṟkaṇ, mīṉkaṇ; mīṉ +cevi = mīṟcevi, mīṉcevi; mīṟṟali, mīṉṟali;mīṟpuṟam, mīṉpuṟam - eṉpaṉavum kāṇka.
			mīṉ kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu.	(naṉ. 213)
muṭiviṭam kūṟal -
āciriyaṉ tāṉ collum ilakkaṇattiṟku vitiyuḷḷa iṭattic collutal muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉṉum utti. ‘laḷīṟṟu iyipiṉām āytam aḵkum’ eṉṉum ic cūttiram, ‘kuṟilvaḻi laḷa-ttav vaṇiyiṉ āytam, ākavum peṟūum alvaḻi yāṉē’ (naṉ. 228) eṉṉum cūttiratti nōkkik kūṟaliṉ, muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉṉum uttiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.			(naṉ. 97 caṅkara.)
muttuvīriya eḻuttatikārac ceytikaḷ -
innūl eḻuttatikāram eḻuttiyal, moḻiyiyal, puṇariyal- eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalkaḷiyuṭiyatu. atikārat taṟciṟappup pāyiram nīṅkalāka ivaṟṟil muṟiyē 115, 45, 298 āka 458 nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. eḻuttiyalil naṉṉūliṉ eḻuttiyaṟ ceyti curukkiyum virittum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eḻuttu, uyirmey, mayakkam- mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pariyāyap peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. aḷapeṭi eṭṭuvakippaṭum eṉak kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. moḻiyiyaliṉ toṭakkattil ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ, peyar viṉi eṉṟa pakuppu, moḻi eḻu vakippaṭum eṉṟa piṟarkūṟṟu - eṉpaṉa kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷa.  vaṭamoḻi eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻil tirintu vaḻaṅkumāṟum vaṭamoḻit  tīrkka kuṇa virutti cantikaḷum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil tirintu vaḻaṅkumāṟum virittup pēcappaṭṭuḷa. puṇariyalil, potup puṇarcci - urupu puṇarcci - uyirīṟu meyyīṟu kuṟṟiyalukarīṟu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ puṇarcci- yāvum naṉṉūliyum tolkāppiyattiyum oṭṭik kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. kō-mā- eṉpaṉa yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟutal, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyi peṟutal, ḻakaram takaram varuvaḻi ṭakara mātal, ḻakaramāṉatu nakaram varuvaḻi ṇakaramātal - mutaliyaṉa putiyaṉa pukutalāka ippuṇariyaluḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷa.
muttuvīriya  eḻuttiyal cuṭṭum pariyāyap peyarkaḷ -
eḻuttu	-	irēki, vari, poṟi- eṉpaṉa 					(nuṟpāeṇ3)
uyir	-	accu, āvi, curam,pūtam - eṉpaṉa (7)
kuṟṟeḻuttu	-	kuṟumi, iraccuvam, kuṟil - eṉpaṉa (9)
neṭṭeḻuttu	-	neṭumi, tīrkkam, neṭil - eṉpaṉa (11)
mey	-	ūmi, uṭal, oṟṟu, kāttiram - eṉpaṉa (13)
valleḻuttu	-	vaṉmi, vaṉkaṇam, vali- eṉpaṉa (15)
melleḻuttu	-	meṉmi, meṉkaṇam, meli- eṉpaṉa (17)
iṭiyeḻuttu	-	iṭimi, iṭikkaṇam, iṭi-eṉpaṉa 				(19)
cārpu	-	pullal, cārtal,puṇartal eṉpaṉa (23)
āytam	-	 aḵkēṉam, taṉinili - eṉpaṉa (28)
cuṭṭu	-	kāṭṭal, kuṟittal - eṉpaṉa (29)
viṉā	-	viṉaval, kaṭāval - eṉpaṉa (30)
aḷapeṭi	-	aḷapu, pulutam- eṉpaṉa (33)
iṭinili 		caṅkam, puṇarcci, ciyōkam, pullal,
mey mayakkam	ṉū	kalattal, mayakkam, iṭinili eṉpaṉa (66)	māttiri	-	maṭṭu, aḷavu, mitam, vari - eṉpaṉa (97)
muttuvīriya eḻuttiyaluḷ kāṇappaṭum vicēṭak kuṟippukkaḷ cila -
melleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭam tali (eḻut. 43). eṭuttalum paṭuttalum eṉa ōci iruvakittu (59). nakaram ‘porun’ eṉa oru coṟkaṇṇēyē iṟutiyāka varum (90). kinnoṭi yākiya māttiri uṉṉukiṟa kālattuk kāl māttiri, virali ūṉṟukiṉṟa kālattu ari, viralkaḷi muṟukkukiṟa kālattu mukkāl, muṟukkiyavaṟṟi ōcippaṭa viṭukkiṟa kālattu oṉṟu- eṉa muṟiyē amiyumāṟu (98)- iṉṉōraṉṉa ceytikaḷ piṟa ilakkaṇa nūlil kāṇappaṭāvāy in nūṟkē vicēṭamāṉa kuṟippukkaḷām.
muttuvīriyap puṇariyaluḷ kāṇappaṭum cila ciṟappuc ceytikaḷ -
taḻāt toṭarkaḷi ‘mayaṅkiyal moḻi’ eṉṟu kuṟikkum mu.vī. tolkāppiya nūṟpāvi oṭṭiyatu. aviyum nili varumoḻikaḷāka niṉṟu puṇartaṟkuriyaṉa.
	e-ṭu: teyva māl vari’ (‘māl teyva vari’ eṉka.)(5)
	ā,mā,kō- eṉṟu peyarkaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟutalumām.
	e-ṭu : 	āviṉi, māviṉi, kōviṉi	(16)
	kō,mā, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ yakara uṭampaṭumey varutalumām.
varumāṟu: kōyil, māyiru ñālam	 (25)
eṇkaḷ ellām iṉcāriyi peṟum.
	e-ṭu :	oṉṟiṉi, nāṉkiṉi	(69)
	nilā eṉṉum peyar iṉcāriyi peṟum. (tol. kūṟuvatu vēṟu. 228 nac.) varumāṟu.: nilāviṉ kānti	(93)
irā eṉṉum peyarkku iṉcāriyi illi (tol. kūṟuvatu vēṟu. 227. nac.)	(94)
ḻakaram vēṟṟumikkaṇ takara nakaraṅkaḷ varumiṭattē, muṟiyē ṭakara ṇakaramākat tiriyum. (ippuṇarppu vīracōḻiyattuḷ kaṇṭatu)
	e-ṭu :kīḻ+ tici = kīṭṭici; cōḻa + nāṭu = cōṇāṭu (210, 211)
muttuvīriyam -
19ām nuṟṟāṇṭil muttuvīra upāttiyāyar eṉpavarāl, tolkāppiyam naṉṉūl mutaliyavaṟṟil ariyavākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷi eḷimippaṭuttik kūṟuvataṟkāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa aintilakkaṇa nul muttuvīriyam. iḵtu eḻuttu col poruḷ yāppu aṇi - eṉṉum aimpirivukaḷi yuṭiyatu. avaṟṟuḷ eḻuttatikāram, eḻuttiyal moḻi yiyal puṇariyal - eṉṉum muppakuppiṉatu. collatikāram, peyariyal viṉiyiyal oḻipiyal- eṉṉum muppiriviṟṟu. poruḷatikāram, akavoḻukka iyal kaḷavoḻukka iyal kaṟpoḻukka iyal - eṉṉum muppālatu. yāppatikāram, uṟuppiyal ceyyuḷiyal oḻipiyal eṉṟa mūviyaliṉatu. iṉi eñciya aṇiyatikāram, collaṇiyiyal poruḷaṇiyiyal ceyyuḷaṇiyiyal - eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalkaḷāṉ iyaṉṟatu. innūṟku uri varintavar tirunelvēlit tiruppāṟkaṭal nātaṉ kavirāyar eṉṟu karutukiṟōm. aintu atikāraṅ kaḷilum muṟiyē 458, 310, 92, 266, 159, āka 1285 nuṟpākkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aintu atikāraṅkaḷilum uḷḷa taṟciṟappup pāyira nuṟpākkaḷiyum kūṭṭa 1290nuṟpākkaḷām.
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum aḷapeṭi vakikaḷ -
iyaṟkiyaḷapeṭi	-	aḻittal, vilikūṟal, 					pulampal - ivaṟṟuḷ varuvatu
ceyaṟki 	-	ceyyuḷil cīrtaḷi keṭṭa 					viṭattup pulavaṉ koḷvatu.
iṉṉici yaḷapeṭi	-	‘keṭuppatūum’ 							eṭuppatūum’ (kuṟaḷ 15)
collici yaḷapeṭi	-	taḷii (taḷintu eṉpatu 					tirintu aḷapeṭuttatu.)
neṭilaḷapeṭi	-	taṉi neṭṭeḻuttu 						aḷapeṭuppatu : āa
kuṟilaḷapeṭi	-	kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭilāki 					aḷapeṭuppatu:
		paḻūup pal
oṟṟaḷapeṭi	-	kaṇṇ
eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭi	-	uvāap patiṉāṉku

muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum tattitam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ -
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum ikara ēkāraṅkaḷ aikāram ākum.
	e-ṭu :	kiriyil uḷḷaṉa kirikam: vērattāl varuvatu viram (vēram - kōpam; viram - pakimi)
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum ukara ūkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ oḷakāram ākum. e-ṭu: kurukulattil vantavar kuravar; cūraṉmakaṉ curi; cōmaṉ putalvaṉ cumiyaṉ.
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum akaram ākāram ākum. e-ṭu: caṉakaṉ makaḷ cāṉaki; tacarataṉ putalvaṉ tācarati.	(moḻi. 43 - 45)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum ‘pēcuṅ kiḷavi’ -
‘pēcuṅ kiḷavi’ yākiya vaḻaṅkum col caṅkatam, pākatam, caṉukkirakam, avappirañcaṉam - eṉa nālvakippaṭum. in nāṉkaṉuḷ, caṅkatamum caṉukkirakamum tēvarmoḻi yākum. avappirañcaṉam eṉpatu iḻintōratu moḻi. ēṉiya pākatam eṉpatu nāṭukaḷellām vaḻaṅkum moḻi.
pākatam taṟpavam eṉavum, taṟcamam eṉavum, tēcikam eṉavum mūvakippaṭum.  āriyac ciṟappeḻuttāṉum, potuvum ciṟappumākiya iraṇṭeḻuttāṉum tamiḻil citintu varuvatu taṟpavamām. e-ṭu : cuki, pōki; araṉ, ari.
āriyattiṟkum tamiḻiṟkum potuveḻuttālākiya moḻi taṟcamamām. e-ṭu : amalam, kāraṇam, kamalam.
tēcikam eṉpatu ticiccol ākum. e-ṭu: tāyit taḷḷi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, tantiyi accaṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum kāṇka.	(moḻi. 26-33)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum moḻiyiṭi eḻutteṇṇikki -
moḻi ōreḻuttu mutalāka oṉpatu eḻuttu iṟutiyākat toṭarntu varum. (pakupatam, pakāppatam - eṉa naṉṉūl patattip pākupaṭuttiyami pōla mu.vī. pākupaṭuttilatu-)
e-ṭu: kā, aṇi, aṟam, akalam, aruppam, ampalavaṉ, araṅkattāṉ, uttarāṭattāṉ, uttaraṭṭāti yāṉ - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.	(moḻi. 9)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum moḻivaki -
muttuvīriyam ēḻ moḻivakikaḷic cuṭṭukiṟatu. avi varumāṟu:
nilam, nīr 	-	pirikkappaṭāta taṉimoḻi
tēraṉ, ūraṉ	-	pirikkappaṭum iṇimoḻi
aracar vantār	-	toṭarntu varum tuṇimoḻi
naṅki, vēṅki	-	taṉimoḻiyum,(nam+ki, vēm+ki 				-eṉat) toṭarmoḻiyum ākum 					potumoḻi
cantiraṉ	-	orumiyik kāṭṭum taṇamoḻi
muṉivarkaḷ	-	paṉmiyik kāṭṭum kaṇamoḻi
āṇ, peṇ	-	ivi irutiṇiyilum kalantu 					potuvāka  varum kalappuṟu moḻi				(moḻi. 8)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum vaṭamoḻic cantikaḷ -
muttuvīriyam, tīrkkacanti kuṇacanti virutticanti- ākiya mūvaki vaṭamoḻic cantikaḷik kuṟikkiṟatu. nilimoḻi yum varumoḻiyum vaṭacollāka amiyumiṭattē ivi nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
a)	nilimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalilum a ā varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa,iṭiyilōr ākāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu :pata + ampuyam = patāmpuyam; cēnā + atipati =  cēnātipati
ā)		nilimoḻiyīṟṟil i ī niṟka varumoḻi mutalilum i ī  varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyil ōr īkāram tōṉṟum,
	e-ṭu : maki + intiraṉ = makīntiraṉ; purī +  īcaṉ = purīcaṉ.
i)	nilimoḻiyīṟṟil u ū niṟka, varumoḻi mutalilum uū varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyil ōr ūkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu: kuru + utayam =  kurūtayam; cuyampū +  ūrccitam = cuyampūrccitam. 
ivi mūṉṟum tīrkka cantiyām.
a)		nilimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil i ī varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyē ōr ēkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu:	nara + intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; umā + īcaṉ = umēcaṉ
ā)		nilimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil u ū varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyē ōr ōkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu:	tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; kaṅkā+ ūrmi = kaṅkōrmi.
ivi iraṇṭum kuṇacantiyām.
a)		nilimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil ē ai varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyē ōr aikāram tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu:	civa + ēkanātaṉ = civikanātaṉ; makā + aicu variyam = makicuvariyam.
ā)		nilimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil ō oḷa varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭiyē ōr oḷakāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu : kalaca + ōtaṉam = kalacutaṉam; makā + oḷaṭatam = makuṭatam.	(moḻi. 34-41)
ivi iraṇṭum virutticantiyām.
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyākkam -
āriya uyireḻuttup patiṉāṟaṉuḷ naṭuvil niṟkum nāṉkum iṟutiyil niṟkum iraṇṭum allāta ēṉiya paṉṉiraṇṭum, āriya meyyeḻuttu muppattēḻaṉuḷ kakaram mutal aintu varukkaṅkaḷiṉ mutalil niṟkum ka ca ṭa ta pa - eṉpaṉavum avaṟṟiṉ iṟutiyil niṟkum ṅa ña ṇa na ma - eṉpaṉavum ya ra la va ḷa - eṉpaṉavum ākiya patiṉintum āriyam tamiḻ eṉṉum irumoḻikkum potuveḻuttukkaḷām. iṉi, āriyat tiṟkē ciṟappāṉa eḻuttuḷ, mēṟakūṟiyavāṟu iṭi yilum iṟutiyilum niṟkum uyir āṟum, potu nīṅkalāka eñciya mey irupattiraṇṭum iṭam peṟum.
āriyattiṟkuriya ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻil vaṭacollāku miṭattē tiriyap peṟum. avi tiriyumāṟu:
	8ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭiyil ya eṉavum tiriyum. 30ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭiyil ya eṉavum tiriyum. 31ām mey yeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭiyil ṭa eṉavum tiriyum. 32ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭiyil ta eṉavum tiriyum. 33ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil a eṉavum iṭiyil ka eṉavum tiriyum. 35ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ka eṉavum iṭiyil kk eṉavum tiriyum.			(moḻi. 11 - 20)
ākāra  iṟutip peyar aikāramākavum, īkāra iṟutippeyar ikaramākavum tiriyap peṟum. e-ṭu :	mālā- māli; purī - puri.
piṟavum naṉṉūlārip piṉpaṟṟiyē moḻintuḷḷavāṟu kāṇka.	(moḻi. 23-26)
tattitam paṟṟiya kuṟippu ‘muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum tattitam’ eṉpataṉkaṇ kāṇka.
mutal īreṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varuṅkāl puṇarcci -
oṉṟu iraṇṭu- eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutal moḻi variṉ, oṉṟu iraṇṭu- eṉpaṉa oru iru- eṉat tirintu niṉṟa niliyil ukaram keṭa, or ir - eṉṟāki, mutal nīṇṭu ōr īr eṉat tirintu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu:	oṉṟu + akal = ōrakal, oṉṟu + uḻakku = ōruḻakku: iraṇṭu +akal= īrakal, iraṇṭu+ uḻakku= īruḻakku	(to.e.455nac.)
mutal eḻuttu -
moḻi tōṉṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa aṭippaṭi eḻuttuk kaḷākiya uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum. iṉi, orucolliṉ mutalil varum eḻuttu mutaleḻuttu eṉavum, mutaṉili eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
e-ṭu :	‘cuṭṭumutal ākiya ikara iṟuti’ - atōḷi 				(to.e.159 nac.)
	‘ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟuti’ -etōḷi 	(e. 159)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal ukaram’ - atu 	(e. 176)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal ākiya ai eṉ iṟuti’ - avi 	(e. 177)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal vakaram’ - av 	(e. 183)
	‘mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalum urittē’ - mūvuḻakku (e. 457)
	‘āṟueṉ kiḷavi mutal nī ṭummē’ - āṟakal 	(e. 458)
	‘akam eṉ kiḷavikku... mutalnili oḻiya’ - aṅki 				(e. 315)
mutal, cārpu: toki vaki viri -
mutaleḻuttiṟku ‘mutal’ eṉṟatu tokiyākavum, ‘uyirum uṭampum’ eṉṟatu vakiyākavum, ‘muppatu’ eṉṟatuviriyāka vum; cārpeḻuttiṟkuc ‘cārpu’ eṉṟatu tokiyākavum. ‘pattum’ eṉṟatu vakiyākavum, ‘oṉṟoḻi munnūṟṟeḻu pāṉ’ eṉṟatu viriyākavum;
ivvirutiṟattu eḻuttiṟkum ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṟatu toki yākavum, ‘mutalcārpu’ eṉṟatu vakiyākavum, ivviru tiṟattu eḻuttiṉ viriyum kūṭṭi ‘oṉṟoḻi nāṉūṟu’ eṉpatu viriyākavum koḷka.	(naṉ. 61. irāmā.)


mutalnili viṉippeyar -
tiri nuri ali taḷir - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa viṉimutaṟ poruṇmi yuṇarttum ikaravikuti puṇarntu keṭṭu, mutaṉili māttiriyāy niṉṟu viṉippeyar ātal paṟṟi, mutaṉili viṉippeyar- mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar - eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum.(cū.vi.pak. 33)
mutal, vaḻi, cārpu: viḷakkam -
mutaṉūlukku vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum oruvaṟku mintaṉum marumāṉum pōlum eṉka. oru nūl taṉakku vaḻinūli nōkkiṉ mutaṉūlākavum, mutaṉūli nōkkiṉ vaḻinūlāka vum, ayalnūli nōkkac cārpunūlākavum niṟkum. eṉavē, mutaṉūl māttiriyāy niṟpatu iṟivaṉnūlum, vaḻinūl māttiriyāy niṟpatu iṟuti nūlum aṉṟi, iṭiniṟkum nūlkaḷ ellām oruvaṟku mintaṉāyiṉāṉ maṟṟavaṟkut tanti āyiṉāṟpōla, mutaṉūlāyum vaḻinūlāyum niṟkum.		(naṉ. 8 caṅkara.)
‘mutalā ēṉa tampeyar mutalu’tal -
uyiroṭu cērntu moḻikku mutalākum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭṭaṉa allāta, moḻikku mutalākāta meykaḷum tammik kuṟippiṭumiṭattu moḻikku mutalil varum. moḻikku mutalākāta meykaḷ ṅ ṭ ṇr l ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ - eṉṟa oṉpatum ām.
	varumāṟu:	‘laṉeṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar’ (to.e. 149 nac.); ‘ṭakāram ākum’(e. 302); ‘ṇakāra iṟuti (e. 302); ‘ṉakāra iṟuti’ - (e. 332); ‘ḷakāra iṟuti’ (e. 396) ‘rakāra ḻakāramāy’ (e. 95 nac.); ‘ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ’ (e. 66, 94)
mutaleḻuttiṉ toki vaki viri -
mutaleḻuttu - eṉat tokiyāṉ oṉṟum, uyireḻuttu uṭampeḻuttu - eṉa vakiyāṉ iraṇṭum, uyir paṉṉiraṇ ṭum uṭampu patiṉeṭṭum eṉa viriyāṉ muppatum ām mutaleḻuttu.	(naṉ. 58 mayili.)
mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭappiṟappum muyaṟcippiṟappum -
uyirum iṭiyiṉa meyyum miṭaṟum, melliṉamey mūkkum, valliṉam mārpum iṭamākak koṇṭu piṟakkum. iḵtu iṭap piṟappu. muyaṟcippiṟappu varumāṟu:
a, ā - aṅkāttalāṉum, i, ī, e, ē, ai - aṅkāppoṭu, mēlvāyppalaṭiyi nāviḷimpu uṟutalāṉum, u., ū, o, ō, oḷa - aṅkāppōṭu, itaḻ kuvitalāṉum piṟakkum. (aṅkāppiṉip piṟavaṟṟukkum koḷka.)
k, ṅ - nāviṉaṭi mēlvāyaṭiyi uṟutalāṉum, c,ñ - nāviṉ naṭu mēlvāynaṭuvi uṟutalāṉum, ṭ, ṇ - nāviṉ nuṉi mēlvāynuṉiyi uṟutalāṉum, t, n - muṉvāyppal aṭiyi nānuṉi uṟutalāṉum, p, m - kīḻmēl utaṭukaḷ uṟutalāṉum, y - nāviṉaṭi mēlvāyiṉ aṭiyi naṉṟāka uṟutalāṉum, r, ḻ - nāviṉ nuṉi mēlvāyi varuṭutalāṉum, l - muṉvāyppal aṭiyi nāviḷimpu vīṅki oṟṟutalāṉum, ḷ - mēlvāyi nāviḷimpu vīṅki varuṭutalāṉum, v - kīḻutaṭu mēṟpalli uṟutalāṉum, ṟ, ṉ - mēlvāyi nāviṉ nuṉi mika uṟutalāṉum piṟakkum.
(uṟutal - poruntutal; varuṭutal - taṭavutal; oṟṟutal - taṭṭutal)	(naṉ. 75 - 86)
mutaleḻuttu, cārpeḻuttu : peyarkkāraṇam -
tāmē tamittu niṟkiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu eṉṟāyiṉa. aviyē tammoṭu tām cārntum, iṭam cārntum, iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum cārntum vikārattāṉ varutaliṉ cārpeḻuttu eṉṟāyiṉa.	(naṉ.60 mayili.)
mutaṟpōli cila -
‘maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu’ - ‘maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu’. ‘poṉyātta tār’ - ‘poṉñātta tār’ eṉa ‘yā’niṉṟa iṭattu ‘ñā’ niṟpiṉum amiyum eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
‘ṇaṉeṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
viṉiyō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar’	(to.e.146 nac.)			(naṉ. 124 irāmā.)
mutaṉili iṟutinili iṭinilikaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭi -
eḻuttukkaḷ tam peyaric colli nilimoḻi varumoḻi kaḷāyp puṇarumiṭattē, tamakkuk kūṟappaṭṭa vitiyik kaṭantu, moḻi mutalākā eḻuttukkaḷ mutalākiyum moḻi yiṭi mayaṅka lākā eḻuttukkaḷ mayaṅkiyum iyalum.
	e-ṭu:‘avaṟṟuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavum tōṉṟum’
(to. e. 24 nac.)
itaṉkaṇ, taṉ peyari moḻitaliṉ lakaram moḻimuta lāyum ‘avaṟṟuḷ’ eṉpataṉ īṟṟu ḷakarattoṭu mayaṅkiyum vantavāṟu.
‘kep peritu’ eṉpuḻi’ eṉpuḻi, ‘ke’ taṉ peyar moḻi taliṉ ‘ekaram meyyōṭu ēlātu’ eṉṟa viti yiṟantu meyyōṭu īṟāy niṉṟavāṟu.	(naṉ. 121)
mutaṉili kālam kāṭṭal -
toṭṭāṉ, viṭṭāṉ, uṟṟāṉ, peṟṟāṉ, pukkāṉ, naṭṭāṉ- eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ iṭinili iṉṟi mutaṉili vikāramāy iṟanta kālam kāṭṭiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟiyē toṭu viṭu uṟu peṟu puku naku - eṉpaṉa mutaṉilikaḷ.			(naṉ. 145 caṅkara.)
mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar, ēval viṉi puṇarumāṟu -
yaraḻa-kkaḷi oḻinta eṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkaḷiyum (ñ ṇ n m l v  ḷ ṉ) iṟutiyākavuṭiya mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyarkaḷum ēvalviṉikaḷum, varumoḻiyāka yakaramey oḻinta piṟameykaḷi (k c t p ñ n m v) mutalākavuṭiya coṟkaḷ varumāyiṉ, perumpāṉmiyum ukaraccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum. cila ēvalviṉikaḷ avvukarac cāriyi poruntā.
ivveṭṭu meyyīṟṟu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum ēvalviṉikaḷum muṟiyē uriñ, uṇ, porun, tirum, vel, vav, tuḷ, tiṉ - eṉpaṉa. ka ca ta pa mutaliya eṭṭu meykaḷi mutalākak koṇṭa varumoḻi muṟiyē kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu, eṉpavaṟṟi mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyaroṭu puṇarkka iṭiyē ukaraccāriyi nikaḻntu muṭiyumāṟu kāṇka.
uriñuk kaṭitu, uriñuc ciṟitu, uriñut tītu, uriñup peritu,
uriñu ñāṉṟatu, uriñu nīṇṭatu, uriñu māṇṭatu, uriñu valitu - eṉavarum.
uṇ, porun - mutaliyavaṟṟukku ukaraccāriyi koṭuttu muṭikka. vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻic cāriyip pēṟṟiṉiaṭuttu valleḻuttu mikutalum, piṟakaṇam varuvaḻi ukarap pēṟ ṟōṭu iyalpāka muṭitalum koḷka.
iṉi, ivveṭṭu ēvalviṉikaḷum nilimoḻiyāka niṟpa, ka ca ta pa mutaliya eṭṭumeykaḷi mutalākavuṭiya varu moḻikaḷ muṟiyē koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā, ñeḷḷā, nākā, māṭā, vaḷavā - eṉak koṇṭu cāriyip pēṟṟiṉit  tantu muṭikka. vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattu valimiku tal illi.
	varumāṟu:	uriñu koṟṟā, uriñu cāttā, uriñu tēvā, uriñu pūtā; uriñu ñeḷḷā, uriñu nākā, uriñu māṭā, uriñu vaḷavā.
ivvāṟē uṇ, porun - mutaliyavaṟṟukku ukaraccāriyi koṭuttu muṭikka.
ukaraccāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum varum ēvalviṉikaḷ ṇ ṉ l ḷ eṉṉum nāṉkumey īṟṟaṉavām. ēṉiya nāṉku īṟukaḷum ukaram peṟṟē varum e-ṭu: uṇ koṟṟā, tiṉ cāttā, vel pūtā, tuḷ vaḷavā - ivi ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa.	(naṉ. 207)
‘muppāṟ puḷḷi’ (1) -
mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟāya āytam. (to. e.2. iḷa., nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam - eṉṟa puḷḷi peṟum eḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum.	(cū.vi.pak. 26; to.po. (cū. 665 : 6) pak. 735 pērā.)(e.ā.pak. 13; e.ku. pak. 10,11)
“āytam eṉṟu ōcitāṉ aṭuppukkūṭṭup pōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu” eṉpatu uṇarttutaṟku ‘āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum’ eṉṟār. itaṟku vaṭivu  kūṟiṉār,  ēṉi oṟṟukkaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōcivikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟu ātaliṉ. itaṉip puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu eṉavē, ēṉiya eḻuttukkaḷ ellām varivaṭiviṉa ātal peṟappaṭum. (to.e. 2 nac. uri)
muppāṟ puḷḷi (2) -
āytam eṉṉum muppāṟ puḷḷi. ‘puḷḷi’ īṇṭu oliyik kuṟikkum. ivveḻuttu nā-aṇṇam-mutalāya uṟuppuṟṟu amiyap piṟavāmal, vāyitaḻ aṅkāppa miṭaṟṟiciyāṉ arimāttiri yaḷavoṭu piṟappatu. ātaliṉ atu tāṉ cārntuvarum a i u eṉṉum kuṟṟuyirōcikaḷiṉ cāyalip peṟṟolikkum. (ekara okaraṅkaḷ ikara ukara oliyuḷ aṭaṅkum.) āytam uyir ēṟaliṉṟi yāṇṭum olippōṭu varum paṇpiṉatu eṉpatu viḷaṅka, olikkuṟip puṭiyatākiya itaṉi vitantu ‘āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum’ eṉṟār āciriyar. ‘muppāṟpuḷḷi’ eṉavē, ataṉ varivaṭivum mukkōṇamāka amittuk koḷḷappaṭum. āytam yāṇṭum olippoṭu niṟṟaliṉ, itaṉi uyir ūrvatilli. ari māttiri yaḷaviṟṟu ātaliṉ itu meyyiṉi ūrvatilli. iṅṅaṉam uyirukkum oṟṟukkum iṭippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, uyir pōlavum oṟṟup pōlavum āytam muṟiyē alaku peṟṟum peṟātum varum.			(to. e. 2 ca. pāla.)
muraṇ eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarp puṇarcci -
muraṇ eṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar ēṉiya toḻiṟpeyar pōla ukarac cāriyiyum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇamāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum peṟātu, potuvitippaṭi alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum, vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	: 	muraṇkaṭitu, ñekiḻntatu, yātu, iḻintatu - alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpu. muraṭkaṭumi, muraṇ ñekiḻcci, muraṇvalimi, muraṇaṭivu - eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ṇakaram ṭakara mākat tirintum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpāka vum puṇarntatu.
			muraṇ + nīṭci = muraṇīṭci - eṉa varumoḻi mutal					nakaram tirintavaḻi nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṇakaram 						keṭṭatu.	(to. e. 150 nac.)
			muraṇ + kaṭumi = muraṇkaṭumi, muraṭkaṭumi. 					araṇ+ kaṭumi = araṇkaṭumi, araṭkaṭumi -
eṉṟa uṟaḻcci muṭivum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ uṇṭu.	(to.e. 309 nac.uri)
muṟṟāytam -
taṉakkuriya arimāttiriyik kuṟiyāmal peṟum āytam muṟṟāytamām. atu taṉakku muṉ kuṟṟeḻuttik koṇṭu, taṉakkup piṉṉarp perumpāṉmiyum ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu - eṉṟa āṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu peṟṟu moḻiyiṉ iṭiyil nikaḻum.
aḵku, kaḵcu, oṉpaḵtu - mutalāka īṟṟu valliṉa vakiyāl varum muṟṟāytam āṟām. (kuṟṟiyalukaramē aṉṟi valliṉap puḷḷiyi ūrntu piṟa uyir variṉum aḵtu āytatti aṭuttuvarum uyirmeyyām.  e-ṭu: paḵṟi)
av+ kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya; a +kāṉ = aḵkāṉ - eṉap puṇarcci vakiyāṉ varum muṟṟāytam oṉṟām.
ilaku - ilaḵḵku, vilaki - vilaḵḵki - eṉac ceyyuḷ vikārat tāl (virittal) varum muṟṟāytam oṉṟām.
ivvāṟṟāl muṟṟāytam eṭṭu ātal aṟiyappaṭum.			(naṉ. 90 uri)

muṟṟiyalukaram keṭṭu muṭital -
atu itu utu - eṉpaṉa aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntumvaḻi, ukaram keṭa, ataṉ itaṉ utaṉ - eṉavarum. avi iṉcāriyiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ukaram keṭa, atiṉ itiṉ utiṉ - eṉa varum.	(to. e. 176 nac. uri)
āṟu eṉpatu aṟu eṉa niṉṟavaḻi, varumoḻiyāka ‘āyiram’ variṉ, aṟu  eṉpataṉ ukaram keṭa, aṟ + āyiram = aṟāyiram - eṉa muṭiyum.	(469)
cuṭṭumutalukaramē aṉṟip piṟa ukaramum uyir varuvaḻik keṭutalumuṇṭu. katavaḻakiyatu, kaḷavaḻakiyatu, kaṉavaḻakiyatu- eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṟuti ukaram keṭa niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl varumoḻi mutal akaram ēṟi muṭintavāṟu,	(176 nac.)
ellām eṉpatu vaṟṟum urupum peṟṟu īṟṟil ummi peṟuvaḻi, ellāvaṟṟiyum- ellāvaṟṟoṭum - eṉa varum. ‘oṭu’ eṉpataṉōṭu ‘um’ cērumiṭattu, oṭuviṉ ukaram keṭa ‘oṭum’ eṉa ummoṭu puṇarum.	(189 nac.)
atu +aṉṟu, itu+aṉṟu, utu+ aṉṟu, ataṉṟu, itaṉṟu,  utaṉṟu - eṉa ukaram keṭṭu niṉṟa takara oṟṟōṭu uyir puṇarum.	(258 nac.)
muṟṟukara īṟṟup puṇarcci -
oṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉurupu, āṟaṉurupu, ēḻu eṇṇum iyalpu eṇṇup peyarum oru iru - eṉṟāṟpōlat tirinta eṇṇup peyarum, muṟṟukara īṟṟu viṉippakuti (aṭu, peṟu- pōlvaṉa), atu itu utu eṉṟa cuṭṭuppeyar - ākiyavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟu muṟṟukaram vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	cāttaṉoṭu ceṉṟāṉ - mūṉṟaṉurupu
			eṉatu tali - āṟaṉurupu
			ēḻu kaṭal - iyalpu eṇṇuppeyar
			orukal, irucol - tirinta eṇṇuppeyar
			aṭukaḷiṟu, peṟuporuḷ - muṟṟukara īṟṟu viṉip 					pakuti
			atu ceṉṟatu, itu kaṇṭāṉ - orumic cuṭṭup 					peyar.
ivi iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.
utukāṇ, utukkāṇ - eṉa iyalpāyum vikāramāyum varuvaṉa arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ‘utukkāṇ’ eṉpatu uṅkē eṉṟa poruḷilvarum oṭṭi niṉṟa iṭiccol eṉpārumuḷar. ‘uvakkāṇ’ eṉpatum uṅkē eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum oṭṭi niṉṟa iṭiccol  (kuṟaḷ 1185 pari.)	(naṉ. 179)
muṟṟukaram keṭutal -
nilimoḻiyīṟṟil niṟkum muṟṟiyalukaram varumoḻi mutaṟ kaṇ uyir varuvaḻik keṭutalum uṇṭu. alvaḻi vēṟṟumi - eṉa iruvaḻiyum koḷka.
e-ṭu: uyarvu + iṉitu= uyarviṉitu; katavu + aṭittāṉ = katavaṭittāṉ	(naṉ. 164)
muṉ: puṇarumāṟu -
muṉ eṉṟa col peyarākavum ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccollākavum, kuṟippu viṉi yecca īṟākavum varum. accol vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyal pākavum īṟṟu ṉakaramtirintum, ēṉiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	muṉ koṇṭāṉ, muṟ koṇṭāṉ; muṉ ñāṉ ṟāṉ, muṉ vantāṉ. vaṉkaṇamvariṉ tiritalē perumpāṉmi. uyirvariṉ, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟākiya nilimoḻi ṉakaram iraṭṭum. muṉ + aṭintāṉ= muṉṉaṭintāṉ.	(to. e. 333 nac.)
muṉṟil : collamippu -
muṉ eṉṟa nilimoḻiyi aṭuttu il eṉṟa varumoḻi puṇarumiṭattu, iṭiyil ṉakaram taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟāka iraṭṭi muṉ+ṉ+il=muṉṉil eṉa varutalē muṟi. ataṉiviṭuttu nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaroṟṟi aṭuttu ataṉ iṉamākiya ṟakaroṟṟu vantu cēra, muṉ+ṟ+il =  muṉṟil eṉa varutal ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūumuṭi pām. illattiṉatu muṉṉiṭam (muṟṟam) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum iccol, il+muṉ= iṉmuṉ- eṉa muṟiyē puṇarntu vārāmal muṉpiṉ nilimāṟi muṉṟil eṉṟu vantami marūuviṉpāṟpaṭum. iṅṅaṉam muṉpiṉ tokkaṉa vaṟṟip piṟkālattār ilakkaṇap pōli eṉpar.			(to.e. 355 nac. uri)
itu kaṭikkaṇ, eṉṟāṟpōla varum marūumuṭipu pōlaṉṟi, muṉṉil eṉa oṟṟu iraṭṭi muṭiyaṟpālatu, iru moḻikkum iyalpu ilatōr oṟṟu mikku muṭinta marūumuṭipu. (ṉakarattōṭu iyiyuṭiyatu ṟ.)	(356 iḷa. uri)
‘muṉṉatiṉ ēṉiya muraṇutal’ -
oṉpaḵtu - nilimoḻi;  pattu, nūṟu - varumoḻi
nilimoḻiyākiya eṭṭiṉmēl oṉṟu, oṉpaḵtu  - eṉap paḵtu eṉṉum iṟutiyāyt tirintu niṉṟāṟ pōla, varu moḻiyākiya (eṇpatil mēl) patti ‘nūṟu’ eṉavum, (eṇṇūṟṟiṉ mēl) nūṟṟi ‘āyiram’ eṉavum tirittu- eṉpatu poruḷ. (muṉṉatiṉ - muṉmoḻi pōla;  iṉ: aintaṉurupu)
oṉpaḵtiṉālē pattiyum nuṟṟiyum perukki eṉap poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar. ‘muraṇi’ eṉṟa coṟku atu poruḷaṉṟu ātalāṉum, atu poruḷāmēṉum ataṉāl varumoḻi ivvāṟu tirintatu eṉat tōṉṟāmiyāṉum, tōṉṟiṉ nilimoḻikkum vikāram kūṟa vēṇṭāmi yāṉum atu poruntātu eṉka.	(naṉ. 194 caṅkara.)
‘muṉṉap poruḷa’ ātal -
iraṇṭu vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ, coṟkaḷ puṇarumiṭattu orēvakiyāṉa eḻuttukkaḷāṉ ākiya toṭarkaḷāy varivaṭivil eḻutap paṭukiyil, ittoṭarukku ipporuḷ eṉṟu collu vāṉ kuṟippāṉē amiyum niliyil, avi ‘muṉṉap poruḷa’ eṉappaṭum. aḵtāvatu veḷippaṭiyākat tiripu aṟap poruḷuṇartta iyalātaṉavāyc colluvāṉ kuṟippāṉum attoṭari eṭuttal paṭuttal ōcik kuṟip pāṉumē uṇarappaṭuvaṉa eṉpatu.
cempoṉ + patiṉtoṭi, cempu + oṉpatiṉ toṭi	iraṇṭum ‘cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi’ eṉṟē amivaṉa.
cempu eṉṟa colli eṭuttu olippataṉāṉum, cem poṉ eṉpataṉi eṭuttu olippataṉāṉumē itu poruḷ teriya uṇarappaṭum. mēlum kūṟupavaṉ pēcuvatu cempip paṟṟiyā, poṉṉip paṟṟiyā - eṉpataṉi uṇarntālum teḷivākap poruḷ pulappaṭum.	(to. e. 142 nac. uri)
muṉṉapporuḷa āvaṉa - kuṟippiṉāṉ poruḷ uṇarap paṭum toṭarkaḷ.
muṉṉili moḻikkaṇ iṟutiyil vārā eḻuttukkaḷ -
oḷa eṉṟa uyir, ñ n m v - eṉṟa meykaḷ, kuṟṟiyalukaram- eṉpaṉa muṉṉili ēvalorumi viṉikkaṇ tāmē īṟāka vārā. eṉavē, oḷavum, ñn m v - eṉṉum meykaḷum ukaram peṟṟu muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ īṟāka varum, kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāka nīṇṭu muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ īṟāka varum.
	e-ṭu:	kuvu koṟṟā, kuvuk koṟṟā; uriñu koṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā; porunu koṟṟā, porunuk koṟṟā; tirumu koṟṟā, tirumuk koṟṟā; tevvu koṟṟā, tevvukkoṟṟā; kūṭṭu koṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā - ivvīṟukaḷ āṟum ukaram peṟṟu valliṉam mikāmalum mikkum uṟaḻntaṉa.	(to. e. 152 nac.)
muṉṉili moḻikku oḷa ñ n m v kuṟṟiyalukaram ivaṟṟi vilakkiyataṉ kāraṇam -
muṉṉili orumi vikutikaḷ i ai āy - eṉpaṉa. ikaram nī eṉṟa peyariluḷḷa īkārattiṉ tiripu. āy eṉpatu ai eṉpataṉ nīṇṭa vaṭivē. aiyaṉ eṉṟa peyariṉ mutaṉili ai. oḷavi eṉṟa peyariṉ mutaṉili oḷa. kaṉṉaṭa moḻi yil āṭūu muṉṉiliviṉikaḷil ai-cērntu ‘kēḷvi’ eṉa varum; makaṭūu muṉṉiliviṉikaḷil oḷa- cērntu ‘kēḷ veḷa’ eṉa varum. ivvāṟē tamiḻilum paṇṭik kālattil āṭūu muṉṉiliviṉikkaṇ aiyum, makaṭūu muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ oḷavum cērttu vaḻaṅkiṉar. piṟkālattup paṭarkkikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi muṉṉilik kaṇṇum aḵṟiṇi vaḻakku vantamiyāl avvēṟupāṭu vēṇṭā eṉa oḻikkappaṭṭapōtu, oḷa muṉṉilikkaṇ varutal nīkkappaṭṭatu.
muṉṉilip paṉmikkaṇ ceyyum- irum- eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ēvalil kāṇum umvikuti paṇṭic ceyyuḷil kāṇappaṭa villi. ‘uṇm eṉa irakkum’ (puṟanā. 178) ‘tiṉm eṉat tarutaliṉ’ (150) - eṉa makara īṟṟu muṉṉilic col vantuḷḷaṉa. iccoṟkaḷi nōkka, makaramey paṉmiyik kuṟikkiṟatu. ceykum- varutum- eṉṟa taṉmippaṉmi muṟṟup pōla, uṇm- tiṉm- eṉpaṉa muṉṉilip paṉmi viṉiyik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. centamiḻil nīm eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa makaratti vilakkiyataṉāl, viṉikkaṇṇum uṇm eṉpatu pōla makara īṟu vārātu vilakkappaṭṭatu. oḷavum makaramum tol. kālattu muṉṉarē vilakkappaṭṭaṉa.
moḻiyiṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum muṟṟiyalukaramāka olikkum. avvāṟu olittal muṉṉiliviṉikkaṇṇē eṉpatum, āṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉpatum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu - eṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum eṉṟu itaṉi viḷakkiyuḷḷār eṉpataṉi nōkkak kuṟṟiya lukarattu iṟuti muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ vārātu eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
ñakaram ‘uriñ’ eṉat toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṉkaṇṇum, nakaram ‘verin’ eṉṟa peyarccoṟkaṇṇum ‘porun’ eṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṉkaṇṇum varumātaliṉ  avi viṉikkaṇ vārā. “ñ n- muṉṉili ēvaṟkaṇ varum; āṇṭu avi ukaram peṟum” eṉṟu naṉṉūlār kūṟiyatu pōlat tol. kuṟippiṭāmiyāl, ña na-kkaḷ toḻiṟ peyarkkaṇ aṉṟi muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ vārā.
vakaram av iv uv - eṉṉum cuṭṭukkaḷilum, tev eṉṉum peyarccollilumaṉṟi varutal iṉmiyiṉ, atu muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ vārātu eṉa vilakkappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu oḷa- ñ n m v -  kuṟṟiyalukaram - eṉpaṉa muṉṉili viṉikkaṇ vārā eṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉa.	(e. ā. pak. 115, 116)
muṉṉili moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟātaṉa -
muṉṉili moḻiyāvatu muṉṉiliviṉi. muṉniṉṟāṉ toḻil uṇarttuvaṉavum muṉniṉṟāṉit toḻiṟ paṭuttuvaṉavum - eṉa muṉṉiliviṉi iru vakittu. muṉniṉ ṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavaṟṟit teḷivu karuti ‘ēval viṉi’ eṉṟu kūṟalām. ivvēval viṉi orumikku ellā īṟṟu viṉippakutikaḷum payaṉpaṭalām;eṉṟālum oḷa eṉṉum uyirīṟu, ñ n m v - eṉṉum puḷḷiyīṟu, kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu - eṉpaṉa muṉṉili ēval orumi viṉiyāka vārā. ivi ēval viṉiyāka varavēṇṭumāyiṉ, uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum ukaram peṟṟu varavēṇṭum; kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu muṟṟiyalukara īṟākiviṭum.
	e-ṭu:	kuvu koṟṟā; uriñu koṟṟā, porunu koṟṟā, tirumu koṟṟā, tevvu koṟṟā, kūṭṭu koṟṟā - ivaṟṟuḷ varumoḻi valleḻuttu vikaṟpittu mikutal koḷka.
kūṭṭu eṉpatu ēvalorumi muṟṟākiyavaḻi muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatu.	(to.e.152 nac. uri)
kuṟṟiyalukarīṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkuk kūṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum (iyalpātalē aṉṟi). (153 iḷa.uri)
muṉṉili viṉi, ēval -
muṉṉiliviṉi eṉpatu taṉmiviṉi paṭarkkiviṉi kaṭku iṉamākiya muṉṉili viṉiyik kuṟikkum. iṉamiṉṟi muṉṉili yoṉṟaṟkē uriyatu ēvalviṉi. ākaliṉ muṉ ṉiliviṉi eṉpatu ēvalviṉiyi uṇarttāmiyiṉ ‘ēval’ eṉat taṉiyē ataṉi vitantu kūṟiṉār.			(naṉ. 161 caṅkara.)
mūvakik kuṟikaḷ -
aṭitoṭi mutaliya nōkkit tokukkumvaḻit tokuttal aṉṟi, vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marūuppōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ marūuvāy, aṭippāṭāka orumoḻi mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum kuṟintu varutalum ceyyuḷvikāramām.			(naṉ. 156 civañā.)
mūvakikkuṟi ceyyuḷmarūu aṉmi -
mutal iṭi kaṭik kuṟiyākiya mūṉṟum vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marūup pōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ marūuvāy aṭippāṭāka varum eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvar. atu poruntātu, aṟuvaki vikāraṅkaḷum ceyyuḷ ceyyum cāṉṟōr aḻaku peṟa acceyyuḷil vēṇṭuḻi varuvikka varum eṉpataṉṟē? ati nōkki iccūttiratti māṭṭeṟintār ātalāl, itaṟkum ceyyuṭ ceyyum cāṉṟōr varuvittuḻi varum eṉpatē karuttu. itu karutiyaṉṟē ‘ovvoru moḻi’ eṉpati ‘orumoḻi’ eṉa ivar iccūttiram ceytār? iṉṉum atu poruntātu eṉpataṟkup ‘pacumpuṟ ṟalikāṇ paritu’ (kuṟaḷ 16) eṉpatil kāṇpatu eṉpatu ‘kāṇpu’ eṉak kaṭik kuṟintatum, ‘cāṉṟōr eṉpilar tōḻi’ eṉpatil ‘cāṉṟōr eṉpār ilar’ eṉpatu ‘eṉpilar’ eṉak kaṭik kuṟintatumē cāṉṟātal uṇarka. iṉi, vaḻakkiṭattum ilikkaṟiyi ‘likkaṟi’ eṉavum, ‘nilā utittatu’ eṉpataṟku ‘lā utittatu’ eṉavum, ‘irāppakal’ eṉpataṟku ‘rāppakal’ eṉavum, kēḻvaraku ‘kēvaraku’, nīrccili ‘nīccīli’, pōki ṟāṉ ‘pōṟāṉ’, pāṭukiṟāṉ ‘pāṭuṟāṉ’ eṉavum, ‘āṉi yēṟum perumpaṟiyaṉ’ eṉṉum marapu paṟṟi vanta ‘tōṭṭi yāṉ eṉpatit ‘tōṭṭi’ eṉavum taṇṇīr ‘taṇṇī’ vennīr ‘vennī’ eṉavum muṟiyē immūvaki vikāraṅkaḷum varutal koḷka. 	(naṉ. 156 irāmā.)
mūvakip peyareccak kuṟippu muṉ vali variṉ iyalpātal -
amarmukatta, kaṭuṅkaṇṇa, ciṟiya, periya, uḷa, ila, pala, cila (ivi peyaraṭiyākap piṟanta peyareccakkuṟippu): poṉṉaṉṉa (iḵtu iṭiccollaṭiyākap piṟantatu): kaṭiya (iḵtu uriccollaṭiyākap piṟantatu) - ivaṟṟi nilimoḻiyākavum, kutiri - cennāy -  takar- paṉṟi - eṉpa vaṟṟi varumoḻiyākavum koṇṭu puṇarppavē, iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 167 caṅkara.)
mūvaki meyt tōṟṟattukku uriya vaḷikaḷ -
valliṉam talivaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu; melliṉam mūkku vaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu; iṭiyiṉam miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu. (to.e. 88 nac. uri)
mūvarāvāṉ oru karuttaṉ -
cey eṉṉum ēvalviṉiyiṉ piṉpu vi-pi- eṉṉum iraṇ ṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu variṉ, ceyvi eṉṉum poruḷip peṟum. iviyiraṇṭum oruṅku variṉum, oṉṟē iṇintu variṉum ēvalmēl ēval tōṉṟa, mūvarāvāṉ oru karuttaṉik kāṭṭum. (ēvuvār mūvar; iyaṟṟutal karuttā oruvaṉ.)
	e-ṭu :	naṭappi, varuvi, maṭivi, cīppi, kēṭpi, aḵkuvi-
			ivi ceyvi eṉṉum ēvalviṉip pakāppatam.
			naṭattuvippi, varuvippi, naṭappippi, kaṟpippi - vipi eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum iṇintum oṉṟē iṇin tum vanta ceyvippi eṉṉum ēvalmēl ēval pakāppatam.	(naṉ. 137 mayili.)
mūvaḷapu icittal -
mūṉṟu māttiri aḷavu olittal. itu taṉitta ōreḻut tiṟku iṉṟu. eṉavē, ībḻuttuk kūṭiya iṭattēyē mūṉṟu māttiri peṟum eṉpatu. itaṉāl aḷapeṭi eṉpatu neṭili aṭutta iṉakkuṟilē eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. vaṭa moḻiyil ōreḻuttē aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl mūṉṟu māttiri aḷavu olikkum eṉpatu tamiḻ nūlārkku uṭaṉpāṭiṉmi peṟappaṭum.	(to. e. 5)
mūviṭattum neṭilēḻum aḷapeṭuttal -
vāaki, īiki, ūukam, pēekaṉ, tiiyal, tōoki,  muuval - eṉavum,
paṭāaki, parīikam, kaḻuumaṇi, parēeram vaḷiiyam, purōoci, maṉuukam - eṉavum,
kurāa, kurīi, kuḻūu, vilēe, virii, ulōo, aṉuu - eṉavum muṟiyē moḻi mutal iṭi kaṭi eṉṟa mūviṭat tum ēḻneṭṭeḻuttum aḷapeṭuttaṉa.	(naṉ. 90 mayili.)
mūviṭattu makara īṟṟup peyar alvaḻip puṇarcci -
mūviṭattu makara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ ellārum, ellīrum, tām, nām, yām, ellām - eṉpaṉa. avi varumoḻi vaṉkaṇa māyiṉ īṟṟu makaram iṉamelleḻuttāyt tiriyum.
varumāṟu: ellāruṅkuṟiyar, ellīruṅkuṟiyīr, tāṅkuṟiyar, tāṅ kuṟiya, nāṅ kuṟiyam, yāṅ kuṟiyēm.
meṉkaṇamākiya ñakaramum nakaramum variṉ, ellāruñ ñāṉṟār, ellārun nīṇṭār, ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr, ellīrun nīṇṭīr, tāñ ñāṉṟār, tān nīṇṭār, nāñ ñāṉṟām, nān nīṇṭām, yāñ ñāṉṟām, yān nīṇṭām - eṉa varumoḻi melleḻuttākiya ñakara nakaramāka makaram tiriyum.
iṭiyiṉamum uyirum variṉ, ellārum yāttār, vantār, aṭintār - eṉa makaram iyalpākap puṇarum. ellām eṉpatu vaṉkaṇam variṉ īṟṟu makaram keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarntu iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyi peṟum. ellām+kuṟiyaḻū ellā +kuṟiyaḻū ellāk + kuṟiya+um= ellāk kuṟiyavum. ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ makarīṟu keṭṭu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarntu iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyi peṟum.
ellām+ ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, vaṭṭu, aṭi= ellā ñāṇum, ellānūlum, ellāmaṇiyum, ellāvaṭṭum, ellā vaṭiyum (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)
ellām vāṭiṉa, ellāmāṭiṉa - eṉa iyalpāka muṭitalum koḷka.
ciṟupāṉmi ellām eṉpatu, vali variṉ makaram keṭṭu melimikku iṟutiyil ummup peṟṟu ellāṅ kuṟiyavum, ellāñ ciṟiyavum - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇikkaṇ ellām eṉpatu, ellāk kollarum, ellā nāykarum, ellā vaṇikarum, ellā aracarum - eṉa īṟu keṭṭu, vaṉkaṇattu valimikku ēṉiya kaṇattu iyalpākap puṇarntum,
ellāṅkuṟiyarum, ellāñciṟiyarum- eṉa īṟu keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikkup puṇarntum, ivviru tiṟattum iṟutiyil ummuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭital koḷka.
ellāṅ kuṟiyar, ellāṅ kuṟiyīr, ellāṅ kuṟiyēm - eṉa īṟu keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikku iṟutikkaṇ ummup peṟātu varutalum, ellām vantēm, ellā maṭintēm- eṉa iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum variṉ iyalpākap puṇartalumuḷa.
num eṉpatu, ‘nīir’ eṉat tirintu payaṉili koṇṭu muṭiyum.  e-ṭu : nīir kaṭiyir.	(to.e.320- 323 , 326 nac.)
mūviṭattum makarīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip puṇarcci -
ellārum eṉpatu tammuccāriyiyum, ellīrum eṉpatu nummuccāriyiyum, ellām eṉpatu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ vaṟṟuccāriyiyum uyartiṇikkaṇ nammuccāriyiyum peṟṟuvarum. ellārum ellīrum eṉpavaṟṟu iṟuti ummuc cāriyi pirittu varumoḻikkup piṉṉark kūṭṭappeṟum.
e-ṭu :	ellārtaṅkiyum, ellīrnuṅkiyum - eṉavum ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum, ellānaṅkiyum - eṉavum
tam num nam - eṉpavaṟṟatu  makaratti varumoḻi valleḻut tiṟ kēṟpa iṉamelleḻuttākat tirittum, ellām eṉpataṉ īṟṟu makarattik keṭuttum puṇarkka. iyalpu kaṇam variṉum ic cāriyikaḷ varum.
e-ṭu :	ellārtamyāḻum, vaṭṭum, aṇiyum - eṉavum
		ellīrnumyāḻum, vaṭṭum, aṇiyum - eṉavum
		ellāvaṟṟuyāppum, valiyum, aṭivum - eṉavum
ellānamyāppum, valiyum, aṭivum - eṉavum varumāṟu kāṇka.
tām nām yām - eṉpaṉa tam nam em - eṉṟāki varumoḻik kēṟpa. īṟṟu makaram keṭa, iṉamelleḻuttu mikku,
taṅki, tañcevi, tantali - eṉa varum. (naṅki.... eṅki..... mutalākak koḷka.)
meṉkaṇattu makaramum iṭiyiṉamum uyirum variṉ iyalpāyum, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭiyum muṭitalum koḷka.
	e-ṭu:	taññāṇ, tannūl - īṟṟu makaram keṭa, varumoḻi melloṟṟu iraṭṭutal.
			tammaṇi, tamyāḻ, tamvaṭṭu - iyalpu
			tam + ili (tammili) - taṉikkuṟil muṉ makaram uyirvara iraṭṭutal
			(nam em - eṉpavaṟṟoṭum oṭṭuka.)
tama kāṇam, ema kāṇam, numa kāṇam - eṉa īṟu akarac cāriyi peṟutalumuṇṭu.
nummiṉ makaram vali variṉ keṭṭu melloṟṟu mikkum, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ variṉ avvoṟṟu iraṭṭiyum, makara yakara vakaraṅkaḷ variṉ  iyalpāyum, uyirvariṉ makara oṟṟu iraṭṭiyum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	nuñcevi; nuññāṇ, nunnūl; nummaṇi, num yāḻ, numvaṭṭu; nummāṟṟal.
ellārum ellīrum - eṉpaṉa iṭiyē tam num - cāriyi peṟāmal ellārkiyum ellīrkiyum - eṉṟāṟ pōla varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartalumuṇṭu.			(to. e. 320, 322, 324, 325 nac. uri)
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip puṇarcci: ciṟappu viti -
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi eḻuvāy nilimoḻiyāy niṟpa, av veḻuvāyākiya nilimoḻipporuḷāl ākiṉṟa ceyappāṭṭu viṉiccol varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum valliṉam potuvitiyāl mikku muṭitalē yaṉṟi, uṟaḻcciyum iyalpum ākum. uyirīṟu meyyīṟu- eṉṟa iruvaki  nilimoḻiyum koḷka.  ippuṇarmoḻi mūṉṟāmvēṟṟumittoki.
	e-ṭu:	arāt tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, cuṟāp pāyappaṭṭāṉ - eṉṟu mikku muṭitalē perumpāṉmi.
	pēykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pēykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ;		uṟaḻcci
	pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ  	ṉū
	pēypiṭikkappaṭṭāṉ, pulikaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ	iyalpu.
kōṭpaṭutal eṉpatu mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi eḻuvāyāl oru cāttaṟku ēṟpaṭṭa nili. koḷḷutal mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi eḻuvāyiṉ toḻil.
tam toḻil:	cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi, cāttaṉākiya eḻuvāyiṉ toḻil.
tammiṉākiya toḻil : cāttaṉ puli kaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ eṉpuḻi, ōr eḻuvāyāl cāttaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭa toḻil; aḵtāvatu kaṭikkappaṭutal.	(naṉ. 256)
‘mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttu’ -
mūṉṟi muṭiyilē yiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavē, muppattu mūṉṟākum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
pēreṇṇi muṉṉark kūṟicciṟṟeṇṇi aṭuttuk kūṟutal tamiḻmarapu. ciṟṟeṇṇi muṉṉark kūṟip pēreṇṇip piṉṉark kūṟutal vaṭamoḻi marapu.
‘mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatu’ eṉpatu kāttiyāyaṉar matam. muppattu mūṉṟu: tamiḻmarapu.
moḻikku mutalākum eṉṟa 22 eḻuttukkaḷum, moḻikku īṟākum eṉṟa 24 eḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muppattu mūṉṟil aṭaṅkuvaṉavē.	(to.e.103 nac.)
‘mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru ātal’ -
uyirmey eḻuttil uyirip pirittāl mey taṉṉuṭiya paḻiya vaṭivattip peṟum.
ālili - āl + ili; ataṉi - ataṉ + ai
eṉavē, uyirmey eṉpatu pirikkum niliyil aminta kalappeḻuttām.	(to. e. 139 nac.)
‘mey piṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟki ātal’ -
ṇakara ṉakara īṟṟu meykaḷ nilimoḻi īṟākumiṭattu varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ muṟiyē ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷāy mey piṟitākum (vaṭivu tiriyum)- eṉṟa viti payaṉpaṭuttappaṭa vēṇṭiya iṭattu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyāyiṉ, attiripu peṟātu iyalpākap puṇartal.
	e-ṭu:	maṇ+ kuṭam = maṭkuṭam - mey piṟitu ātal
			poṉ +kuṭam = poṟkuṭam - mey piṟitu ātal
			maṇ + koṇarntāṉ = maṇ koṇarntāṉ
			poṉ + koṇarntāṉ = poṉ koṇarntāṉ - eṉa ṇakara. ṉakara īṟukaḷ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. (to.e. 157 nac.)
mey piṟitu ātal -
itu puṇarcciyil nikaḻum mūvakit tiripukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. mey piṟitu ātal - vaṭivu vēṟupaṭutal.
	e-ṭu	:	maṇ+ kuṭam = maṭkuṭam - ṇakaram ṭakaramāy vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭatu. col + kēṭṭāṉ= coṟ kēṭṭāṉ - lakaram ṟakaramāy vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭatu.
			yāṉ + ai = eṉṉi- ‘yā’ eṉpatu ‘e’eṉa vaṭivu vēṟupaṭa, ṉakaram iraṭṭittatu.
iṅṅaṉam ōreḻuttup piṟitōr eḻuttāyt tiriyum tiri piṉit tol. ‘mey piṟitātal’ eṉṟār.	(to.e. 109 nac.)
‘meymmi yākal’ -
iyalpākap puṇartal. meymmi paṭṭāṅku ātaliṉ, iyalpām. nāy+ kōṭpaṭṭāṉ = nāy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ - itu ‘meymmi yātal’ eṉṉum iyalpu puṇarcci.			(to.e. 156 nac. uri)
meymayakkam -
oru colliṉkaṇṇum puṇarmoḻiyiṉkaṇṇum oru mey taṉṉoṭum piṟameykaḷoṭum kūṭum kūṭṭam. ‘iṭinili meymayakkam’, kāṇka.
meymayakkam : uṭaṉilimayakkam, vēṟṟunili mayakkam -
mey patiṉeṭṭaṉuḷḷum k c t p - eṉṉum nāṉkum oḻittu niṉṟa patiṉālu meyyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟu māṟi vantu oṉṟuvatu (vēṟṟunili) meymayakkamām. rakara ḻakaram oḻittu niṉṟa patiṉāṟu meyyum tam muṉṉart tām vantu oṉṟuvatu uṭaṉili mayakkamām. iccollappaṭṭa iru kūṟṟu muppatu mayakkum moḻiyiṭiyilēyām. uyirmey mayakkam iṉṉataṉ piṉṉar iṉṉatām eṉṟa variyaṟi illi.	(naṉ. 109 mayili.)
meymayakkam orumoḻi irumoḻikkaṇ kōṭal -
iṉṉa meykku iṉṉa mey naṭpeḻuttu, iṉṉamey pakiyeḻuttu eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭē meymayakkamum puṇarccivitikaḷum amintaṉa.
lakaraḷakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ yakaram mayaṅkum eṉpataṉukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭut tarumpōtu, kolyāṉi, veḷyāṟu- eṉṟa viṉittoki paṇputtokikaḷi oru moḻiyākak koṇṭu nac. kuṟippiṭṭu, ittokiyallāt taṉi moḻikkaṇ coṟkaḷ āciriyarkālattu iruntu piṉṉar iṟantaṉapōlum eṉṟu karutukiṟār.
iṅṅaṉamē ñn m v - eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar yakaram vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriñ yātu, porun yātu, tirum yātu, tev yātu - eṉṟu irumoḻikaḷip puṇarttu eṭuttukkāṭṭut tarutali virumpātu, utāraṇaṅkaḷ iṟantaṉa eṉṟār. nūl marapu orumoḻikkuḷ amiyum ceytikaḷic collavē amiyum ātaliṉ, oru collilēyē mey mayakkam varum ceytiyik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu nac. karuttu. iru moḻikkaṇ varum meymayakkam ‘puṇarcci’ eṉṟa vēṟu peyaril amitaliṉ, ataṉi meymayakkattuḷ aṭakkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu avar karuttu.	(to. e. 24, 27 nac. uri)
meymayakkam orumoḻikkum puṇarmoḻikkum potu vātaliṉ, mēṟkūṟiya puṇarmoḻic ceykiyellām taliyāya aṟiviṉōri nōkka oruvāṟṟāṉ kūṟiyavā ṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.	(23 iḷa. uri) 

meymayakkam nikaḻum iṭaṉ -
tol. taṉimoḻi ilakkaṇatti moḻimarapiṉkaṇ kūṟiyuḷḷār; meymayakkatti nūlmarapil kūṟiyuḷḷār. immeymayakkam oru moḻikkaṇ koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatōr ilakkaṇamāyiṉ, āciriyar taṉimoḻiyiṉip paṟṟik kūṟum moḻimarapil kūṟiyiruppār. moḻiyiṉ mutalil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ, īṟṟil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟik kūṟumiṭattēyē moḻiyiṭi niṟkum meymayakkattik kūṟa vēṇṭum. moḻimarapil kūṟātu nulmarapil meymayakkam kūṟiya ataṉāl, itaṉi orumoḻikkaṇṇēyē koḷḷa vēṇṭuvataṉṟu. iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa ceyti, iṉṉa mey yeḻuttiṉōṭu iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu mayaṅkum eṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉik kūṟiya attuṇiyē ākum. itaṉi iṭinili mayakkam eṉṟu kūṟuvatu takkataṉṟu; meymayak kam eṉṟalē takum. immayakkattiṟkum puṇarnilikkum vēṟṟumi uṇṭu. puṉkāl, palkutal - mutaliya coṟkaḷil kakarattoṭu mayaṅkiya ṉakara lakara meykaḷ, cantiyil poṟ kuṭam, pāṟkuṭam- eṉṟāṟ pōlat tirivaṉa āyiṉa.			(e. ā. pak. 23, 24)
meymayakkam nulmarapil kūṟappaṭṭa kāraṇam -
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷum īṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyiṭippaṭuttu uṇarappaṭa vēṇṭu taliṉ, taṉiyeḻuttup paṟṟiya nūlmarapil kūṟappaṭātu, moḻiyiṭippaṭuttu uṇarappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟik kuṟikkum moḻimarapil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. moḻiyiṭiyē varum iṭinili meymayakkamum moḻiyiṭik kāṇappaṭuva toṉṟātaliṉ atuvum moḻimarapil vikkappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉiṉ, moḻikku iṭiyē varum eḻutteṉṉātu, iv veḻuttukku ivveḻuttu naṭpu, allaṉa paki - eṉṟa ceytiyiyē viḷakkutaliṉ, icceyti nulmarapil kūṟap paṭṭatu.	(cū.vi. pak. 57)
‘meyyiṉ  iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇi’yē varutal -
taṉimey arimāttiri aḷaviṟṟu. atu nāc ciṟitu puṭi peyarum ciṟṟoli ātaliṉ, taṉimeyyik kūṟik kāṭṭa lākātu. ākavē, akara uyiroṭu cērttu meyyiṉi uyirmeyyāka olittalum, akaram ēṟiya meyyākak kūṟik kāṭṭalum marapāyiṉa.
	e-ṭu :
‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’	(to.e. 19 nac.)
‘melleḻut teṉpa ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa’	(to.e. 20 nac.)
‘iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa’	(to. e. 21 nac.)
ivvāṟu meykaḷ akarattoṭu civaṇiyē olivaṭivilum varivaṭivilum kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 46 nac.)
meyyiṉ iyaṟki -
uruvāki vaṭivu peṟum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ taṉmi yāvatu, avi olippu uṭiyaṉavāyum niṟṟal. puḷḷi yoṭum+ niliyal = puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal. ummiyāṉ, puḷḷiyoṭu nillāmal uyirōṭiyintu uyirmeyyākavum niṟkum eṉakkoḷka. (uyir mey - uyirkkum mey) meyyāṉatu olippiṉṟi uruvātalum, uyirppu unta ari māttiriyaḷavu olittalum, uyir iyiya icittalum ākiya mūṉṟu nilikaḷi yuṭittu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.			(to. e. 15 ca. pāla.)
meyyum oṟṟum puḷḷiyum -
patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷum vaṭivu koḷḷum nilimikkaṇ cevip pulaṉāvatilliātaliṉ, annili mey eṉṟum oṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. avaṟṟic cevippulaṉākka vēṇṭiṉ, akaram mutaliya uyirppicikaḷāṉ untāmal, aṉukaraṇa ōciyi uyirppākki unta avi cevippula ṉākum. aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttat tuṇipuriyum ōciyi arimāttiriyaḷavē icittal vēṇṭum eṉa, ‘meyyiṉ aḷavē ariyeṉa moḻipa’ eṉpar tolkāppiyaṉār. ammeyyiṉatu muḻuttaṉmiyum pulappaṭutta vēṇṭiṉ, akaravuyiric cāriyiyākak koṭuttu ataṉāl veḷip paṭuttal vēṇṭum. itaṉi āciriyar ‘meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum’ eṉṟār.
ivvāṟāka aṉukaraṇa uyirppiciyum akaraccāriyiyum iṉṟi ammeykaḷ moḻiyuṟuppāka ōr uyireḻuttiyō uyirmey yeḻuttiyō cārntu avaṟṟiṉpiṉ varumiṭattu naṉku olikkum anniliyi (al-kāl-aval- kāval) āciriyar ‘puḷḷi’ eṉac cuṭṭik kūṟuvar. ākaliṉ puḷḷimey orumoḻiyiṉ iṭiyilum īṟṟilumē niṟkum. itaṉi ‘meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal’ eṉṟār.
olippuṟṟu niṟkum puḷḷimeykaḷ annilimikkaṇ uyir ēṟa iṭam tārā; olippunili nīṅki vaṭivukoṇṭa muṉṉiya niliyiltāṉ uyirēṟa iṭam tarum. itaṉip ‘puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu, meyyoṭu civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē’ eṉpār āciriyar. aṅṅaṉam meyyiṉi ūrntu ammeyyiṉi veḷippaṭuttuṅkāl, uyir taṉ talimippāṭṭiṉiyum piṟappiṭattiyum iḻantu meykkut tuṇiyākiṟatu. mey talimip pāṭuṟṟu uyir mey eṉa nikaḻkiṟatu. ātaliṉ uyirmeyyiṉi meyyiṉ piṟitoru niliyākavē āciriyar kūṟukiṟār. ‘puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum... uyirtta lāṟē’ eṉpatu tol. cūttiram.			(to. e. pak. 11 ca. pāla.)


meyyeḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇam -
meyyeḻuttut taṉittolikkum iyalpiṉṟi, vāyuṟuppuk kaḷāṉ uruvāki vaṭivuṟṟu aṉukaraṇa ōciyāṉ olinili eyti, taṭiyuṟṟu veṭittum aṭivuṟṟu naḻuviyum taṭi yiṉṟi uraciyum nekiḻntum, neñcuvaḷi - miṭaṟṟuvaḷi - mūkkuvaḷi - icikaḷāṉ veḷippaṭṭuc cevippulaṉām; uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉpiṉ uyirici alakiṉ tuṇiyāṉ puḷḷiyuṟṟu olikkum; vāyuṟuppuk kaḷiṉ toḻil muyaṟciyum vaḷiyiciyum kāraṇamāka vaṉmi - meṉmi - iṭimi - eṉṉum taṉmikaḷip peṟum; uyiriciyit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ untu talāṉ uyirmeyyāki icittu alaku peṟum; uyirum kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum ēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu, av vaḻit taṉatu māttiriyi iḻantu, ēṟiya uyiriṉ māttiri yaḷavē taṉakku aḷavākak koṇṭu, uyirmey eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu peṟṟu niṟkum; moḻikkaṇ viṭṭici yātu toṭarntu varum;uṭaṉniṟṟaṟku ēlāta mey iṇiya varuvaḻit tirintu piḷavupaṭāmal toṭarum;  taṉakkup piṉ varum uyirmeyyiṉ oliyi mayakkit tiripupaṭuttum; uyirik kūṭiyō cārntō collāyum colluṟuppā yum amintu poruḷ viḷakkum; paṇṇicikaḷiyum vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyum uṇṭākkum; uyirppicikaḷi ēṟṟu neṭumi - kuṟumi - eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu peṟum; tammi ūrnta uyirkaḷukku vaṉmi - meṉmi - iṭimi - eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭukaḷi eytuvikkum;tām uyirmey āvataṟkut tuṇiceyyum uyirkaḷit tamakkuriya piṟappiṭattiṉiṉṟu piṟakkac ceytu tāmē talimi peṟum;mey-puḷḷi- uyirmey- eṉṉum mūvakiyākac ceyaṟpāṭu peṟṟuvarum.		(to. e. pak. 19, 20 ca. pāla.)
‘meyyoṭu civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē’ -
niṟutta colliṉ iṟuti niṟkum puḷḷiyeḻuttiṉ muṉ kuṟittuvarucolliṉ mutal varum uyirtāṉ piḷavupaṭṭu icikkāmal, ari māttiri olippuṭiyatāy niṟkum puḷḷiyeḻuttiṉ iyalpiṉik keṭuttu, olippu illāta meyvaṭivākki ataṉoṭu porunti icikkum.
	e-ṭu	:	nūl +aḻaku =nūlaḻaku; 	pāl + āṟu =pālāṟu;	āl+ili =ālili; aruḷ +īki = aruḷīki;	pukaḻ + oḷaviyam =pukaḻeḷaviyam				(to. e.  138 ca. pāla.)
mey: vēṟu peyarkaḷ -
ūmi eṉiṉum, oṟṟu eṉiṉum, uṭal eṉiṉum, kāttiram eṉiṉum meyyeṉṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 13)
melliṉam -
ṅñṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa āṟu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum mel leṉṟa mūkkoliyāl piṟappaṉa ātaliṉ mel liṉattic cārntaṉavām. (im melleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭam mūkku eṉṉum naṉṉūl. muttu vīriyam eḻut. 43ām nuṟpā mel liṉam āṟum piṟakkum iṭam tali eṉkiṟatu.)	(naṉ. 69)
melleḻuttu āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya peyarātal -
ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa āṟu meykaḷum melleḻuttām. ivaṟṟip piṉṉar ‘melleḻuttu mikiṉum’(323), ‘mel leḻuttu iyaṟki’- (145) eṉṟāṟpōla eṭuttuk kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭiyum, ivi meṉmiyāṉa mūkkoliyāṉ piṟattal karutiyum ivaṟṟi melleḻuttu eṉṟār.			(to.e. 20 nac. uri)
melleḻuttu iyaṟki uṟaḻtal -
moḻikku mutalil varuvaṉa valliṉa uyirmey - 4, melliṉa uyirmey -3, iṭiyiṉa uyirmey-2, uyir - 12 eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ valliṉam allāta meṉmi iṭimi uyirkkaṇaṅkaḷ ‘iyalpu kaṇam’ eṉappaṭum. ivi varu moḻi mutalil variṉ, nilimoḻi īṟṟilō varumoḻi mutalilō tiripu ēṟpaṭāmal iyalpākap puṇartalē perumpāṉmi. āṉāl mutalmoḻi toṭarmoḻiyāy niṟka ataṉi aṭuttu varum meṉkaṇam  iyalpātalē yaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmi mikkum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : 	katir+ñeri = katirñeri, katirññeri; katir +nuṉi = katirnuṉi, katirnnuṉi; katir +muri = katirmuri, katirmmuri
ivvāṟu melleḻuttu iyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭitalu muṇṭu.	(to. e. 145 nac.)
melleḻuttu mikā marappeyarkaḷ -
‘peṟunavum’ eṉṟa ummiyāl, attikkāy - akattikkāy - āttikkāy - iṟalikkāy- ilantikkāy - mutalāyiṉa melleḻuttup peṟā eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ.165 mayili.)
melleḻuttu: vēṟu peyarkaḷ -
meṉmi eṉiṉum, meṉkaṇam eṉiṉum, meli eṉiṉum melleḻutteṉṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī.eḻu. 17)
‘melloṟṟu valloṟṟiṟutik kiḷioṟṟu ātal’ -
‘melloṟṟu iṟutivalloṟṟum kiḷivalloṟṟum’ - eṉṟu pirittup poruḷ ceytaṉar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. 	(to.e.415, 414)
	e-ṭu :kuraṅku+kāl= kurakkukkāl - melloṟṟu ṅakaram iṟuti valloṟṟātal. eṇku +kuṭṭi = eṭkuk kuṭṭi - melloṟṟu ṇakaram taṉ kiḷiyāṉa ṭakara oṟṟātal.
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantaṉar.
kiḷiyoṟṟu eṉpatu avvav varukka oṟṟukkaḷi.
‘melloṟṟuk kiḷivalloṟṟākum’- eṉṟa toṭarkku, melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa melloṟ ṟellām iṟutiyil niṟkum valloṟṟākum; aḵtāvatu iṟutiyil niṟkum valloṟṟāyt tiriyum eṉpatu.
	e-ṭu:	ōryāṇṭu + kuḻavi = ōryāṭṭik kuḻavi
			kaṉṟu +tiraḷ = kaṟṟuttiraḷ
yāṇṭu, kaṉṟu - eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟiluḷḷa ṇakaraṉakaraṅkaḷ ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷākiya iṉoṟṟukkaḷākat tirital kaṇṭu, anta ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ piṟa eḻuttukkaḷoṭu kūṭi niṉṟavaḻiyum avvāṟu tiriyum eṉṟa karuti, eṇku+ kuṭṭi = eṭkuk kuṭṭi, eṉpu +kāṭu = eṟpukkāṭu - eṉap piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkiyami tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. (e. ā. pak. 168. 169)
melittal, melippu -
valleḻutti iṉamelleḻuttāka meypiṟitākkik kōṭal. aḵtāvatu valleḻutti iṉamelleḻuttākat tirittal
makarīṟṟup peyar vaṉkaṇam varumoḻi mutalil variṉ īṟṟu makaram keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu iṭiyē mikku,
maram +kiḷi =marakkiḷi - eṉṟāṟ pōla vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ varum. āṉāl iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyākap puṇarumpōtu, maram +kuṟittāṉ =maraṅ kuṟittāṉ - eṉa valleḻuttu mika vēṇṭiya iṭattu iṉamelleḻuttu varutal ‘melippoṭu tōṉṟal’ ākum. melippu - melleḻuttu.	(to.e.157 nac.)
meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
alvaḻikkaṇ meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	kuraṅku kaṭitu, pañcu ciṟitu, vaṇṭutītu, pantu peritu,  naṇpu peritu, kaṉṟu peritu.
		1. 	melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum,  2. melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, 
3. melloṟṟut tiriyātu aikāramum val leḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu :	1.	yāṇṭu  - ōryāṭṭi yāṉi, aiyāṭṭi erutu
		2.	aṉṟu, iṉṟu - aṟṟik kūttar, iṟṟip pōr
		3.	maṉṟu, paṇṭu	- maṉṟit  tūtu, maṉṟip paṉi; 		paṇṭic cāṉṟōr.       (to.e.425 nac. uri)
āṅku, īṅku, ūṅku, yāṅku, aṅku, iṅku, uṅku, eṅku - eṉpaṉa valloṟṟu mikku, āṅkuk koṇṭāṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum. yāṅku eṉpatu yāṅkuk koṇṭāṉ - eṉa varutal perumpāṉmi; yāṅku koṇṭāṉ- eṉa iyalpāyum muṭiyum.	(nac . uri 427-429)
meṉtoṭarmoḻi vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ val leḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu. e-ṭu: kuraṅkukkāl
iṭiyē melloṟṟu valloṟṟāyt tirintu valleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	: irupput toṭar, kurakkukkāl
			tattam iṉamāṉa valloṟṟākat tiritalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu :	eṇku+kuṭṭi =eṭkukkuṭṭi; eṉpu + kāṭu: eṟpukkāṭu; aṉpu + taḷi=aṟputtaḷi.
			paṟampiṟ pāri, kuṟumpiṟ cāṉṟōr - eṉa mel loṟṟut tiriyāmiyum uṇṭu.
			iyalpu kaṇattilum kurakku ñāṟci, kurakku viral, kurakku (u)kir - eṉa melloṟṟu iṉavalloṟ ṟātalumuṇṭu.	(414 nac. uri)
meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukarīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ (pullum aṭaṅkum) ammup peṟṟut teṅkaṅkāy,  cīḻkampul, kampam pulam, kamukaṅkāy eṉa varutalum koḷka. (teṅku, kamuku - iviyiraṇṭum pulliṉam.)	(415 nac. uri)
melloṟṟu valloṟṟu ākātu ammuccāriyi peṟum marappeyarkaḷum uḷa. e-ṭu: kuruntaṅ kōṭu, puṉkañ cetiḷ	(416 nac. uri)
akkuccāriyi peṟuvaṉavum uḷa. e-ṭu:kuṉṟakkūki, maṉṟap peṇṇi 	(418 nac. uri)
melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyum marappeyarkaḷum koḷka. (pulliṉamum aṭaṅkum) e-ṭu: vēppaṅ kōṭu, īccaṅ kuli; (īñcu : pulliṉam)	(416 nac. uri)
nilimoḻiyīṟṟil meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟpa, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum, alvaḻip puṇarcci yil iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 181)
	e-ṭu:	vantu kaṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ.
			ēḻām vēṟṟumiiṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa aṅku iṅku uṅku eṅku āṅku īṅku ūṅku yāṅku āṇṭu īṇṭu yāṇṭu - eṉṉum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ muṉ varum valliṉam mikum.
	e-ṭu:	aṅkuk kaṇṭāṉ, iṅkuc ceṉṟāṉ, āṇṭut tantāṉ, yāṇṭup pōṉāṉ.... mutalākak kāṇka.
ēḻām vēṟṟumik kālapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa aṉṟu iṉṟu eṉṟu paṇṭu muntu- eṉṉum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ muṉvarum valliṉam iyalpākavē muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu:	aṉṟu kaṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu ceṉṟāṉ, paṇṭu tantāṉ, muntu pōyiṉāṉ	(naṉ. 181 caṭakō. uri)
vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇ, meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu moḻikaḷuḷ cila nilimoḻikaḷ (nāṟkaṇamvariṉum) tamakku iṉamākiya vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara moḻi kaḷākat tiriyum.
	e-ṭu:	maruntu +pi = maruttuppi; kuraṅku + maṉam =  kurakkumaṉam; irumpu + valimi = iruppuvalimi; kaṉṟu +ā =kaṟṟā (kaṉṟoṭu kūṭiya pacu)
ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcci. vēppaṅkāy eṉpatum atu; iṭiyē ammuccāriyi mikkatu.
			nañcu + pakimi=  naccup pakimi (uvamattoki)
			irumpu +maṉam =iruppumaṉam (ituvumatu)
			eṉpu + uṭampu = eṟpuṭampu (irupeyaroṭṭu)
			ivi alvaḻip puṇarcci.
			kuraṅku +kuṭṭi =kuraṅkukkuṭṭi, kurakkukkuṭṭi (vēṟṟumi)
			aṉpu+taḷi =aṉputtaḷi, aṟputtaḷi (alvaḻi)	
iruvaḻiyum vikaṟpittu vantavāṟu.	(naṉ. 184)
aṉṟu, iṉṟu, (paṇṭu, muntu) aiyāṇṭu, mūvāṇṭu- eṉpaṉa aṟṟip poḻutu, iṟṟi nāḷ (paṇṭikkālam, munti vaḷam) aiyāṭṭip pirāyattāṉ, mūvāṭṭik kuḻavi- eṉa aikāraccāriyi īṟṟil peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa. aṉṟu mutaliya nāṉkum melloṟṟu valloṟṟāyt tirintu aikārac cāriyi ēṟṟalum, paṇṭu- muntu- eṉpaṉa tiripiṉṟi aikāram ēṟṟalum kāṇka.	(naṉ. 185)
meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram -
ukaram ēṟiya valloṟṟukkaḷuḷ ( ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu) oṉṟu moḻiyīṟṟatāy niṟpa ataṉ ayaleḻuttu oru melliṉa meyyāka variṉ, ammoḻiyiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramām.  kuṟṟiyalukarattukku māttiri ari.
	e-ṭu :	aṅku, pañcu, vaṇṭu, pantu, ampu, naṉṟu
			melloṟṟu āṟu ātaliṉ, ataṉi aṭuttu varum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramum āṟām.				(naṉ. 94)
meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam -
iruntu koṇṭu, kaṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ, ceṉṟu tantāṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōla ivvakik kuṟṟiyalukarīṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpākap puṇarum.	(to. e. 427. nac.)
ēḻāvataṉ kālapporuḷavākiya coṟkaḷ iyalpākap puṇarvaṉa uḷa.
e-ṭu :	paṇṭu koṇṭāṉ, muntu koṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu			 koṇṭāṉ, aṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, eṉṟu koṇṭāṉ.	(430 iḷa.)
	uṇṭu eṉṟa col, uṇṭu poruḷ - uḷ poruḷ- eṉa iruvakiyākap puṇarum.	(e. 430 nac.)
	uṇṭu kāṇam, uṇṭu cākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmari,
	uṇṭu ñāṉam, uṇṭu yāḻ, uṇṭu āṭi - eṉa 		iyalpākap puṇarvaṉavē perumpāṉmi.				(430 nac. uri)
	vaṇṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu		 vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	(420 nac.)
	peṇṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉcāriyi yōṭu 		aṉcāriyiyum peṟṟup peṇṭiṉki - peṇṭaṉki - 	eṉa varum.	(421 nac.)
		oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu - eṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷ aṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉṟaṉ kāyam - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. oṉṟaṉāṟ koṇṭa kāyam eṉa vēṟṟumip puṇarcci (419 nac.)
meṉtoṭarmoḻip puṇarcci -
kuraṅku, kaḻañcu, eṇku, maruntu, pāmpu, eṉpaṉa- meṉ toṭarkkuṟṟukarīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ. ivi vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriya varu moḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; iyalpukaṇamāyiṉ melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirital oṉṟumē uṇṭu-
varumāṟu: kurakkukkāl, kurakkuccevi, kurakkuttali, kurakkup puṟam - eṉavum, kurakkuñāṟci, kurakkuniṇam, kurakku mukam, kurakkuviral, kurakku (u) kir- eṉavum varum.
ñeṇṭu, pantu, paṟampu, kuṟumpu - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa mel loṟṟu valloṟṟu ākātaṉa; varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, iṉcāriyi ēṟṟu ataṉ ṉakaram ṟakara mākat tirintu muṭitalum ēṟṟa peṟṟiyākak koḷka.
varumāṟu: ñeṇṭukkāl, pantuttiraṭṭu; paṟampiṟ pāri, kuṟumpiṟ cāṉṟōr. 
‘maṉṉē’ eṉṟataṉāṉ. kurakkukkuṭṭi - kuraṅkiṉkāl, pāpput tōl- pāmpiṉ tōl- eṉa oṉṟu tāṉē ōriṭattut tirintum ōriṭattut tiriyātum varutalum koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ’aṟputtaḷi’ (nālaṭi 12:2) aṉpiṉāṟ ceyta taḷi - eṉa vēṟṟumi yātalēyaṉṟi, aṉpākiya taḷi-eṉa alvaḻiyum ātaliṉ, alvaḻikkaṇṇum melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirital koḷka.	(i.vi.eḻut. 103)
meṉtoṭar vēṟṟumikkaṇ vaṉtoṭarāyt tirivaṉavum, tiriyātaṉavum, iraṇṭumāvaṉavum
kurakkukkāl, kaḻaccukkōl, eṭṭukkuṭṭi, maruttuppi, pāppukkāl eṟpuccaṭṭam - eṉat tirintaṉa. nāṟkaṇat toṭum oṭṭuka.
ñeṇṭukkāl, vaṇṭukkāl, pantut tiraṭci, kōṅkili, icaṅkili, puṉkaṅkāy, potumpiṟ pūvi, kuṟumpiṟ koṟṟaṉ - eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa tiriyāvām.
kurakkukkaṭi, kuraṅkiṉ kaṭi, vēppaṅkāy, vēmpiṉ kāy, pāpputtōl, pāmpiṉ tōl - eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa tirintum tiriyātum vantaṉa.	(naṉ. 183 mayili.)
moḻi iṭip pōli -
moḻi iṭikkaṇ akarattukku aikāram  ca ña ya - eṉṉum meykaḷukku muṉṉarp pōliyāka varum.
	e-ṭu :	aracu - aricu, ilañci - iliñci, arayar -ariyar.
aikārattil piṉṉarum yakarattiṉ piṉṉarum, ciṟupāṉmi yāka nakarattiṟku ñakaram pōliyāka varum.
	e-ṭu	:	minniṉṟa - miññiṉṟa; kinniṉṟa - kiññiṉṟa; ceynniṉṟa - ceyññiṉṟa; neynniṉṟu - neyñ ñiṉṟu; ivi ceyyuḷ vaḻakku.
ulaka vaḻakkilum ainnūṟu, aiññūṟu, cēynalūr cēyñalūr eṉa varumāṟu kāṇaka.	(naṉ. 123, 124)
moḻi iṟutip pōli -
kuṟiliṇi aṭutta makarīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu makarattukku ṉakaram pōliyāka varutaluṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	:	akam - akaṉ ; mukam -mukaṉ; nilam- nilam; kalam - kalaṉ 	(naṉ. 122)
iṉi, kuṟṟiyalukarīṟṟup peyariṉ iṟuti ukarattukku ‘ar’ pōliyāka varutalum, īṟṟu lakarattukku rakaram pōliyāka varutalum, īṟṟu makarattukku lakaram pōliyāka varutalum, ciṟupāṉmi lakarattukku ḷakaram pōliyāka varutalum uriyiṟ kōṭalām.
	e-ṭu	:	curumpu, curumpar, vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar ;
			pantal - pantar, cāmpal - cāmpar, kuṭal - kuṭar;
			tiṟam- tiṟal, pakkam - pakkal, kūvam - kūval;
			matil - matiḷ	(naṉ. caṭakōpa. uri)
moḻi iṟuti varum eḻuttukkaḷ -
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya  ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum meyyeḻuttup patiṉoṉṟum,  kuṟṟiyalukaramum moḻi yiṟutikkaṇ niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷām. āka avi irupattu nāṉku.	(naṉ. 107)
tāmēyum aḷapeṭuppuḻiyum meyyoṭu kūṭiyum varum uyirīṟu nūṟṟaṟupattu mūṉṟum, meyyīṟu patiṉoṉṟum, kuṟṟukara īṟu oṉṟum - ākap potuvakiyāṉum ciṟappu vakiyāṉum īṟu nūṟṟeḻupattintum koḷka. avaṟṟuḷ utāraṇam kāṇātaṉa irupattiraṇṭu. aviyum vantuḻik kāṇka.	(i.vi. 29 uri)
a ā i ī u ū ē ai ō - eṉṉum uyir oṉpatum, ṇakara makara ṉakaram eṉṉum melliṉam mūṉṟum, ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - eṉṉum iṭiyiṉam āṟum moḻikku īṟām eḻuttukkaḷ (okaram nakarattoṭu kūṭi ‘no’ eṉa ivvoru colliṭattē māttiram īṟāka varum eṉpar uriyāciriyar)	(nē. eḻut. 8)
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara okaram nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya pattum, melliṉa eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ṇakāra makāra ṉakāraṅkaḷākiya mūṉṟum, iṭiyiṉa eḻuttukkaḷuḷ vakāroṟṟu nīṅkalāṉa aintum moḻi iṟuti eḻuttukkaḷām.	(vī.cō. cantip. 8)
arukiyē moḻiyīṟāka varum ñakāra nakāra vakāra oṟṟukkaḷi viṭuttu ēṉiyavaṟṟiyē vīracōḻiyam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ -
uyireḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakāra cakāra takāra nakāra pakāra makāra varukkaṅkaḷum, vakāra varukkattil u ū o ō- allāta eṭṭum, yakāra varukkattil  a ā u ū ō ōḷa- ākiya āṟum, ñakāra varukkattil a ā e ō - ākiya nāṉkum eṉṉum iviyaṉittum tamiḻc collukku moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷām. āka, avi 12, 72, 8, 6,4 - eṉa 102 ām. 			(vī. cō. cantip.7)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉi raṇṭum, cakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, takara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, nakaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, pakaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum. makaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, vakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ eṭṭum, ñakara varukkattil mūṉṟum, yakara varukkattil mūṉṟum āka, ittoṇṇuṟṟeṭṭu eḻuttum moḻikku mutalām.
(vakaram a ā i ī e ē ai oḷa - eṉṉum eṭṭuyiroṭum, ñakaram ā e o - eṉṉum mūṉṟuyiroṭum, yakaram ā ū ō - eṉṉum mūṉṟuyiroṭum kūṭi moḻi mutalām eṉka.)			(nē. eḻut. 7)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakara cakara takara nakara pakara makara varukka uyirmeykaḷ taṉittaṉiyē paṉṉiraṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāka eḻupattiraṇṭum, u ū o ō- allāta eṭṭu uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya vakaravarukka uyirmey eṭṭum, a ā u ū ō oḷa- eṉṉum āṟu uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya yakaravarukka uyirmey āṟum, a ā e o - eṉṉum nāṉku uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya ñakara varukka uyirmey nāṉkum, akara uyiroṉṟoṭum kūṭiya ṅakara uyirmey oṉṟum āka 103 eḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāvaṉa.	(naṉ. 102-106)
moḻikku mutalām eḻuttukkaḷ potuvum ciṟappumām iruvakiyāṉum toṇṇuṟṟu nāṉkām eṉpatu aṟika. ‘cuṭṭuyā ekara....... mutalākummē’ (naṉ. 106) eṉṟārum uḷarālō eṉiṉ, mutalāvaṉa ivi īṟāvaṉa ivi eṉa īṇṭuk karuvi ceytatu mēl nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutalōṭu iyiyap puṇarkkumporuṭṭaṉṟē? avvāṟu puṇarttaṟku iyipillāta ṅakaramum aṅṅaṉam - iṅṅaṉam - uṅṅaṉam- yāṅṅaṉam- eṅṅaṉam - eṉa ivvāṟu moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal payaṉil kūṟṟām eṉa maṟukka, aṉṟiyum, aṅku- āṅku, yāṇṭu - yāṇṭiyatu, aṉṉa - eṉṉa eṉṟāṟpōlum ivvoṟṟukkaḷum moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal vēṇṭutalāṉ, avarkkum atu karuttaṉṟu eṉka.			(i.vi.27 uri)
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ aṭṭavaṇi -
	tol	vīra	nēmi	naṉṉūl	ilakkaṇa	muttu	cuvāmi
	kāppiyam	  cōḻiyam	nātam		viḷakkam	vīriyam	nātam
	uyir	12	12	12	12	12	12	12

	va	8	8	8	8	8	8	8
	ya	1	6	3	6	1	6	8
	ña	3	4	3	4	3	4	6
kuṟṟiyalukaram	 1	-	-	-	-	-	-
	ṅa	-	-	-	1	-	-	-
kūṭutal :	94	102	98	103	94	102	106
